# Rashi on Daniel

רש"י על דניאל

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sefaria vocalized edition (Segments: 764, License: unknown)

Version (En): The Judaica Press complete Tanach with Rashi, translated by A. J. Rosenberg (Segments: 760)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:1:1)


**בִּשְׁנַת שָׁלוֹשׁ לְמַלְכוּת יְהוֹיָקִים.** וְכִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר כֵּן? וַהֲלֹא הוּא מָלַךְ בִּשְׁנַת אַרְבַּע לִיהוֹיָקִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ירמיהו כה:א) ״בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִעִית לִיהוֹיָקִים בֶּן יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה הִיא הַשָּׁנָה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה לִנְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּר״. וּמַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר **בִּשְׁנַת שָׁלוֹשׁ**? **בִּשְׁנַת שָׁלוֹשׁ** לְמִרְדּוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מלכים ב כד:א) ״וַיְהִי לוֹ יְהוֹיָקִים עֶבֶד שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים וַיָּשָׁב וַיִּמְרֹד בּוֹ״. שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים וּבִשְׁנַת שָׁלֹשׁ עָלָה עָלָיו. וְהִיא שְׁנַת שְׁמוֹנֶה לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, דְּאָמַר מַר: שָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה כָּבַשׁ נִינְוֵה, שְׁנִיָּה עָלָה וְכָבַשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים, וְשָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים עֲבָדוֹ וְשָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים מָרַד בּוֹ, וְהִיא הָיְתָה שְׁנַת אַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה לְמַלְכוּת יְהוֹיָקִים: חָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים קֹדֶם שֶׁכְּבָשׁוֹ וְשָׁלֹשׁ שֶׁעֲבָדוֹ וְשָׁלֹשָׁה שֶׁמָּרַד בּוֹ, וְאָז מֵת יְהוֹיָקִים תַּחַת יָדוֹ וְהִמְלִיךְ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר תַּחְתָּיו יְהוֹיָכִין בְּנוֹ:


**In the third year of the reign of Jehoiakim** Now is it possible to say so? Did he [Nebuchadnezzar] not reign in the fourth year of Jehoiakim, as is stated (Jer. 25:1) “in the fourth year of Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah that is the first year of Nebuchadnezzar?” So what is the meaning of “In the third year?” In the third year of his rebellion, as is stated (II Kings 24:1): “and Jehoiakim was his vassal for three years; then he turned and rebelled against him”; and in the third year, he overcame him, and that was the eighth year of Nebuchadnezzar, for the master said (Seder Olam ch. 24): In his first year he conquered Nineveh, and in his second year he advanced and vanquished Jehoiakim, who served him for three years and rebelled against him for three years. That was the eleventh year of the reign of Jehoiakim: five years before he vanquished him, three years that he served him, and three years that he rebelled against him. And then Jehoiakim died under his hand, and in his stead, Nebuchadnezzar enthroned Jehoiachin, his son.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:2:1)


**וּמִקְצָת כְּלֵי בֵית הָאֱלֹהִים.** לָקַח וּמִקְצָתָם נִשְׁאֲרוּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ירמיהו כז:יט): ״כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ צְבָאוֹת אֶל הָעַמּוּדִים וְעַל הַיָּם וְעַל הַמְּכֹנוֹת וְעַל יֶתֶר הַכֵּלִים הַנּוֹתָרִים בָּעִיר הַזֹּאת״:


**and some of the vessels of the House of God** he took, and some of them remained, as is stated (in Jeremiah 27:19): “so has the Lord of Hosts said concerning the pillars, concerning the sea, concerning the bases, and concerning the rest of the vessels, which Nebuchadnezzar did not take into exile (sic).”


###### Rashi on Daniel 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:2:2)


**וַיְבִיאֵם אֶרֶץ שִׁנְעָר בֵּית אֱלֹהָיו.** לְקַלֵּס לַעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁלּוֹ הֵבִיא שָׁם אֶת הַשִּׁבְיָה כֻּלָּהּ, אַף אֶת הָאֲנָשִׁים, **וְאֶת הַכֵּלִים הֵבִיא** אַחַר כָּךְ בֵּית הָאוֹצָר:


**and he brought them to the land of Shinar, to the house of his god** to praise his idols; he brought the entire captivity there, even the people, and the vessels he brought afterwards to the treasury.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:3:1)


**הַפַּרְתְּמִים.** דּוּכָסִים:


**the nobles** Heb. הַפַּרְתְּמִים, dukes.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:4:1)


**וַאֲשֶׁר כֹּחַ בָּהֶם.** פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה שֶׁיִּהְיוּ אוֹנְסִין עַצְמָן מִן הַשְּׂחוֹק וּמִן הַשִּׂיחָה וּמִן הַשֵּׁנָה מִפְּנֵי אֵימַת מַלְכוּת וְשֶׁמַּעֲמִידִים עַצְמָן כְּשֶׁנִּצְרָכִים לִנְקָבֵיהֶם (סנהדרין צג:):


**and who have strength** Our Sages explained (Sanh. 93b) that they should restrain themselves from laughing, speaking and sleeping, out of fear of the throne, and resist the urge when they feel the need to relieve themselves.


###### Rashi on Daniel 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:4:2)


**וּלְלַמְּדָם סֵפֶר.** מוּסָב עַל **אֲשֶׁר כֹּחַ בָּהֶם**:


**and to teach them the script** This refers back to “who have strength.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:5:1)


**וַיְמַן.** לְשׁוֹן הַזְמָנָה:


**allotted** Heb. וַיְמַן, an expression of preparation.


###### Rashi on Daniel 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:5:2)


**מִפַּת בַּג.** הוּא שֵׁם מַאֲכַל הַמֶּלֶךְ בִּלְשׁוֹן כַּשְׂדִּים. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים **פַּת בַּג** לֶחֶם, וְאֵין נִרְאֶה כֵּן, שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּתִיב ״וַיְהִי הַמֶּלְצַר נֹשֵׂא אֶת פַּת בָּגָם וְנֹתֵן לָהֶם זֵרְעוֹנִים״ (דניאל א:טז), וְאֵין זֵרְעוֹנִים חֲלִיפֵי לֶחֶם אֶלָּא חֲלִיפֵי תַבְשִׁיל:


**of... food** Heb. מִפַּת־בַּג. That is the name of the king’s fare in the language of the Chaldeans. Some interpret פַּת־בַּג, [as] bread, but it does not appear correct, for it is written (below verse 16): “The steward would take away פַּת־בָּגָם... and give them pulse.” Now pulse is not a substitute for bread, but a substitute for cooked food.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:7:1)


**בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּר.** עַל שֵׁם עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁל בָּבֶל שֶׁקְּרוּיָה בֵּל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״דִּי שְׁמֵהּ בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּר כְּשׁוּם אֱלָהִי״ (דניאל ד:ה). בֵּלְטְשַׁאצַּר שֵׁם לְשׁוֹן חָכְמָה בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי:


**Belteshazzar** He named him after the god of Babylon, which was called Bel, as it is said (4: 5): “whose name is Belteshazzar, like the name of my god.” Teshazzar is the Aramaic expression denoting wisdom.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:8:1)


**יִתְגָּאַל.** לְשׁוֹן לִכְלוּךְ:


**not to be defiled** Heb. לֹא יָתְגָּאַל, an expression of sullying.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:9:1)


**שַׂר הַסָּרִיסִים.** שֶׁהָיָה שַׂר עַל מְשָׁרְתֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**the chief officer** who was the chief over the king’s servants.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:10:1)


**אֲשֶׁר מָנָה.** אֲשֶׁר הִזְמִין וְסִפֵּק:


**who allotted** who prepared and provided.


###### Rashi on Daniel 1:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:10:2)


**כְּגִילְכֶם.** דּוּגְמַתְכֶם, וּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה הוּא: בֶּן גִּילוֹ (סנהדרין לז:):


**like you** כְּגִילְכֶם, of your likeness. It is the language of the Mishnah; one of his likeness.


###### Rashi on Daniel 1:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:10:3)


**וְחִיַּבְתֶּם אֶת רֹאשִׁי לַמֶּלֶךְ.** תִּגְרְמוּ לִי לְהִתְחַיֵּב לְהַתִּיז רֹאשִׁי:


**and you will forfeit my head to the king** You shall bring about that I will be condemned to be beheaded.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:11:1)


**הַמֶּלְצַר.** הַסּוֹדֵר אֶת הַמָּנוֹת וְאֶת הַקְּעָרוֹת שֶׁקּוֹרִין מאישטרי״ב אל״ה:


**the steward** Heb. הַמֶּלְצַר, the one who arranges the portions and the plates, called maestre sala, seneschal, steward.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:12:1)


**נֵס.** לְשׁוֹן נִסָּיוֹן:


**test** Heb. נַס an expression of (נִסָיוֹן) testing.


###### Rashi on Daniel 1:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:12:2)


**מִן הַזֵּרֹעִים.** מִין קִטְנִיּוֹת, תַּחַת פַּת בַּג (לעיל ח):


**some pulse** a type of beans instead of the food.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:13:1)


**וְכַאֲשֶׁר תִּרְאֶה.** לְפִי מַה שֶּׁתִּרְאֶה עֲשֵׂה:


**and as you will see** According to what you see, do.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:16:1)


**נוֹשֵׂא אֶת פַּת בָּגָם.** נוֹטְלוֹ לְעַצְמוֹ:


**carry away their food** He would take it for himself.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:18:1)


**אֲשֶׁר אָמַר הַמֶּלֶךְ לַהֲבִיאָם.** שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ לְפָנָיו:


**that the king ordered to bring them** that they come before him.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:20:1)


**הַחַרְטֻמִּים.** אֵלּוּ שֶׁשּׁוֹאֲלִים בְּטִימֵי מֵתִים, בְּעַצְמוֹת מֵתִים, כְּמָה דְאַתְּ אָמַר: שְׁחִיק עַצְמוֹת, שְׁחִיק טַמְיָא:


**the necromancers** These are the ones who inquire of טִימֵי מֵתִים, the bones of the dead, as you say, “May his bones be crushed,” שְּׁחִיק טַמַיָא.


###### Rashi on Daniel 1:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:20:2)


**הָאַשָּׁפִים.** אֵלּוּ שֶׁדּוֹחֲקִים בַּמַּזָּל, כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּתַנְחוּמָא (תנחומא מקץ ב):


**the astrologers** These are the ones who “press” the constellation. So it is explained in Tanhuma (Miketz 2, Buber, Miketz 4).



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Daniel 1:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/1:21:1)


**וַיְהִי דָנִיֵּאל.** בִּגְדֻלָּה בְּבָבֶל **עַד שְׁנַת אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ**. לְדִבְרֵי הָאוֹמֵר הֲתָךְ זֶה דָנִיֵּאל (מגילה טו.), וְלָמָּה נִקְרָא שְׁמוֹ הֲתָךְ שֶׁחֲתָכוּהוּ מִגְּדֻלָּתוֹ, צְרִיכִין אָנוּ לוֹמַר שֶׁזֶּהוּ כּוֹרֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁלִּפְנֵי אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ. וּלְדִבְרֵי הָאוֹמֵר שֶׁכָּל דִּבְרֵי מַלְכוּת נֶחְתָּכִין עַל פִּיו, נֹאמַר כּוֹרֶשׁ זֶה הוּא דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַשֵּׁנִי אַחַר אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ, וּבִימֵי דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי שֶׁהִשְׁלִיכוּ לְגוֹב אֲרָיוֹת לֹא יָרַד מִגְּדֻלָּתוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דניאל ו:כט) ״וְדָנִיֵּאל דְּנָה הַצְלַח בְּמַלְכוּת דָּרְיָוֶשׁ וּבְמַלְכוּת כּוֹרֶשׁ פַּרְסָאָה״, לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁבִּימֵי כּוֹרֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן הָיָה בִּגְדֻלָּה:


**And Daniel was there** in greatness in Babylon until the first year of King Cyrus. According to the one who says that Hathach (Esther; 4:5, 6, 9, 10) is Daniel; and why was he called Hathach? Because he was cut off (חֲתָכוּהוּ) from his greatness; [according to his explanation] we must say that this is Cyrus I, who preceded Ahasuerus. But according to the one who says that all royal affairs were decided (נֶחְתָּכִים) by his orders, we must say that this Cyrus was Darius II, [who came] after Ahasuerus, and in the days of Darius the Mede, when they cast [Daniel] into the lions’ den, he was not demoted from his greatness, as it is said (6:29): “And this Daniel prospered during the reign of Darius and during the reign of Cyrus the Persian.” We learn that during the days of Cyrus I he was [still] in his greatness.



## Chapter 2


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:1:1)


**וּבִשְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְמַלְכוּת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר כֵּן, אֶלָּא **בִּשְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם** לְחֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת; כָּךְ שְׁנוּיָה בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (פרק כח). וּקְרָאוֹ **לְמַלְכוּת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר**, עַל שֶׁהֶרְאָה זְדוֹנוֹ לִכָּנֵס לְתוֹךְ דְּבִיר מַלְכּוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם:


**Now in the second year of Nebuchadnezzar’s reign** It is impossible to say this, except that [it happened] in the second year after the destruction of the Temple. So it is taught in Seder Olam (ch. 28), and Scripture called it “of Nebuchadnezzar’s reign,” because he demonstrated his insolence by entering the Inner Sanctum of the Sovereign of the Universe.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:1:2)


**וַתִּתְפָּעֶם רוּחוֹ.** בְּפַרְעֹה נֶאֱמַר ״וַתִּפָּעֶם רוּחוֹ״ (בראשית מא:ח) שֶׁלֹּא נֶעְלַם מִמֶּנּוּ אֶלָּא פִּתְרוֹן הַחֲלוֹם, וְכָאן שֶׁנֶּעְלַם מִמֶּנּוּ גַּם הַחֲלוֹם נִכְפְּלָה הַלָּשׁוֹן **וַתִּתְפָּעֶם**. לְשׁוֹן פְּעִימָה וַהֲלִימָה, (וּבַחוּמָּשׁ פֵּרֵשׁ רַשִׁ״י זַ״ל לְשׁוֹן נְקִיפָה):


**and his spirit was troubled** Heb. וַתִּתְפָּעֶם רוּחוֹ. Concerning Pharaoh it says (Gen. 41:8): וַתִּפָּעֶם רוּחוֹ, for only the interpretation of the dream was hidden from him, but here, since the dream too was hidden from him, the expression is doubled: וַתִּתְפָּעֶם, an expression of pulsating and beating.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:1:3)


**נִהְיְתָה עָלָיו.** לְשׁוֹן שִׁבָּרוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״הוֹוָה עַל הוֹוָה״ (יחזקאל ו:יא):


**was interrupted** Heb. נִהְיְתָה, an expression of breaking like (Ezek. 7:25): “Breach upon breach (הוָה עַל הוָה).”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:4:1)


**לְעָלְמִין חַיֵּי.** לְעוֹלָם יִחְיֶה:


**May the king live forever** Aram. מַלְכָּא לְעָלְמִין חֱיִי.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:4:2)


**אֱמַר חֶלְמָא לְעַבְדָּךְ.** אֱמוֹר הַחֲלוֹם לְעַבְדֶּךָ:


**Tell your servants the dream** [as translated,] Tell your servants the dream.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:1)


**מִלְּתָא מִנִּי אַזְדָּא.** הַדָּבָר מִמֶּנִּי הָלַךְ׃


**The matter has escaped me** The thing has left me.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:2)


**הֵן לָא תְהוֹדְעוּנַּנִי חֶלְמָא וּפִשְׁרֵהּ.** אִם לֹא תוֹדִיעוּנִי הַחֲלוֹם וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ:


**If you do not let me know the dream and its meaning** [as translated,] if you do not let me know the dream and its meaning.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:3)


**הַדָּמִין תִּתְעַבְדוּן.** לְאֵבָרִים תִּתְחַתְּכוּ, וְהַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא הַדּוֹמֵי הַדְמוּהָ בְּמַסֶּכֶת גִּטִּין (גיטין סז:):


**you shall be torn limb from limb** Aram. הַדָמִין תִּתְעַבְּדוּן. You shall be cut to pieces, and there are many such in the Gemara (Gittin 67b): “Arrange the cuts” (הַדוּמֵי הַדְמּוּהָ).


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:4)


**נוֹלִי.** אַשְׁפָּה:


**a dungheap** Aram. נְוָלִי, a dungheap.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:5:5)


**יִתְּשָׂמוּן.** לְשׁוֹן ״וַיָּשֶׂם״ (דניאל א:ב), יְהוֹן שׂוּמוֹת לְאַשְׁפּוֹת:


**shall be made** Aram. יִתְּשָּׂמוּן an expression of וְיָשֵּׂם, and he will make. They shall be made into dungheaps.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:6:1)


**וְהֵן.** וְאִם:


**But if** [as translated,] But if.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:6:2)


**וּנְבִזְבָּה.** דּוֹרְנוֹת:


**and lavish presents** [as translated,] presents.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:6:3)


**לָהֵן.** אַךְ:


**but** [as translated,] but.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:6:4)


**הַחֲוֺנִי.** תַּגִּידוּ לִי:


**tell me** [as translated,] tell me.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:7:1)


**תִּנְיָנוּת.** שֵׁנִית:


**a second time** [as translated,] a second time.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:7:2)


**נְהַחֲוֵה.** נַגִּיד:


**we shall tell** [as translated,] we shall tell.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:8:1)


**מִן יַצִּיב יָדַע אֲנָה.** בֶּאֱמֶת יוֹדֵעַ אֲנִי:


**in truth, I know** [as translated,] in truth, I know.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:8:2)


**יַצִּיב.** דָּבָר אֱמֶת וְיַצִּיב:


**truth** Aram. יַצִיב, a true and permanent thing.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:8:3)


**דִּי עִדָּנָא אַנְתּוּן זָבְנִין.** אֲשֶׁר זוֹ הַשָּׁעָה אַתֶּם מְסוּרִים לַמָּוֶת:


**that this time you are doomed** Aram. דִּי עִדָּנָא אַנְתוּן זַבְנִין, that this time you are delivered to death.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:8:4)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דִּי חֲזֵיתוֹן.** כָּל כְּנֶגֶד שֶׁאַתֶּם רוֹאִים שֶׁהָלַךְ מִמֶּנִּי הַדָּבָר וְאַתֶּם כּוֹפְלִים לִי לְהַגִּיד לָכֶם:


**because you see** because you see that the matter escaped me, but you repeatedly ask me to tell you.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:1)


**דִּי הֵן חֶלְמָא לָא תְהוֹדְעִינַּנִּי.** שֶׁאִם הַחֲלוֹם וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ לֹא תוֹדִיעוּנִי, אַחַת הִיא דָתְכֶם לָמוּת:


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:2)


**וּמִלָּה כְּדִבָה וּשְׁחִיתָה הִזְדַּמִּנְתּוּן.** אוֹ אִם דָּבָר כָּזָב וְנִשְׁחָת תָּכִינוּ עַצְמְכֶם לוֹמַר לְפָנַי:


**or if you have prepared yourselves...a false and corrupt word** Or if you have prepared yourselves to say before me a false and corrupt word.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:3)


**עַד דִּי עִדָּנָא יִשְׁתַּנֵּא.** עַד אֲשֶׁר יִשְׁתַּנֶּה הָעֵת, כְּלוֹמַר: קֹדֶם נְטוֹת צִלְלֵי עֶרֶב (ירמיה ו:ד), שֶׁיִּשְׁתַּנֶּה מוֹעֵד הַבֹּקֶר:


**until the time changes** until the time changes, i.e., before the shadows of evening slant, when the time of morning changes.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:4)


**עַד דִּי עִדָּנָא יִשְׁתַּנֵּא.** לְשׁוֹן גְּעָרָה וְגִזּוּם הוּא. כָּךְ יֵעָנֵשׁ, וְהָעֹנֶשׁ כְּבָר פֵּרֵשׁ לְמַעְלָה. וְדוּגְמָתוֹ ״לָכֵן כָּל הֹרֵג קַיִן״ (בראשית ד:טו):


“Until the time changes” is an expression of rebuke and an exaggeration: so will he be punished, and the punishment was already delineated above. A similar instance is (Gen. 4:15): “Therefore, whoever kills Cain.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:9:5)


**לָהֵן.** אַךְ הַחֲלוֹם אִמְרוּ לִי מַה חָלַמְתִּי, וְאֵדַע כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁהַפִּתְרוֹן תַּגִּידוּ:


**but** the dream; tell me what I dreamed, and I shall know that you will surely tell me the interpretation.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:10:1)


**דִּי מִלַּת מַלְכָּא.** אֲשֶׁר דְּבַר הַמֶּלֶךְ יוּכַל לְהַגִּיד:


**who...the king’s word** who will be able to tell the king’s word.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:10:2)


**כָּל קֳבֵל.** כָּל כְּנֶגֶד, כְּלוֹמַר: תְּשׁוּבָתֵנוּ זוֹ אֱמֶת הִיא, לְעֻמַּת שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה מֶלֶךְ מֵעוֹלָם שֶׁשָּׁאַל דָּבָר כָּזֶה:


**because** Aram. כָּל קַבֵל, lit. all opposite; i.e., our reply is true, in view of the fact that there was never a king who asked anything like this.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:10:3)


**מִלָּה כִדְנָה.** דָּבָר כָּזֶה:


**such a thing** Aram. מִלָה כִדְּנָה, a thing like this.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:10:4)


**וְאַשָּׁף.** הַדּוֹחֵק בַּמַּזָּל:


**astrologer** who presses the constellation.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:11:1)


**יְקִירָה.** כְּבֵדָה, וּמַשָּׂאָהּ כָּבֵד:


**difficult** Aram. יַקִירָה, heavy, and its burden is heavy.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:11:2)


**וְאָחֳרָן לָא אִיתַי דִּי יְחַוִּנַּהּ.** וְאַחֵר אֵין אֲשֶׁר יַגִּידֶהָ:


**and there is no other who can tell it** and there is no other who will tell it.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:11:3)


**לָהֵן אֱלָהִין.** אַךְ הַמַּלְאָכִים אֲשֶׁר מְדוֹרָם עִם בָּשָׂר וָדָם אֵינֶנּוּ:


**but the angels** but the angels whose dwelling is not with flesh and blood.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:11:4)


**מְדָרְהוֹן.** מְגוּרֵיהוֹן. וּמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא **וְאָחֳרָן לָא אִיתַי** וְאַהֲרֹן **לָא אִיתַי** כֹּהֵן הַלּוֹבֵשׁ אוּרִים וְתֻמִּים אֵינוֹ כָאן שֶׁיַּגִּיד לְךָ. אָמַר לָהֶם: כָּל כָּךְ הָיָה כֹּחַ בְּאוֹתוֹ בַּיִת וּנְתַתֶּם לִי עֵצָה לְהַחֲרִיבוֹ?! מִיָּד כָּעַס וְצִוָּה לְהָרְגָן:


**whose dwelling** Aram. מְדָרְהוֹן, their dwelling. The Midrash of Rabbi Tanhuma (ad loc.) [says]: וְאַחֲרָן לָא אִיתַי, and Aaron is not here. A priest who wears the Urim and Tummim is not here to tell you. He said to them, “That Temple was so powerful, and you advised me to destroy it?” Immediately, he became angry and commanded to slay them.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:12:1)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דְּנָה.** לְעֻמַּת הַתְּשׁוּבָה הַזֹּאת:


**In view of this** in view of this reply.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:12:2)


**מַלְכָּא בְּנַס וּקְצַף שַׂגִּיא.** הָיָה הַמֶּלֶךְ בְּזַעַף וְקֶצֶף גָּדוֹל:


**in great wrath and anger** Aram. מַלְכָּא בְּנַס וּקְצַף שַּׂגִיא. The king was in great wrath and anger.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:12:3)


**בְּנַס.** בְּזַעַף, ״וְהִנָּם זֹעֲפִים״ (בראשית מ:ו) תִּרְגֵּם אוּנְקְלוֹס: ״וְהָא אִינּוּן נְסִיסִין״:


(בְּנַס) means in wrath. (Gen. 40:6): “And behold they were angry (זעַפִים), and Onkelos renders: נסִיסִין.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:12:4)


**וַאֲמַר לְהוֹבָדָה.** וַיֹּאמֶר לְהַאֲבִיד, וָי״ו שֶׁל **וַאֲמַר** נִתְעַצְּמוּ בּוֹ מְנַחֵם וְדוּנָ״שׁ, מְנַחֵם דָּחַק עַצְמוֹ לָתֵת בֵּי״ת שֶׁל **בְּנַס** בִּיסוֹד הַתֵּיבָה וְלֹא פָּתַר **בְּנַס וּקְצַף** לְשׁוֹן שְׁמוֹת דְּבָרִים אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן פֹּעַל רָגַז וְקָצַף הַרְבֵּה, וְאָמַר לְהַאֲבִיד וָי״ו שֶׁבְּ**וַאֲמַר** דְּחָקוֹ לִפְתֹּר כֵּן, וְדוּנָשׁ אָמַר אִי אֶפְשָׁר לִהְיוֹת בֵּי״ת **בְּנַס** יְסוֹד בַּתֵּיבָה אֶלָּא שֵׁם דָּבָר הוּא, וְכֵן פָּתְרוֹ בְּזַעַף וְקֶצֶף גָּדוֹל אָמַר לְהַאֲבִיד, וְשָׁם הַוָּי״ו טְפֵלָה וִיתֵרָה כְּמוֹ וָי״ו שֶׁל ״וְרֶכֶב וָסוּס״ (תהלים עו:ז). וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר אֵין הַוָּי״ו דְּחוּקָה לִהְיוֹת טְפֵלָה וִיתֵרָה כִּי כֵּן פִּתְרוֹנוֹ **מַלְכָּא בְּנַס וּקְצַף שַׂגִּיא** הַמֶּלֶךְ נִהְיָה בְּזַעַף וְקֶצֶף גָּדוֹל וְאָמַר בְּזַעְפּוֹ לְהַאֲבִידָם וּמִן הַמִּקְרָאוֹת קִצְרֵי לָשׁוֹן הִיא:


**and ordered to destroy** Aram. וַאֲמַר לְהוֹבָדָה. About this “vav” of וַאֲמַר, Menahem and Dunash disputed. Menahem (p. 46) forced himself to make the “beth” of בְּנַס, a radical and did not interpret בְּנַס וּקְצַף as nouns, but as an expression of a verb: was greatly wroth and angry and ordered to destroy. The “vav” in וַאֲמַר forced him to interpret it this way. Dunash (p. 9) says that it is impossible for the “beth” to be a radical, but it is a noun, and he interpreted it in this manner: with great wrath and anger he ordered to destroy, and the “vav” there is superfluous, like the “vav” of (Ps. 76:7): “chariot (וְרֶכֶב) and horse.” But I say that the “vav” is not forced to be superfluous, but so is its interpretation: מַלְכָּא בְּנַס וּקְצַף שַּׂגִּיא, the king was in great wrath and anger, and he ordered in his wrath to destroy them. Accordingly, this is an elliptical verse.
