###### Rashi on Daniel 2:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:28:2)


**חֶלְמָךְ וְחֶזְוֵי רֵאשָׁךְ עַל מִשְׁכְּבָךְ.** אֲשֶׁר עַל מִשְׁכְּבָךְ זֶהוּ:


**your dream and the visions of your head on your bed** which are on your bed.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:29:1)


**רַעְיוֹנָךְ.** הִרְהוּרְךָ:


**your thoughts** Aram. רַעְיוֹנָיךְ.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:29:2)


**עַל מִשְׁכְּבָךְ סְלִיקוּ.** מָה שֶׁהָיִיתָ מְהַרְהֵר בַּיּוֹם מָה יִהְיֶה אַחֲרֶיךָ, **עַל מִשְׁכָּבְךָ עָלוּ**:


**came while on your bed** What you were thinking of during the day, what would be after you, came [to you] on your bed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:29:3)


**וְגָלֵא רָזַיָּא הוֹדְעָךְ.** וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְגַלֶּה רָזִים הוֹדִיעֲךָ אֶת אֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה:


**and the Revealer of secrets** and the Holy One, blessed be He, Who reveals secrets, let you know what will come about.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:30:1)


**וַאֲנָה לָא בְחָכְמָה.** וַאֲנִי לֹא מֵרוֹב הַחָכְמָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בִּי מִשְּׁאָר הַבְּרִיּוֹת יוֹתֵר נִגְלָה לִי הַסּוֹד הַזֶּה:


**And I, not with wisdom** And I, not with superior wisdom that I have, more than other creatures, was this secret revealed to me.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:30:2)


**לָהֵן עַל דִּבְרַת דִּי פִשְׁרָא לְמַלְכָּא יְהוֹדְעוּן.** יוֹדִיעוּךָ מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**but in order that they should let the king know the interpretation** They should let you know from heaven.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:30:3)


**וְרַעְיוֹנֵי לִבְבָךְ תִּנְדַּע.** וְהִרְהוּרֵי לְבָבְךָ שֶׁאַתָּה מְהַרְהֵר תָּמִיד לָדַעַת מִי יָקוּם אַחֲרֶיךָ:


**and you should know the thoughts of your heart** and the thoughts of your heart about which you are constantly thinking, [which are] to know who will rise after you.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:30:4)


**תִּנְדַּע.** תֵּדַע:


**you should know** [as translated,] you should know.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:1)


**חֲזֵה הֲוַיְתָ.** רוֹאֶה הָיִיתָ:


**you were watching** [as translated,] you were watching.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:2)


**וְאֵלּוּ.** לְשׁוֹן וְהִנֵּה:


**and behold** Aram. וַאֲלוּ, an expression of “behold.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:3)


**צַלְמָא דִּכֵּן רַב.** צֶלֶם אֲשֶׁר הָיָה לוֹ כַּן גָּדוֹל:


**an image which had a large base** [as translated,] an image which had a large base.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:4)


**וְזִיוֵהּ יַתִּיר קָאֵם לְקָבְלָךְ.** הָיָה עוֹמֵד לְנֶגְדְּךָ:


**with unusual splendor, was standing opposite you** [as translated,] standing opposite you.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:31:5)


**וְרֵוֵהּ דְּחִיל.** וְתָאֳרוֹ נוֹרָא:


**and its form was frightening** [as translated,] and its form was frightening.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:32:1)


**דִּי דְהַב טָב.** שֶׁל זָהָב טוֹב׃


**of fine gold** [as translated,] of fine gold.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:32:2)


**חֲדוֹהִי.** חָזֶה שֶׁלּוֹ:


**its breast** [as translated,] its breast.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:32:3)


**וּדְרָעוֹהִי.** וּזְרוֹעוֹתָיו:


**and its arms** [as translated,] and its arms.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:32:4)


**מְעוֹהִי וְיַרְכָתֵהּ.** בִּטְנוֹ וִירֵכוֹתָיו:


**its belly and thighs** [as translated,] its belly and thighs.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:33:1)


**שָׁקוֹהִי דִּי פַרְזֶל.** שׁוֹקָיו שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל:


**Its legs were of iron** [as translated,] Its legs were of iron.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:33:2)


**מַנְהוֹן דִּי פַרְזֶל וּמִנְּהֵן דִּי חֲסַף.** מֵהֶם שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל וּמֵהֶם שֶׁל חֶרֶס:


**were partly of iron and partly of clay** [as translated,] were partly of iron and partly of clay [i.e., part of each foot was of iron and part of clay].



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:34:1)


**דִּי הִתְגְּזֶרֶת אֶבֶן.** אֲשֶׁר נֶחְתְּכָה וְנִבְדְּלָה אֶבֶן אַחַת:


**until one stone was hewn** that one stone was cut and separated.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:34:2)


**דִּי לָא בִידָיִן.** אֲשֶׁר לֹא בְיָדַיִם, כִּי אִם מֵאֵילֶיהָ:


**without hands** not with hands, but by itself.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:34:3)


**וּמְחָת לְצַלְמָא.** וְהִכִּיתָ אֶת הַצֶּלֶם:


**and it struck the image** [as translated,] and it struck the image.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:34:4)


**וְהַדֵּקֶת הִמּוֹן.** וְהִדִּיקָה אוֹתָם:


**and crumbled them** [as translated,] and crumbled them.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:1)


**בְּאֵדַיִן דָּקוּ כַחֲדָה.** אָז הוּדְקוּ יַחַד כָּל מִינֵי מַתָּכוֹת וְחֶרֶס שֶׁהָיָה בּוֹ:


**Then...crumbled together** Then all the types of metals and the clay that were in it crumbled together.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:2)


**וַהֲווֹ כְּעוּר מִן אִדְּרֵי קַיִט.** וְהָיוּ כְּמוֹץ מִגָּרְנוֹת הַקַּיִץ:


**and they were like chaff from the threshing floors of the summer** [as translated,] and they were like chaff from the threshing floors of the summer.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:3)


**וּנְשָׂא הֲמוֹן רוּחָא.** וְתִשָּׂא אוֹתָם הָרוּחַ:


**and the wind carried them off** [as translated,] and the wind carried them off.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:4)


**וְכָל אֲתַר לָא הִשְׁתְּכַח לְהוֹן.** כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא נוֹדַע מְקוֹמָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן, וְלֹא נִכָּר שֶׁהָיוּ שָׁם מֵעוֹלָם:


**and no place was found for them** i.e., their original place was unknown, and it was unrecognizable that they were ever there.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:35:5)


**הֲוָת לְטוּר רַב.** נַעֲשֵׂית הַר גָּדוֹל:


**became a huge mountain** [as translated,] became a huge mountain.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:36:1)


**דְּנָה חֶלְמָא.** הֲרֵי זֶה הַחֲלוֹם שֶׁחָלַמְתָּ:


**This is the dream** Behold this is the dream that you dreamed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:36:2)


**וּפִשְׁרֵיהּ נֵאמַר וְגוֹ׳.** וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ נֹאמַר לָךְ:


**and its interpretation we shall recite, etc.** and its interpretation we shall tell you.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:37:1)


**מֶלֶךְ מַלְכַיָּא.** פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה: כָּל מֶלֶךְ מַלְכַיָּא דְּדָנִיֵּאל מֶלֶךְ בָּשָׂר וָדָם, חוּץ מִזֶּה, שֶׁכְּנֶגֶד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אָמַר (שבועות לה:). וְכֵן פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: מֶלֶךְ הַמְּלָכִים שֶׁהוּא אֱלֹקֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**The King of kings** Our Sages, of blessed memory, explained (Shev. 35b): Every mention of “king” in Daniel refers to an earthly king, except this one, which he said in reference to the Holy One, blessed be He, and this is what it means: The King of kings, Who is the God of heaven.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:37:2)


**מַלְכוּתָא חִסְנָא.** מַלְכוּת חָזָק וְכָבֵד נָתַן לְךָ׃


**a strong... kingdom** gave you a strong and prominent kingdom.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:38:1)


**וְהַשְׁלְטָךְ בְּכָלְהוֹן.** וְהִשְׁלִיטְךָ עַל כֻּלָּם, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ גּוֹזֵר עַל הַסּוּס לֹא יְהֵא צוֹנֵף, וְעַל הָעוֹף לֹא יִפְרַח, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְאֶת חַיַּת הַשָּׂדֶה נָתַתִּי לְעָבְדוֹ״ (ירמיה כז:ו):


**and has given you dominion over them all** And He gave you dominion over them all, so that even if you decree upon a horse, it will not whinny, or upon a bird, it will not fly, as it is said (Jer. 27: 6): “and even the beasts of the field I have given to him to serve him.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:38:2)


**אַנְתְּ הוּא רֵאשָׁה דִּי דַהֲבָא.** רֹאשׁ הַזָּהָב שֶׁל הַצּוּרָה שֶׁרָאִיתָ אַתָּה הוּא, שֶׁמַּלְכוּתְךָ חֲזָקָה וְעַתָּה קַיֶּמֶת, וְהִיא חֲשׁוּבָה מְאֹד:


**You are the head of gold** You are the golden head of the image that you saw, for your kingdom is strong, and now it is in existence and is very prominent.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:39:1)


**וּבָתְרָךְ תְּקוּם מַלְכוּ אָחֳרִי אֲרַע מִנָּךְ**. וְאַחֲרֶיךָ, אַחַר מַלְכוּת בֵּלְשַׁצַּר בִּנְךָ, תָּקוּם מַלְכוּת שֶׁתִּטֹּל הַשְּׂרָרָה מִזַּרְעֲךָ, נְמוּכָה וּשְׁפֵלָה מִמַּלְכוּת שֶׁלְּךָ:


**And after you will arise another kingdom lower than you** And after you, after the reign of your son, Belshazzar, will arise a kingdom that will take the ruling power from your seed, lower and humbler than your kingdom.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:39:2)


**אֲרַע.** תַּחְתּוֹנָה וּנְמוּכָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהַכֶּסֶף נָמוּךְ וְשָׁפָל מֵהַזָּהָב, וְרָאִיתָ שֶׁהֶחָזֶה שֶׁהִיא אַחַר הָרֹאשׁ הִיא שֶׁל כֶּסֶף, כָּךְ תְּהֵא מַלְכוּת מָדַי וּפָרַס אַחַר מַלְכוּת בָּבֶל שְׁפָלָה מִמַּלְכוּת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**lower** Aram. אֶרַע, lower, as silver is lower and humbler than gold, and you saw that the breast, which is after the head, was of silver; so will the kingdom of Media and Persia, which will follow the kingdom of Babylon, be humbler than the kingdom of Nebuchadnezzar.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:39:3)


**וּמַלְכוּ תְּלִיתָאָה אָחֳרִי.** אַחֶרֶת:


**and another, a third kingdom** [as translated,] another.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:39:4)


**דִּי נְחָשָׁא.** חֲסִינָא כִּנְחֹשֶׁת, וְהִיא מַלְכוּת יָוָן מַלְכוּת אֲלֶכְּסַנְדְּרוֹס מוֹקְדָן:


**of copper** as strong as copper, and that is the kingdom of Alexander of Macedon.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:1)


**וּמַלְכוּ רְבִיעָאָה.** הִיא קָשָׁה **כְּפַרְזְלָא**, כַּאֲשֶׁר רָאִיתָ הַשּׁוֹקַיִם שֶׁהֵם רְבִיעִים לָרֹאשׁ וְלֶחָזֶה וְלַמֵּעַיִם וְהֵם שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל:


**And a fourth kingdom** will be as hard as iron, as you saw the thighs, which are the fourth from the head, the breast, and the belly, and they are of iron.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:2)


**מְהַדֵּק וְחָשֵׁל כֹּלָּא.** מְרַדֵּד וּמְהַדֵּק כָּל מִינֵי מַתָּכוֹת שֶׁמְּרַדְּדִין אוֹתָם בְּקוּרְנָס:


**crumbles and flattens everything** flattens and crumbles all kinds of metal, which are flattened with a hammer.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:3)


**וְחָשֵׁל**. וּמְרַדֵּד, וְהַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא: חַשְּׁלֵי דּוּדֵי (כתובות עז.), בֵּיעֵי חֲשִׁילְתָא (חולין צב.):


**and flattens** Aram. וְחָשֵּׁל, and flattens. There are many such [words] in the Talmud. Keth. 77a, “who thins copper for kettles (חַשְּׁלָא דוּדֵי)”; Hul. 93a: “crushed testicles (בֵּיעֵי חֳשִּׁלָתָא).”


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:4)


**כָּל אִלֵּין.** כָּל מִינֵי מַתָּכוֹת הַלָּלוּ שֶׁרָאִיתָ בַּצֶּלֶם:


**all these** all these kinds of metals that you saw in the image.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:40:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:40:5)


**תַּדִּיק וְתֵרֹעַ.** תַּדִּיק וּתְרוֹצֵץ:


**it will crumble and shatter** It will crumble and shatter all the nations.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:41:1)


**חֲסַף דִּי פֶחָר.** חֶרֶס שֶׁל יוֹצֵר, וְהַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא מָאנֵי דְפַחְרָא (תענית ז.):


**potter’s clay** Aram. חֲסַף דִּי פֶחָר, a potter’s clay, and there are many instances of this in the Talmud (Taanith 7a): “vessels of a potter (מָאנֵי דְפַחְרָא).”


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:41:2)


**מַלְכוּ פְלִיגָה תֶהֱוֵה.** מַלְכוּת חֲלוּקָה תִּהְיֶה, שְׁנֵי מְלָכִים יִהְיוּ מִמֶּנָּה כְּאֶחָד, חָזָק וְחַלָּשׁ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְפֹרָשׁ לְמַטָּה **מִן קְצָת מַלְכוּתָא תֶהֱוֵה תַקִּיפָא**:


**it will be a divided kingdom** It will be a divided kingdom; two kings will rise from it at once, strong and weak, as explained below (verse 42): “part of the kingdom will be strong.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:41:3)


**וּמִן נִצְבְּתָא דִי פַרְזְלָא לֶהֱוֵא בַהּ.** אַף הַחַלָּשׁ יְהֵא חָזָק עַל אֲחֵרִים עַל יְדֵי חוֹזֶק חֲבֵרוֹ, שֶׁיִּירְאוּ מִפָּנָיו. וְזֶה **מִן נִצְבְּתָא דִי פַרְזְלָא לֶהֱוֵא בַהּ**, מִן חוֹזֶק מַצַּב הַבַּרְזֶל, יִהְיֶה בַּמֶּלֶךְ הַחַלָּשׁ:


**and in it will be some of the strength of iron** Even the weak one will be stronger than the other nations through the strength of his fellow, for they will fear him. And this is the meaning of וּמִן נִצְבְּתָא דִּי פַרְזְלָא לֶהֶוֵא בָהּ ; some of the strength of the stability of iron will be in the weak king.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:41:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:41:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:41:4)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דִּי חֲזַיְתָה.** כָּל־עוּמַּת שֶׁרָאִיתָ בַּרְזֶל מְעֹרָב בַּחֲרָסִית הַטִּיט:


**in view of what you saw** in view of what you saw—iron mixed with clay.



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:43:1)


**מִתְעָרְבִין לֶהֱוֹן בִּזְרַע אֲנָשָׁא וְגוֹ׳.** מִתְחַתְּנִים יִהְיוּ עִם שְׁאָר הָאֻמּוֹת, וְלֹא יִהְיוּ שְׁלֵמִים וְנִדְבָּקִים עִמָּם בֶּאֱמֶת וְלֵב שָׁלֵם, וְדָתֵיהֶם שׁוֹנוֹת מִדָּתֵי שְׁאָר הָאֻמּוֹת:


**that they will mingle with the seed of men** They will intermarry with the other nations but they will not be at peace and truly cleave to them wholeheartedly, and their laws will differ from the laws of the other nations.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:43:2)


**הָא כְּדִי פַרְזְלָא לָא מִתְעָרַב.** הֲלֹא הוּא כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֵין הַבַּרְזֶל נִדְבָּק יָפֶה עִם הַחֶרֶס:


**as iron does not mix with clay** Is it not just as iron does not stick well to clay?



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:44:1)


**וּבְיוֹמֵיהוֹן דִּי מַלְכַיָּא אִנּוּן.** בִּיוֹמֵיהוֹן שֶׁל מְלָכִים הַלָּלוּ, בְּעוֹד מַלְכוּתָם שֶׁל רוֹמִיִּים קַיֶּמֶת:


**And in the days of these kings** in the days of these kings, when the kingdom of Rome is still in existence.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:44:2)


**יְקִים אֱלָהּ שְׁמַיָּא.** מַלְכוּת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁלְּעוֹלָם **לֹא תִתְחַבַּל מַלְכוּ**, וְהִיא מַלְכוּת מֶלֶךְ הַמָּשִׁיחַ:


**the God Of heaven will set up a kingdom** The kingdom of the Holy One, blessed be He, which will never be destroyed, is the kingdom of the Messiah.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:44:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:44:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:44:3)


**תַּדִּק וְתָסֵף.** תַּדִּק וּתְכַלֶּה כָּל אֵלּוּ הַמַּלְכֻיּוֹת:


**it will crumble and destroy** It will crumble and destroy all these kingdoms.



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:45:1)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דִּי חֲזֵית.** כָּל עוּמַּת שֶׁרָאִיתָ שֶׁנִּתְפָּרְקָה אֶבֶן מִן הָהָר שֶׁהִדִּיקָה כָּל הַצֶּלֶם, זֶה פִּתְרוֹן שֶׁהַמַּלְכוּת הַחֲמִישִׁית תְּכַלֶּה וּתְרוֹצֵץ אֶת כֻּלָּם:


**Just as you saw** just as you saw that a stone was broken off the mountain, which crumbled the entire image. This is the interpretation that the fifth kingdom will destroy and shatter them all.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:45:2)


**מָה דִּי לֶהֱוֵא אַחֲרֵי דְנָה.** מַה שֶׁיִּהְיֶה אַחַר זֹאת, אַחַר מַלְכוּת זוֹ שֶׁלְּךָ:


**what will be after this** what will be after this, after this kingdom of yours.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:45:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:45:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:45:3)


**וְיַצִּיב חֶלְמָא.** אֱמֶת הוּא הַחֲלוֹם:


**and its interpretation is reliable** for the Eternal of Israel neither lies nor repents.



### Verse 46

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:46:1)


**אָמַר לְנַסְּכָה.** רָצָה לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ אֱלוֹהַּ:


**ordered to offer up... libations** He wished to deify him.



### Verse 47

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:47:1)


**מִן קְשׁוֹט.** בֶּאֱמֶת:


**truly** [as translated,] in truth.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:47:2)


**וּמָרֵא מַלְכִין.** אֲדוֹנֵי הָאֲדוֹנִים:


**and the Master of the kings** The Lord of lords.


###### Rashi on Daniel 2:47:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:47:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:47:3)


**רָזָא דְנָה.** סֵתֶר זֶה:


**this secret** [as translated,] this secret.



### Verse 48

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:48:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:48:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:48:1)


**לְדָנִיֵּאל רַבִּי.** לְדָנִיֵּאל גָּדַל עַל כָּל הַשָּׂרִים:


**elevated Daniel** He elevated Daniel over all the princes.



### Verse 49

###### Rashi on Daniel 2:49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/2:49:1)


**וּמַנִּי עַל עֲבִידְתָּא דִּי מְדִינַת בָּבֶל.** וַיַּפְקֵד עַל כָּל צָרְכֵי הַמַּמְלָכוֹת אֶת חֲנַנְיָה מִישָׁאֵל וַעֲזַרְיָה:


**and he appointed, over the affairs of the capital cities of Babylon** He appointed, over all the necessities of the kingdom, Hananiah Mishael, and Azariah.



## Chapter 3


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:1:1)


**עֲבַד וְגוֹ׳.** מָלְאָה לִבּוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת צֶלֶם שֶׁל זָהָב:


**made an image** He had an urge to make an image of gold.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:1:2)


**פְּתָיֵהּ אַמִּין שִׁית אֲקִימֵהּ וְגוֹ׳.** אִם אֵין עָבְיוֹ אֶלָּא שֵׁשׁ, הֵיאַךְ יָכוֹל לַעֲמֹד בְּגֹבַהּ **אַמִּין שִׁתִּין**? אָמַר רַב בִּיבִי: מַעֲמִידִין אוֹתוֹ וְנוֹפֵל, עַד שֶׁהֵבִיאוּ כָּל זָהָב שֶׁל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם וְשָׁפְכוּ דִּימוֹס עַל רַגְלָיו, לְקַיֵּם מַה שֶּׁנֶּאֱמַר (יחזקאל ז:יט) ״וְזָהָבָם לְנִדָּה יִהְיֶה״:


**its width six cubits; he set it up etc.** If its thickness was only six [cubits], how could it stand at a height of sixty cubits? Said Rav Bibi: They would set it up and it would fall, until they brought all the gold of Jerusalem and poured a base around its feet, to fulfill what was said (Ezek. 7:19): “and their gold will be for a repugnant thing.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:1:3)


**בְּבִקְעַת דּוּרָא.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם׃


**in the plain of Dura** the name of a place.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:2:1)


**לַאֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנַיָּא.** דּוּכְסִין:


**the satraps** Aram. לַאֲחַשְּׁדַרְפְּנַיָּא dukes.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:2:2)


**פַּחֲוָתָא.** שָׁלְטוֹנִין:


**governors** rulers.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:2:3)


**אֲדַרְגָּזְרַיָּא גְדָבְרַיָּא וְגוֹ׳.** כֻּלָּם שֵׁם עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת הֵם:


**the judges, the treasurers, etc.** They are all names of nations.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:2:4)


**לַחֲנֻכַּת צַלְמָא.** תְּחִלַּת עֲבוֹדוֹתָיו:


**the dedication of the image** the beginning of its worship.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:4:1)


**וְכָרוֹזָא קָרֵא בְחָיִל.** הַכָּרוֹז יוֹצֵא בְּכֹחַ גָּדוֹל:


**And the announcement was issued aloud** [as translated,] and the announcement was issued aloud.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:4:2)


**לְכוֹן אָמְרִין עַמְמַיָּא.** לָכֶם כָּל הָעַמִּים וְהַלְּשׁוֹנוֹת אוֹמְרִים מִכְּרוּז מִצְוַת הַמֶּלֶךְ בְּמִשְׁלַחַת הַמֶּלֶךְ׃


**To you we are saying, O peoples** [as translated,] To you, all peoples and nations, we are announcing the command of the king as a message from the king.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:5:1)


**בְּעִדָּנָא דְּתִשְׁמְעוּן קָל קַרְנָא מַשְׁרוֹקִיתָא.** קוֹל הַקֶּרֶן שׁוֹרֶקֶת, כְּמוֹ ״אֶשְׁרְקָה לָהֶם וַאֲקַבְּצֵם״ (זכריה י:ח):


**At the time you hear the sound of the whistling horn** Aram. קָל קַרְנָא מַשְּׁרוֹקֵיתָא, the sound of the whistling horn, like (Zech. 10:8) “I will whistle (אֶשְּׁרְקָא) to them, and I will gather them.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:5:2)


**קִתְרוֹס סַבְּכָא וְגוֹ׳.** כֻּלָּם כְּלֵי זֶמֶר הֵם:


**the clavichord, the harp, etc.** They are all musical instruments.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:5:3)


**זְנֵי זְמָרָא.** מִינֵי זֶמֶר. ״לְמִינוֹ״ (בראשית א:יא) תַּרְגּוּם אוּנְקְלוֹס: לִזְנוֹהִי:


**kinds of music** Aram. זְנֵי זְמָרָא kinds of music. (Gen. 1:11) “After its kind,” Onkelos renders: לִזְנוֹהִי.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:6:1)


**אַתּוּן נוּרָא.** גּוּמָּא שֶׁעוֹשִׂין לִשְׂרוֹף בָּהּ אֲבָנִים לְסִיד, וְהוּא כִּבְשָׁן שֶׁבְּכָל הַמִּקְרָא:


**a fiery furnace** a pit that is made to burn rocks into lime, and that is כִּבְשָּׁן in all the Scriptures.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:7:1)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דְּנָה.** כָּל עוּמַּת זוֹ שֶׁנִּזְהֲרוּ בְּמִצְוַת הַמֶּלֶךְ עַל כָּךְ:


**In view of this** in view of this, that they took heed to observe the king’s command concerning this bagpipes would be played.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:7:2)


**בֵּהּ זִמְנָא.** בּוֹ בַּזְּמַן שֶׁיִּשְׁמְעוּ קוֹל הַזֶּמֶר, שֶׁהוּא לְסִימָן שֶׁיָּבוֹאוּ הַמִּשְׁתַּחֲוִים לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת, **נָפְלִין כָּל עַמְמַיָּא וְגוֹ׳**:


**at that time** At the time that they hear the sound of the music, which is a sign that those who prostrate themselves should come to prostrate, all peoples would fall etc.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:8:1)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דְּנָה.** כָּל עוּמַּת זוֹ שֶׁרָאוּ שׂוֹנְאֵי הַיְּהוּדִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ שָׁעָה לְהַלְשִׁין עֲלֵיהֶם:


**In view of this** in view of this, that their enemies saw that there is a time to denounce them.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:8:2)


**קְרִיבוּ גֻבְרִין כַּשְׂדָּאִין וַאֲכַלוּ קַרְצֵיהוֹן.** הִלְשִׁינוּ עֲלֵיהֶם, ״לֹא תֵלֵךְ רָכִיל״ (ויקרא יט:טז) מְתַרְגְּמִינָן ״וְלָא תֵיכוּל קוּרְצִין״, וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי שֶׁכֵּן הָיְתָה דַּת שֶׁל הוֹלְכֵי רָכִיל לֶאֱכֹל לְגִימָה בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁמַּלְשִׁינִין שָׁם, וְהוּא סִימָן קִיּוּם דְּבָרִים, שֶׁמֵּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה יֵשׁ עֲלֵיהֶם לְבָרֵר דִּבְרֵיהֶם וּלְאַמֵּת לָשׁוֹן הָרַע שֶׁאָמְרוּ. וְאוֹתָהּ סְעוּדָה נִקְרֵאת אֲכִילַת קוּרְצִין, לְשׁוֹן ״קוֹרֵץ בְּעֵינָיו״ (משלי ו:יג), שֶׁהוּא לְסִימָן לָשׁוֹן הָרַע:


**some Chaldean men approached and denounced** Aram. וַאֲכַלוּ קַרְצֵיהוֹן informed about them. (Lev. 19: 16) “Do not go as a talebearer among your people,” is translated by Targum as קוּרצִין לָא תֵיכוּ I say that it was the custom of talebearers to eat a meal in the place where they informed, and that was a sign of the verification of their words; for at that time it was incumbent upon them to clarify their words and to verify the slander that they spoke, and that meal was called eating קוּרצִין, an expression of (Prov. 6:13) “he winks (קוֹרֵץ) with his eyes,” which is a sign of slander.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:9:1)


**עָנוֹ וְאָמְרִין.** צָוְחוּ. כָּל עֲנִיָּה שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא לְשׁוֹן קוֹל רָם הוּא:


**They spoke up and said** Aram. עֲנוֹ, they shouted. Every [expression of] עֲנִיָה in Scriptures is an expression of raising the voice.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:10:1)


**שַׂמְתָּ טְּעֵם.** גָּזַרְתָּ דָּבָר:


**gave an order** issued a decree.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:12:1)


**אִיתַי.** יֵשׁ:


**There are** Aram. אִיתַי


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:12:2)


**יְהוּדָאִין.** מִשֵּׁבֶט יְהוּדָה:


**Judahite men** of the tribe of Judah.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:12:3)


**אֵלֶּךְ.** אֵלֶּה:


**these** Aram. אִלֵּךְ


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:12:4)


**לֹא שָׂמוּ עֲלָךְ מַלְכָּא טְעֵם.** לֹא חָשׁוּ לָתֵת עֵצָה לְעַצְמָן לָחוּשׁ עַל גְּזֵרוֹתֶיךָ:


**did not take counsel to follow your decree** Aram. לָא שָּׂמוּ עֲלָיִךְ מַלְכָּא טְעֵם. They did not care to give themselves counsel to care for your decrees.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:13:1)


**אֲמַר לְהַיְתָיָה.** צִוָּה לְהָבִיא:


**ordered to bring** [as translated,] ordered to bring.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:13:2)


**הֵיתָיוּ.** הוּבָאוּ׃


**were brought** [as translated,] were brought.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:14:1)


**הַצְדָא.** שֶׁמָּא גְּזֵרוֹתַי צָדוּ וְשַׁמָּה וְדָבָר רֵיק הִיא בְּעֵינֵיכֶם, אַתֶּם **שַׁדְרַךְ מֵישַׁךְ וַעֲבֵד נְגוֹ** אֲשֶׁר לֵאלֹהַי אֵינְכֶם עוֹבְדִים? ה״א שֶׁל **הַצְדָא** מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת בִּלְשׁוֹן תְּמִיהָה:


**Is it meaningless** Aram. הַצְדָא. Are my decrees void and desolate and an empty thing in your eyes, Shadrach, Meshach, and Abednego, that you do not worship my god? The “hey” of הַצְדָא is used as an interrogative expression.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:14:2)


**דִּי הֲקֵימֶת.** שֶׁהֲקִימוֹתִי:


**that I have set up** [as translated,] that I have set up.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:15:1)


**כְּעַן הֵן אִיתֵיכוֹן עֲתִידִין.** עַתָּה הִנְּכֶם עֲתִידִים עַל כָּרְחֲכֶם לְקַבֵּל זוֹ עֲלֵיכֶם:


**Now, behold you are destined** Now, behold you are destined against your will to accept this upon yourselves.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:15:2)


**וְהֵן לָא תִסְגְּדוּן.** וְאִם לֹא תִשְׁתַּחֲווּ תִּהְיוּ מֻשְׁלָכִים:


**and if you do not prostrate yourselves** and if you do not prostrate yourselves, you shall be cast.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:15:3)


**לְגוֹ.** לְתוֹךְ:


**into** into the midst of.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:15:4)


**וּמַן הוּא אֱלָהּ.** וּמִי הוּא שׁוּם אֱלוֹהַּ שֶׁיַּצִּיל אֶתְכֶם מִיָּדִי?


**and who is a god** and who is a god who will save you from my hands?



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:16:1)


**לְמַלְכָּא נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר.** לָמָּה הוּזְכַּר שְׁמוֹ? אֶלָּא כָּךְ אָמְרוּ לוֹ: אִם לְקַבֵּל עָלֵינוּ גְּזֵרָתְךָ לָתֵת מִסִּין וְאַרְנוֹנִיּוֹת וְגֻלְגָּלִיּוֹת, אַתָּה מֶלֶךְ עָלֵינוּ, אִם לִכְפּוֹר בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר שְׁפַל אֲנָשִׁים בְּעֵינֵינוּ וּבָזוּי עַם אַתָּה:


**to the king, ‘Nebuchadnezzar** Why is his name mentioned? But this is what they said to him: “If [it concerns] taking upon ourselves to pay taxes, either the tax from the crops or the head taxes, you are king over us, [but] if [it concerns] denying the Holy One, blessed be He, [you are merely] Nebuchadnezzar. In our eyes, you are a lowly person and the most despised of the people. You and a dog are equal.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:16:2)


**לֹא חַשְׁחִין אֲנַחְנָא.** לֹא חוֹשְׁשִׁין אָנוּ עַל זֹאת לִטּוֹל עֵצָה מַה לְּהָשִׁיבְךָ, כִּי הַמַּעֲנֶה מוּכָן וְשָׁגוּר בְּפִינוּ:


**we do not care** We do not care to take counsel on what to answer you, for the answer is ready and fluent in our mouths.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:17:1)


**יָכִיל לְשֵׁיזָבוּתַנָא.** יָכוֹל לְהַצִּילֵנוּ מִכָּל צָרָה:


**He can save us** He can save us from all our troubles.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:17:2)


**מִן אַתּוּן נוּרָא.** וּמִן יְדָךְ מַלְכָּא יְשֵׁיזִיב מִן הַכִּבְשָׁן וּמִן יָדְךָ יַצִּיל׃


**From the burning fiery furnace and from your hands, O king, He will save us** from the furnace and from your hands He will save.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:18:1)


**וְהֵן לָא.** וְאִם לֹא יַחְפֹּץ לְהַצִּיל גּוּפֵינוּ **יְדִיעַ לֶהֱוֵי לָךְ**:


**And if not** And if He will not wish to save our bodies, let it be known to you, etc.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:1)


**הִתְמְלִי חֲמָא.** נִתְמַלֵּא חֵמָה:


**was filled with wrath** [as translated,] was filled with wrath.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:2)


**וּצְלֵם אַנְפּוֹהִי אֶשְׁתַּנִּי.** וּפַרְצוּף פָּנָיו נִשְׁתַּנּוּ:


**and the features of his face changed** [as translated,] and the features of his face changed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:3)


**לְמֵזֵא לְאַתּוּנָא.** לְהַצִּית לְכִבְשַׁן הָאֵשׁ וּלְחַמְּמוֹ׃


**to heat the furnace** to light the fiery furnace and to heat it.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:4)


**חַד שִׁבְעָה עַל דִּי חֲזֵה לְמֵזְיֵהּ.** עַל אַחַת שֶׁבַע פְּעָמִים שֶׁשִּׁעֲרוּהוּ עַד כְּדֵי הֶסֵּקוֹ, וּכְאִלּוּ יַבְעִירוּהוּ שִׁבְעָה שִׁעוּרִין כָּמוֹהוּ:


**seven times as much as it should be heated** instead of once, seven times as much as they calculated to heat it up; and it was as though they would kindle it seven times as much as it required.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:19:5)


**לְמֵזְיֵהּ.** לְהַסִּיקוֹ:


**to be heated** lit. to heat it.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:20:1)


**וּלְגֻבְרִין גִּבָּרֵי חַיִל דִּי בְחֵילֵהּ.** וְלַאֲנָשִׁים גִּבּוֹרֵי כֹחַ אֲשֶׁר בְּחֵילוֹ:


**certain mighty men in his army** [as translated,] certain mighty men in his army.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:20:2)


**אָמַר לְכַפָּתָה.** צִוָּה לֶאֱסוֹר אוֹתָם, שֶׁרָאָה מִתְחַזְּקִים לַעֲנוֹתוֹ קָשׁוֹת, וְאָמַר: גִּבּוֹרִים הֵם אֵלּוּ, וְצִוָּה לַגִּבּוֹרִים אֲשֶׁר בְּחֵילוֹ לֶאֱסוֹר אוֹתָם:


**And he commanded...to bind** He commanded to bind them because he saw that they were strengthening themselves to answer him harshly, and he said, “These are mighty”; so he commanded the mighty men in his army to bind them.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:1)


**גּוּבְרַיָּא אִלֵּךְ.** אֲנָשִׁים אֵלּוּ:


**these men** [as translated,] these men.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:2)


**כְּפִיתוּ בְּסַרְבָּלֵיהוֹן.** נִתְקַשְּׁרוּ בְּבִגְדֵי מַעֲטְפוֹתָן:


**were bound in their cloaks** They were bound in the garments in which they enwrapped themselves.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:3)


**פַּטְּשֵׁיהוֹן וְכַרְבְּלָתְהוֹן** מִינֵי מַלְבּוּשׁ מַלְכוּת שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָיוּ יוֹצְאִין לִידּוֹן לִפְנֵי נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר דִּינֵי מִיתָה, לֹא שִׁנּוּ בִגְדֵיהֶם לְהַשְׁפִּיל אֶת גְּדֻלָּתָם. מִכָּאן לָמְדוּ חֲכָמִים, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ בִּשְׁעַת הַסַּכָּנָה לֹא יְשַׁנֶּה אָדָם מִן הַשְּׂרָרָה שֶׁהֻחְזַק בָּהּ:


**their trousers, their robes** types of royal raiment that they had; and although they were going forth to be judged before Nebuchadnezzar regarding their death sentence, they did not change their clothes to humble their greatness. From here the Sages learned that even in times of danger, a person should not change from the ruling position to which he has become accustomed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:4)


**וְכַרְבְּלָתְהוֹן.** יֵשׁ לוֹ דֻגְמָא: ״וְדָוִד מְכֻרְבָּל״ (דברי הימים א טו:כז):


**their robes** Aram. וְכַרְבְּלָתְהוֹן. There is a word similar to this (in I Chron. 15: 27): “And David was clothed (מְכֻרְבָּל).


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:21:5)


**וּרְמִיו לְגוֹא אַתּוּן נוּרָא.** וְהֻשְׁלְכוּ לְתוֹךְ כִּבְשָׁן בּוֹעֵרָה:


**and they were cast into the burning, fiery furnce** [as translated,] and they were cast into the burning, fiery furnace.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:22:1)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דְּנָה.** כָּל עוּמַּת זֹאת אֲשֶׁר מִצְוַת הַמֶּלֶךְ הָיְתָה עַזָּה:


**In view of this** in view of this, that the king’s command was strong.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:22:2)


**וְאַתּוּנָא אֵזֵה יַתִּירָא.** וְהַכִּבְשָׁן הוּסַּק מְאֹד:


**and the furnace was heated excessively** and the furnace was heated to a great degree.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:22:3)


**גּוּבְרַיָּא אִלֵּךְ.** אֵלּוּ הָאֲנָשִׁים הַגִּבּוֹרִים אֲשֶׁר קָשְׁרוּ אֶת חֲנַנְיָה מִישָׁאֵל וַעֲזַרְיָה וְהִשְׁלִיכוּם לַכִּבְשָׁן:


**these men** these mighty men who had bound Hananiah, Mishael, and Azariah and had cast them into the furnace.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:22:4)


**קְטִיל הִמּוֹן שְׁבִיבָא דִּי נוּרָא.** שָׂרַף אוֹתָם כֹּחַ שְׁבִיב הָאֵשׁ:


**were killed by a spark of fire** The power of a spark of fire burned them.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:23:1)


**מְכַפְּתִין.** קְשׁוּרִין:


**bound** [as translated,] bound.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:24:1)


**תְּוַהּ.** תָּמַהּ׃


**was bewildered** Aram. תְּוַהּ, wondered.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:24:2)


**וְקָם בְּהִתְבַּהֲלָה.** וְעָמַד בְּבֶהָלָה:


**and stood up in haste** Aram. וְקָם בְּהִתְבְּהָלָה.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:24:3)


**עָנַיִן וְאָמְרִין.** עוֹנִין וְאוֹמְרִים לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**answered and said** Aram. עָנַיִן וְאָמְרִין They answered and said to the king.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:24:4)


**יַצִּיבָא מַלְכָּא.** אֱמֶת דְּבַר הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**The king is true** Aram. יַצִיבָא מַלְכָּא The king has spoken the truth.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:25:1)


**שְׁרַיִן מַהְלְכִין בְּגוֹא נוּרָא.** מוּתָּרִין מֵאֲסוּרֵיהֶן וְהוֹלְכִים בְּתוֹךְ הָאֵשׁ:


**free men walking in the midst of the fire** freed from their bonds and walking in the midst of the fire.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:25:2)


**וַחֲבָל לָא אִיתַי בְּהוֹן.** וַחֲבָלָה לֹא יֵשׁ בָּהֶם:


**and there is no wound upon them** [as translated,] and there is no wound upon them.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:25:3)


**וְרֵוֵהּ דִּי.** רְבִיעָאָה וְתוֹאֲרוֹ שֶׁל רְבִיעִי:


**and the form of the fourth** [as translated,] and the form of the fourth.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:25:4)


**דָּמֵה לְבַר אֱלָהִין.** דּוֹמֶה לְמַלְאָךְ שֶׁרָאִיתִי בִּהְיוֹתִי עִם סַנְחֵרִיב כְּשֶׁנִּשְׂרְפוּ אוּכְלוּסָיו, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיַּךְ בְּמַחֲנֵה אַשּׁוּר״ (מלכים ב יט:לה), וּנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר הָיָה שָׁם וְנִמְלַט בְּתוֹךְ עֲשָׂרָה שֶׁנִּמְלְטוּ מֵהֶם:


**is like [that of] an angel** It is like the angel I saw when I was with Sannecherib, when his armies were burned, as it is said (II Kings 19: 35): “[And an angel of the Lord went forth] and slew...of the camp of Assyria.” And Nebuchadnezzar was there and fled among the ten who fled from them.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:26:1)


**קָרֵב לִתְרַע אַתּוּן נוּרָא** נִגַּשׁ לְשַׁעַר הֶחָצֵר שֶׁהַכִּבְשָׁן בְּתוֹכָהּ, לְדַבֵּר עִמָּהֶם; וּמִתְּחִלָּה הָיָה רוֹאֶה אוֹתָם מֵרָחוֹק:


**approached the gate of the... fiery furnace** He approached the gate of the court, in which the furnace was situated, to speak with them, and at first, he saw them from afar.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:26:2)


**פּוּקוּ וֶאֱתוֹ.** צְאוּ וּבֹאוּ, וְלֹא אָמַר לָהֶם עֲלוּ וּבֹאוּ. מִכָּאן לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁצָּף קַרְקָעִית הַכִּבְשָׁן וְהִשְׁוָה לָאָרֶץ, שֶׁלֹּא יִטְרְחוּ לַעֲלוֹת:


**Step out and come** “step out and come,” but he did not say to them, “step out and ascend.” From here we learn that the floor of the furnace floated up and became even with the ground, so that they should not be troubled to go up.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:26:3)


**בְּאֵדַיִן נָפְקוּן.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּטוּחִין בַּנֵּס הֶעָשׂוּי לָהֶם, לֹא מָלְאָה לִבָּם לְזַלְזֵל בַּמַּלְכוּת לָצֵאת שֶׁלֹּא בִּרְשׁוּת:


**Then...came out** Although they were confident of the miracle that had been performed for them, they did not dare to treat the king lightly, to go out without permission.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:1)


**וּמִתְכַּנְּשִׁין אֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנַיָּא.** אַרְבַּע אֻמּוֹת נִמְנוּ לְמַעְלָה בָּאֲסִיפָה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁלֹּא נִמְנוּ כָּאן: אֲדַרְגָּזְרַיָּא דְּתָבְרַיָּא גְּדָבְרַיָּא תִּפְתָּיָא, הֵם גִּבּוֹרֵי הַחַיִל אֲשֶׁר הֲרָגָם שְׁבִיב הָאֵשׁ בְּהַשְׁלִיכָם אֶת חֲנַנְיָה וַחֲבֵרָיו לָאוּר:


**And the satraps...gathered** Four nations were counted above in the first gathering who were not counted here: Adargazeria, Dethaveria, Gedaveria, Tiftaei. They are the mighty men whom the spark of fire had killed when they cast Hananiah and his colleagues into the fire.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:2)


**חָזַיִן לְגֻבְרַיָּא אִלֵּךְ.** רוֹאִין אֶת הָאֲנָשִׁים הָאֵלֶּה, חֲנַנְיָה וַחֲבֵרָיו:


**saw these men** saw these men: Hananiah and his colleagues.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:3)


**דִּי לָא שְׁלֵט נוּרָא בְּגֶשְׁמְהוֹן.** אֲשֶׁר לֹא שָׁלְטָה הָאֵשׁ בְּגוּפָם:


**that the fire had taken no effect on their bodies** [as translated,] that the fire had taken no effect on their bodies.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:4)


**גִּשְׁמְהוֹן.** גּוּפָן בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי, וְיֵשׁ רַבִּים בְּזֶה הַסֵּפֶר:


**on their bodies** בְּגֶשְּׁמְהוֹן, their bodies, in Aramaic, and there are many [instances of it] in this Book.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:5)


**וּשְׂעַר רֵאשֵׁיהוֹן לָא הִתְחָרַךְ.** שְׂעַר רֵאשֵׁיהוֹן לָא נֶחֱרַךְ:


**the hair of their heads was not singed** [as translated,] the hair of their heads was not singed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:6)


**לֹא שָׁנוּ.** לֹא נִשְׁתַּנּוּ מִמַּרְאִיתָן:


**their robes were not changed** They did not change from their appearance.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:27:7)


**לֹא עֲדָת בְּהוֹן.** לֹא סָרָה לְתוֹכָם:


**and the smell of fire had not been absorbed by them** Aram. לָא עֲדַת בְּהוֹן, did not pass into their midst.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:1)


**עָנֵה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר.** צָוַח נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**Nebuchadnezzar cried out** [as translated,] Nebuchadnezzar cried out.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:2)


**דִּי הִתְרְחִיצוּ עֲלוֹהִי.** אֲשֶׁר בָּטְחוּ עָלָיו:


**who trusted him** [as translated,] who trusted him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:3)


**וּמִלַּת מַלְכָּא שַׁנִּיו.** וּמִלַּת הַמֶּלֶךְ שִׁנּוּ שֶׁלֹּא לְקַיְּמוֹ:


**deviated from the command of the king** They deviated from the king’s command by not fulfilling it.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:4)


**וִיהַבוּ גֶשְׁמְהוֹן.** וּמָסְרוּ גוּפָם:


**and risked their lives** lit. delivered their bodies.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:28:5)


**לֵאלָהֲהוֹן.** זוּלָתִי לֵאלֹהֵיהֶם:


**except to their God** [as translated,] except to their God.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:1)


**שִׂים טְעֵם.** כְּלוֹמַר: יוּשַׂם חֹק:


**Now an order is issued by me** [as translated,] Now an order is issued by me.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:2)


**דִּי יֵאָמַר שָׁלוּ.** אֲשֶׁר יֹאמַר מִשְׁגֶּה, כְּלוֹמַר: שׁוּם דָּבָר שֶׁל גְּנַאי:


**that will speak amiss** that will speak in error, i.e., any disrespectful word.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:3)


**הַדָּמִין.** אֵבָרִים, כְּלוֹמַר: יְנַתְּחוּהוּ לְאֵבָרִים:


**limb from limb** Aram הַּדָּמִין limbs; i.e., they will cut him into limbs.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:4)


**נְוָלִי יִשְׁתַּוֶּה.** אַשְׁפָּה יוּשַׂם:


**shall be made a dungheap** [as translated,] shall be made a dungheap.


###### Rashi on Daniel 3:29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:29:5)


**דִּי יְכוּל לְהַצָּלָה כִּדְנָה.** אֲשֶׁר יָכוֹל לְהַצִּיל כָּזֹאת:


**that can save in this manner** [as translated,] that can save in this manner.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:30:1)


**הַצְלַח.** הִצְלִיחַ וְהִגְדִּיל אוֹתָם:


**made...prosper** made them prosper and aggrandized them.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:31:1)


**נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר מַלְכָּא לְכָל עַמְמַיָּא.** זֶה טֹפֶס כְּתַב הָאִגֶּרֶת: נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר הַמֶּלֶךְ שׁוֹאֵל לְשָׁלוֹם לְכָל הָעַמִּים:


**King Nebuchadnezzar [says] to all peoples** This is the text of the missive: King Nebuchadnezzar greets all peoples.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Daniel 3:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/3:32:1)


**שְׁפַר קֳדָמַי לְהַחֲוָיָה.** טוֹב לִי לְהַגִּיד:


**it behooves me to recite** It is good for me to tell.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:1:1)


**שְׁלֵה הֲוֵית בְּבֵיתִי.** שָׁלֵיו הָיִיתִי:


**was tranquil in my house** [as translated,] was tranquil in my house.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:2:1)


**וַיְדַחֲלִנַּנִּי.** וְאִיֵּם עָלַי וְהִפְחִידַנִי:


**and it frightened me** It brought upon me fear and fright.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:5:1)


**וְעַד אָחֳרָן.** וְעַד אֲשֶׁר בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה הוּבָא לִפְנֵי דָּנִיֵּאל:


**Until, at last,** until, at last, Daniel was brought in before me.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:5:2)


**כְּשׁוּם אֱלָהִי.** עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁל בָּבֶל בֵּל שְׁמָהּ, טְשַׁצַּר שֵׁם לְשׁוֹן חָכְמָה הוּא בָּאוּמּוֹת:


**like the name of my god** The deity of Babylon was named Bel. Teshazzar is the name of an expression of wisdom in Aramaic.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:6:1)


**וְכָל רָז לָא אָנֵס לָךְ.** וְכָל סָתוּם לֹא נֶאֱנַס וְלֹא נִכְחַד מִמְּךָ׃


**and no secret is hidden from you** No mystery is robbed [from you] or denied you.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:8:1)


**רְבָה אִילָנָא וּתְקִיף.** גָּדַל הָאִילָן וְהוּחְזַק וְעָצַם:


**The tree grew and became strong** The tree grew and became stronger and stronger.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:8:2)


**יִמְטֵא לִשְׁמַיָּא.** הִגִּיעַ הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**reached the sky** [as translated,] reached the sky.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:1)


**עָפְיֵהּ שַׁפִּיר.** עַנְפּוֹ נָאֶה:


**its branches were beautiful** [as translated,] its branches were beautiful.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:2)


**וְאִנְבֵּהּ שַׂגִּיא.** פִּרְיוֹ רַב:


**and its fruit was plentiful** [as translated,] and its fruit was plentiful.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:3)


**מָזוֹן לְכֹלָּא בֵהּ.** וּכְדֵי מָזוֹן לְכָל בְּרִיָּה הָיָה בּוֹ:


**and on it was sustenance for all** On it was enough sustenance for all


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:4)


**תַּטְלֵל.** לְשׁוֹן צֵל:


**took shade** Aram. תַּטְלֵל, an expression of shade.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:5)


**יְדוּרוּן.** לְשׁוֹן מָדוֹר וְלִינָה:


**dwelt** Aram. יְדֻרָן, an expression of dwelling and lodging.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:9:6)


**יִתְזוֹן.** לְשׁוֹן מָזוֹן:


**was nourished** Aram. יִתְּזִין, an expression of food.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:10:1)


**חָזֵה הֲוֵית.** רוֹאֶה הָיִיתִי:


**I saw** lit. I was seeing.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:10:2)


**וְאָלוּ עִיר וְקַדִּישׁ.** וְהִנֵּה מַלְאָךְ שֶׁהוּא עֵר לְעוֹלָם וְקָדוֹשׁ:


**and behold a wakeful holy one** i.e., an angel who is forever awake and holy.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:1)


**קָרָא בְחַיִל.** צוֹעֵק בְּכֹחַ:


**Crying out loudly** crying with might.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:2)


**גּוֹדּוּ אִילָנָא.** קוּצּוּ הָאִילָן:


**Cut down the tree...shake off its branches** shake off and cause its branches to fall.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:3)


**אַתַּרוּ עָפְיֵהּ.** הִשִּׁירוּ וְהִפִּילוּ עֲנָפָיו:


**shake off its branches** shake off and cause its branches to fall.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:4)


**וּבַדַּרוּ אִנְבֵּהּ.** הֵפִיצוּ פִּרְיוֹ:


**and scatter its fruit** [as translated,] and scatter its fruit.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:11:5)


**תְּנוּד חֵיוְתָא מִן תַּחְתּוֹהִי.** יָנוּדוּ הַחַיּוֹת מִתַּחְתָּיו:


**the beasts shall wander away from beneath it** [as translated,] the beasts shall wander away from beneath it.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:1)


**בְּרַם עִקַּר שָׁרְשׁוֹהִי בְּאַרְעָא שְׁבוּקוּ.** אַךְ עִקַּר שָׁרְשָׁיו הַנִּיחוּ:


**But leave its main roots in the ground** but its main roots leave over.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:2)


**וּבֶאֱסוּר דִּי פַרְזֶל וּנְחָשׁ.** כְּלוֹמַר: תְּנוּ רַגְלָיו בְּכַבְלֵי בַרְזֶל, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁעוֹשִׂין לְסוּס שֶׁמַּנִּיחִין אוֹתוֹ בָּאָפָר כָּךְ כַּבְלוּהִי בְּכַבְלִים:


**and in fetters of iron and copper** i.e., place his feet in iron fetters as is done to a horse that is left in the meadow; so shall you chain him with fetters.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:3)


**בְּדִתְאָא.** בַּעֲשַׂב הַשָּׂדֶה, כְּלוֹמַר: בָּאָחוּ, שֶׁלֹּא יָזוּז מִשָּׁם:


**in the grass** Aram. בְדִתְאָא in the grass of the field, i.e., in the meadow, so that he should not move from there.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:4)


**יִצְטַבַּע.** יְהֵא שָׁרוּי תָּמִיד, לְשׁוֹן טְבִילָה:


**it shall be drenched** Aram. יִצְטַבַּע, it shall always be drenched, an expression of immersion.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:12:5)


**חֲלָקָה.** חֶלְקוֹ:


**its lot** [as translated,] its lot.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:13:1)


**וְשִׁבְעָה עִדָּנִין.** שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים:


**and seven periods** seven years.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:13:2)


**יַחְלְפוּן עֲלוֹהִי.** יַעַבְרוּ עָלָיו בִּגְזֵרָה זוֹ:


**will pass over him** will pass over him with this decree.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:14:1)


**שְׁאֵלְתָא.** גְּזֵרָה. וְעַל יְדֵי שֶׁנִּמְלָכִין בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא קוֹדֶם שֶׁיִּגְזְרוּ הַגְּזֵרָה, קוֹרֵא אוֹתָהּ שְׁאֵלְתָא:


**the edict** a decree, but since they take counsel from the Holy One, blessed be He, before they issue the decree, he calls it a question.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:14:2)


**עַד דִּבְרַת.** כְּמוֹ לְמַעַן:


**in order that** Aram. עַד דִּבְרַת, like לְמַעַן.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:14:3)


**דִּי יִנְדְּעוּן חַיַּיָּא.** שֶׁיֵּדְעוּ כָּל הַחַיִּים:


**that the living should know** in order that all the living should know.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:14:4)


**וּלְמַן דִּי יִצְבֵּא.** לְמִי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה:


**and to whom He wishes** [as translated,] and to whom He wishes.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:15:1)


**פִּשְׁרָא.** פִּתְרוֹנוֹ:


**its interpretation** [as translated,] its interpretation.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:15:2)


**וְאַנְתְּ כָּהֵל.** וְאַתָּה יָכוֹל:


**but you can** [as translated,] but you can.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:1)


**אֱדַיִן דָּנִיֵּאל.** אֶשְׁתּוֹמַם שָׁתַק:


**Then Daniel, whose name is Belteshazzar, was bewildered** He was silent.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:2)


**וְרַעְיוֹנוֹהִי.** וּמַחְשְׁבוֹתָיו:


**and his thoughts** [as translated,] and his thoughts.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:3)


**יְבַהֲלוּנֵיהּ.** שֶׁהָיָה יָרֵא לִפְתּוֹר לוֹ חֲלוֹם:


**alarmed him** for he was afraid to interpret the dream for him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:4)


**מָרִי.** אֲדוֹנִי, אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (שבועות לה:): **מָרִי חֶלְמָא לְשָׂנְאָךְ** קֹדֶשׁ הוּא. תָּלָה עֵינָיו לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְאָמַר: חֲלוֹם זֶה יִתְקַיֵּם עַל שׂוֹנַאֲךָ זֶה. וְאִם תֹּאמַר לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר אָמַר, וַהֲלֹא יִשְׂרָאֵל שׂוֹנְאוֹ, אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיְּקַלְלֵם?


**My lord** Aram. מָרִאי. Our Rabbis said (Shevu. 35b): “My Lord, may the dream be for Your enemies,” refers to God. He raised his eyes to the Holy One, blessed be He, and said, “May this dream be fulfilled upon this enemy of Yours.” But if you say that he said it to Nebuchadnezzar, was not Israel his enemy? Is it possible that he would curse them?


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:16:5)


**לְעָרָךְ.** לְשׂוֹנְאָךְ, כְּמוֹ ״וַיְהִי עָרֵךְ״ (שמואל א כה):


**for your foes** Aram. לְעָרָךְ, like (Sam. 28:16): “and has become [the supporter of] your foes (עָרֶךָ).”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:21:1)


**דִּי מְטָת עַל מָרִי מַלְכָּא.** אֲשֶׁר הִגִּיעַ עַל אֲדוֹנִי הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**which befalls my lord, the king** [as translated,] which befalls my lord, the king.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:22:1)


**וְלָךְ טָרְדִין מִן אֲנָשָׁא.** וְאוֹתְךָ יִטְרְדוּ מִבְּנֵי אָדָם:


**And they will banish you from mankind** [as translated,] and they will banish you from mankind.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:22:2)


**לָךְ מְצַבְּעִין.** אוֹתְךָ יִטְבְּלוּ:


**and with the dew of the heavens you shall be drenched** They will immerse you.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:22:3)


**וְשִׁבְעָה עִדָּנִין יַחְלְפוּן עֲלָךְ.** יֵשׁ לוֹמַר: גְּמוּל בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ שֶׁהֶחֱרִיב, שֶׁנִּבְנְתָה בְּשֶׁבַע שָׁנִים (מלכים א ו:לח):


**and seven periods shall pass over you** We may say that this is the recompense for the Temple, which he destroyed, which was built in seven years.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:23:1)


**וְדִי אֲמַרוּ וְגוֹ׳.** אֲשֶׁר אָמְרוּ לְהַנִּיחַ שָׁרְשׁוֹהִי שֶׁל אִילָן, וְהוּא הַפִּתְרוֹן: **מַלְכוּתָא לָךְ קַיֶּמֶת.** הַמַּלְכוּת עוֹמֶדֶת לְךָ, סוֹפְךָ שֶׁתָּשׁוּב לְךָ מַלְכוּתְךָ:


**And what they said, etc.** What they said—to leave the roots of the tree—this is the interpretation: מַלְכוּתָךְ, your kingdom—will remain yours. Your kingdom will ultimately be restored to you.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:23:2)


**מִן דִּי תִנְדַּע דִּי שַׁלִּיטִין שְׁמַיָּא.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁתֵּדַע שֶׁהָעֶלְיוֹנִים מוֹשְׁלִים בָּעוֹלָם:


**as soon as you know that Heaven rules** as soon as you know that the heavenly beings rule over the world.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:1)


**לָהֵן מַלְכָּא.** בֶּאֱמֶת אַתָּה מֶלֶךְ, שְׁמַע לַעֲצָתִי:


**Indeed, O king** Indeed, O king, hearken to my counsel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:2)


**מַלְכִּי.** עֲצָתִי, כְּמוֹ ״אִיעָצְךָ״ (במדבר כד:יד) ״אֲמַלְכִינָךְ״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**my counsel** Aram. מִלְכִּי, my counsel, like (Num. 24:14): “I shall advise you (אִיעָצְךָ),” which is translated: אֲמַלְּכִינָךְ.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:3)


**יִשְׁפַּר עֲלָךְ.** עֲצָתִי תִּיטַב בְּעֵינֶיךָ לִשְׁמֹעַ מִלַּי:


**please you** May my counsel please you that you hearken to my words.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:4)


**וַחֲטָאָךְ בְּצִדְקָה פְרֻק.** וַחֲטָאָךְ בִּצְדָקָה פְּרֹק מֵעַל צַוָּארֶךָ:


**and with charity you will remove your sin** With charity, break your sin off your neck.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:5)


**וַעֲוָיָתָךְ בְּמִחַן עֲנָיִן.** וַעֲוֹנוֹתֶיךָ פְּרוֹק בַּחֲנִינַת עֲנִיִּים:


**and your iniquity by showing mercy to poor** and remove your iniquities by showing mercy to the poor.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:24:6)


**הֵן תֶּהֱוֵא אַרְכָא לִשְׁלֵוְתָךְ.** אוּלַי תְּהֵא שָׁהוּת לִשְׁלוֹמְךָ, שֶׁלֹּא תְּמַהֵר הָרָעָה לָבוֹא. וּמָה רָאָה דָנִיֵּאל לְהַשִּׂיא עֵצָה טוֹבָה לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר? רָאָה יִשְׂרָאֵל עֲנִיִּים שִׁפְלֵי גוֹלָה מַחֲזִירִין עַל הַפְּתָחִים, וְהִשִּׂיאוֹ עֵצָה לְחָנְנָם. אָמַר לוֹ: עֲנִיִּים אֵלּוּ שֶׁהִגְלֵיתָם רְעֵבִים הֵם, פַּרְנְסֵם! וְכֵן עָשָׂה, פָּתַח אוֹצְרוֹתָיו וְהָיָה מְפַרְנְסָם כָּל שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ:


**perhaps your tranquility will last** Perhaps there will be time for your tranquility, that the evil should not come swiftly. Now what did Daniel see to give Nebuchadnezzar good advice? He saw the Jews poor and humbled by the exile, going from door to door begging for alms, and he gave him advice to deal mercifully with them. He said to him, “These poor people whom you exiled are hungry; nourish them.” And so he did. He opened his storehouses and fed them for a full twelve months.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:25:1)


**כֹּלָּא מְטָא.** כָּל הַפִּתְרוֹן הִגִּיעַ עַל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**All this befell** The entire interpretation befell Nebuchadnezzar.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:26:1)


**מְהַלֵּךְ הֲוָה.** הוֹלֵךְ הָיָה:


**he was walking** [as translated,] he was walking.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:27:1)


**הֲלָא דָא הִיא.** שָׁמַע עֲנִיִּים בָּאִים עַל הַפֶּתַח וְצוֹעֲקִים, אָמַר: מָה קוֹל הֶהָמוֹן הַזֶּה בְּאָזְנָי? אָמְרוּ לוֹ עֲבָדָיו: אֵלּוּ הָעֲנִיִּים שֶׁקָּבַעְתָּ לָהֶם עֵת פַּרְנָסָתָם. אָמַר: **הֲלָא דָא הִיא בָּבֶל רַבְּתָא וְגוֹמֵר**, וְאִלְמָלֵא בִּזְבַּזְתִּי אוֹצְרוֹתַי לְפַרְנָסַת עֲנִיִּים, מֵהֵיכָן הָיִיתִי בּוֹנֶה כָּל הַפַּלְטְרִין הַלָּלוּ? מֵעַתָּה אֵינִי מְפַרְנְסָם. כָּךְ דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא:


**Is this not** He heard poor people coming to the door and crying out. He said, “What is the sound of this multitude in my ears?” His servants said to him, “These are the poor for whom you arranged a time to feed.” He said, “Is this not the great Babylon, etc. If I had squandered my storehouses, from where would I have built all these palaces? From now on, I shall not feed them.” Rabbi Tanhuma (Mishpatim 4) explained it in this manner.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:27:2)


**וְלִיקָר הַדְרִי.** וּלְכָבוֹד תִּפְאַרְתִּי:


**and for the honor of my glory** [as translated,] and for the honor of my glory.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:28:1)


**עוֹד מִלְּתָא בְּפֻם מַלְכָּא, עֲדָת מִנָּךְ.** סָרָה מִנָּךְ:


**has turned away from you** [as translated,] has turned away from you.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:29:1)


**טָרְדִין.** לְשׁוֹן גֵּרוּשִׁין:


**they will banish** Aram. טָרְדִין. an expression of driving away.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:30:1)


**סָפַת.** הַגְּזֵרָה כָּלְתָה עָלָיו:


**was fulfilled** The decree was executed upon him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:30:2)


**גִּשְׁמָהּ.** גּוּפֵיהּ:


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:30:3)


**שַׂעְרֵהּ כְּנִשְׁרִין רְבָה.** שַׂעֲרוֹתָיו כְּכַנְפֵי נְשָׁרִים גָּדְלוּ עַל כָּל גּוּפוֹ:


**his hair grew like [the feathers of] eagles** His hair grew over his entire body like eagles’ feathers.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:30:4)


**וְטִפְרוֹהִי כְצִפְּרִין.** וְצִפָּרְנָיו כְּצִפּוֹר:


**and his nails like birds** and his nails were like a bird’s [talons].



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:31:1)


**וְלִקְצָת יוֹמַיָּא.** לְקֵץ הַיָּמִים שֶׁקָּבְעוּ לוֹ, לְסוֹף שֶׁבַע הַשָּׁנִים:


**And at the end of the days** at the end of the days that were set for him; at the end of the seven years.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:31:2)


**נָטְלַת.** נָשָׂאתִי:


**raised** [as translated,] raised.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:31:3)


**וּמַנְדְּעִי עֲלַי יְתִיב.** וְדַעְתִּי עָלַי חָזְרָה:


**and my understanding was restored to me** [as translated,] and my understanding was restored to me.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:31:4)


**וּלְעִלָּאָה בָּרְכֵת.** וְאֶת הָעֶלְיוֹן בֵּרַכְתִּי:


**and I blessed the Most High** [as translated,] and I blessed the Most High.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:32:1)


**כֻּלָּה חֲשִׁיבִין.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ: הַאי חִירְגָּא דְּיוֹמָא לָא שְׁמֵיהּ אָבָק הַנִּרְאֶה בַּשֶּׁמֶשׁ (יומא כ:):


**are reckoned as nothing** Aram. כְּלָה. Our Rabbis said (Yoma 20b): The dust of the sun is called לָא i.e., the dust that appears in the sun.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:32:2)


**וּכְמִצְבְּיֵהּ עָבֵד בְּחֵיל שְׁמַיָּא.** וְכִרְצוֹנוֹ עוֹשֶׂה בִּצְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**and according to His will, He does with the host of heaven** [as translated,] and according to His will, He does with the host of heaven.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:32:3)


**וְלָא אִיתַי דִּי יְמַחֵא בִידֵהּ.** וְלָא יֵשׁ שֶׁיִּמְחֶה בְּיָדוֹ:


**and no one can stop His hand** Aram. וְלָא אִיתַי דִּי יְמַחֵא בִידֵהּ. And there is none who will stop His hand.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:1)


**בֵּהּ זִמְנָא.** בְּאוֹתוֹ זְמָן:


**At that time** [as translated,] at that time.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:2)

**and I returned to the glory of my kingdom** [as translated,] and I returned to the glory of my kingdom.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:3)


**וְזִיוִי.** וְתֹאַר פָּנַי:


**and my splendor** the features of my face.
