[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:4)


**וְלִי הַדָּבְרַי וְרַבְרְבָנַי יִבְעוֹן.** וְאוֹתִי יוֹעֲצַי וְשָׂרַי הָיוּ מְבַקְּשִׁים וַחֲפֵצִים שֶׁאָשׁוּב:


**my leaders and my dignitaries were seeking me** and My advisers and my princes were seeking me and hoping for my return.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:5)


**הִתְקַנְתָּ.** הוּכַנְתִּי:


**and I was established** [as translated,] and I was established.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:33:6)


**וּרְבוּ יְתֵרָה הוֹסַפְתָּ לִי.** וּגְדֻלָּה יְתֵרָה מִן הָרִאשׁוֹנָה נִתּוֹסְפַת לִי, אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (שבת קנ.) שֶׁרָכַב עַל אֲרִי וְקָשַׁר תַּנִּין בְּרֹאשׁוֹ:


**and excessive greatness was added to me** and more greatness than the earlier [kings] was added to me. Our Rabbis said (Shab. 150a) that he rode astride a lion and tied a serpent to its head.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Daniel 4:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:34:1)


**וְאָרְחָתֵהּ דִּין.** וּדְרָכָיו מִשְׁפָּט:


**and Whose ways are just** His ways are just.


###### Rashi on Daniel 4:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/4:34:2)


**וְדִי מְהַלְּכִין בְּגֵוָה.** וַאֲשֶׁר מְהַלְּכִין בְּגַאֲוָה יָכוֹל לְהַשְׁפִּיל:


**who walk with arrogance** Those who walk with arrogance He can humble.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:1:1)


**בֵּלְשַׁאצַּר מַלְכָּא.** בְּנוֹ הָיָה, וּמָלַךְ לְאַחַר אֱוִיל מְרֹדַךְ שֶׁמָּלַךְ תַּחַת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, וְגַם (הוּא) הָיָה בְּנוֹ שֶׁל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**King Belshazzar** He was his son, and he reigned after Evil-merodach, who reigned instead of Nebuchadnezzar, and (he) too was the son of Nebuchadnezzar.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:1:2)


**עֲבַד לְחֶם רַב.** עָשָׂה סְעוּדָּה גְּדוֹלָה. מָצִינוּ בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסִיפוֹן שֶׁנִּלְחַם בַּיּוֹם עִם דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי וְכוֹרֶשׁ וְנִצַּח הַמִּלְחָמָה, וְלָעֶרֶב עָשָׂה מִשְׁתֶּה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּבָּא עָלָיו יְשַׁעְיָה ״עָרוֹךְ הַשֻּׁלְחָן צָפֹה הַצָּפִית אָכוֹל שָׁתֹה קוּמוּ הַשָּׂרִים וְגוֹ׳״ (ישעיה כא:ה) שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַמִּשְׁתֶּה חָזְרוּ אוֹיְבָיו וְנִלְחֲמוּ עַל הָעִיר וּלְכָדוּהָ:


**made a great feast** We find in Josephon (Book 1, ch. 3) that he waged war by day with Darius the Mede and Cyrus and was victorious in the battle. So in the evening he made a feast, as Isaiah had prophesied about him: (21: 5) “Setting the table, setting up the lamp, eating, drinking. ‘Arise, princes, etc.’” For in the midst of the feast the enemies returned, waged war on the city and captured it.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:1:3)


**וּלְקָבֵל אַלְפָּא חַמְרָא.** כְּנֶגֶד אֶלֶף אִישׁ הָיָה שׁוֹתֶה יַיִן:


**as much wine as the thousand** The equivalent of one thousand men [was the amount] he was drinking [in] wine.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:2:1)


**אָמַר בְּטַעַם חַמְרָא.** אָמַר בַּעֲצַת הַיַּיִן שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּכֵּר:


**said, with the counsel of the wine** He said with the counsel of the wine that he had imbibed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:2:2)


**לְהַיְתָיָה.** לְהָבִיא:


**to bring** [as translated,] to bring.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:2:3)


**דִּי הַנְפֵּק.** שֶׁהוֹצִיא׃


**that...had taken out** [as translated,] had taken out.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:2:4)


**שֵׁגְלָתָהּ.** לְשׁוֹן מַלְכָּא בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲרָמִי, כְּמוֹ ״וְהַשֵּׁגַל יוֹשֶׁבֶת אֶצְלוֹ״ (נחמיה ב:ו):


**his queen** Aram. שֵּׁגְלָתֵה, an expression of a queen in Aramaic, like: (Neh. 2:6): “and the queen (וְהַשֵׁגָל) was sitting beside him.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:3:1)


**הַיְתִיו.** הוּבָאוּ׃


**were brought** [as translated,] were brought.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:3:2)


**דִּי הַנְפִּקוּ.** שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּ:


**that they had taken out** [as translated,] that they had taken out.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:5:1)


**נָפְקוּ: אֶצְבְּעָן.** יָצְאוּ אֶצְבְּעוֹת יַד אָדָם מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**the fingers...emerged** The fingers of a human hand emerged from heaven.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:5:2)


**לָקֳבֵל נֶבְרַשְׁתָּא.** לְמוּל הַמְּנוֹרָה אֲשֶׁר לִפְנֵי הַשֻּׁלְחָן, וְדֻגְמָתוֹ בְּמַסֶּכֶת יוֹמָא: עָשְׂתָה נִבְרֶשֶׁת שֶׁל זָהָב (יומא לז.):


**opposite the candelabrum** opposite the menorah that was before the table, and a similar word appears (in Tractate Yoma 37a): “made a candelabrum (נִבְרֶשֶׁת) of gold.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:5:3)


**עַל גִּירָא.** עַל סִיד טִיחַ כּוֹתֶל הַבַּיִת, כְּמוֹ (ישעיהו כז:ט) ״כְּאַבְנֵי גִר מְנֻפָּצוֹת״, וְהוּא דּוֹמֶה לְסִיד:


**on the plaster** on the lime that is plastered on the wall, like (Isa. 27:9): “like crushed chalkstones (אַבְנֵי גֵר),” which resemble lime.

**his color changed** His features changed upon him.

**and his thoughts** [as translated,] and his thoughts.

**and the belts of his loins became untied** The belts of his loins opened because his loins were emptying out of fear, and his belt opened.

**and the belts of his loins became untied** were untied.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:6:5)


**וְאַרְכֻבָּתֵהּ.** וּבִרְכָּיו:


**and his knees** [as translated,] and his knees.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:6:6)

**shall wear purple** following the custom of the nobles.

**and when his heart became haughty** [as translated,] and when his heart became haughty.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:20:2)


**לְהָזָדָה.** לְהַרְשִׁיעַ:


**so that he dealt wickedly** [as translated,] so that he dealt wickedly.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:20:3)


**הִנַּחְתָּ.** הוֹרִיד:


**he was deposed** Aram. הָנְחַת, lit. he took him down.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:20:4)


**הֶעְדִּיוּ מִינָּהּ.** הֵסִירוּ מִמֶּנּוּ:


**was removed from him.** Aram. הֶעְדִּיו, He removed from him.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:21:1)


**טָרִיד.** נִגְרָשׁ:


**was banished** Aram. טְרִיד was driven away.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:22:1)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דִּי כָל דְּנָה יְדַעְתָּ.** כָּל עוּמַת אֲשֶׁר כָּל זֶה יָדַעְתָּ שֶׁאֵרַע לְאָבִיךָ, וְהָיָה לְךָ לִהְיוֹת יָרֵא מִלִּפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**in view of the fact that you know all this** in view of the fact that you know all this that befell your father, and you should have feared the Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:24:1)


**שְׁלִיחַ פַּסָּא דִּי יְדָא.** נִשְׁלַח כַּף יָד:


**the palm of a hand was sent forth** [as translated,] the palm of a hand was sent forth.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:24:2)


**וּכְתָבָא דְנָה רְשִׁים.** וְזֶה הַמִּכְתָּב אֲשֶׁר חָקַק:


**and it inscribed this writing** and this is the writing that it inscribed.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:26:1)


**מְנֵא מְנָא.** מְנָה אֱלָהָא, כְּלוֹמַר **מְנָא** זֶהוּ פֵּרוּשׁוֹ **מְנָה אֱלָהָא מַלְכוּתָךְ**:


**MENE God has counted** i.e., MENE means that God has counted your kingdom.


###### Rashi on Daniel 5:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:26:2)


**וְהַשְׁלְמָה.** חָשַׁב קֵץ שֶׁנִּפְסַק מַלְכוּת שֶׁל אָבִיךָ וְזַרְעוֹ וּמָצָא שֶׁכְּבָר הֻשְׁלְמָה:


**and has brought it to an end** He calculated the end of the allotted time for the kingdom of your father and his seed, and found that it has already been completed.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:27:1)


**תְּקֵל תְּקִילְתָּא בְמֹאזַנְיָא.** תְּקֵל זֶהוּ פֵּרוּשׁוֹ: נִשְׁקַלְתָּ לְפָנָיו בְּמֹאזְנַיִם וְנִמְצֵאתָ חָסֵר מִכָּל צְדָקָה:


**TEKEIL You were weighed on the scales** TEKEIL means that you were weighed on the scales and found wanting of all righteousness.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Daniel 5:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/5:28:1)


**פְּרֵס פְּרִיסַת מַלְכוּתָךְ וְגוֹ׳.** כְּלוֹמַר: ״וּפַרְסִין״ מַשְׁמַע שְׁנֵי פְרָסִין, הָאֶחָד: פְּרִיסַת מַלְכוּתָךְ, נִשְׁתַּבְּרָה מַלְכוּתְךָ, וְהַשֵּׁנִי, שֶׁנִּתְּנָה לְמָדַי וּפָרָס:


**Your kingdom has been broken up, etc.** i.e., UFARSIN means two definitions of PEREIS; one is that your kingdom פְּרִיסַת, your kingdom has been broken up, and the second one is that it has been given to Media and Persia (וּפָּרַס).



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:1:1)


**וְדָרְיָוֶשׁ מָדָאָה קַבֵּל מַלְכוּתָא כְּבַר שְׁנִין שִׁתִּין וְתַרְתֵּין.** לָמָּה מָנָה שְׁנוֹתָיו? לוֹמַר לְךָ: בְּיוֹם שֶׁנִּכְנַס נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר לַהֵיכָל בִּימֵי יְהוֹיָקִים (צריך לומר: יְהוֹיָכִין), נוֹלַד שִׂטְנוֹ דָּרְיָוֶשׁ (סדר עולם כח). מִגָּלוּת יְכָנְיָה וְעַד כָּאן שִׁשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנָה, וְאָמַר מַר: גָּלוּ בִּימֵי יְהוֹיָכִין בְּשֶׁבַע לְכִבּוּשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים, שֶׁהוּא שְׁמוֹנֶה לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, נִשְׁתַּיְּרוּ לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֶׁבַע, שֶׁהֲרֵי אַרְבָּעִים וְחָמֵשׁ מָלַךְ, וְשָׁלֹשׁ וְעֶשְׂרִים דְּאֱוִיל מְרֹדַךְ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה בְּמַסֶּכֶת מְגִלָּה (מגילה יא:), שְׁתַּיִם שֶׁעָבְדוּ לְבֵלְשַׁצַּר, הֲרֵי שִׁשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם:


**And Darius the Mede received the kingdom at the age of sixty-two** Why does he count his years? To tell you that on the day that Nebuchadnezzar entered the Heichal in the days of Jehoiachin, his adversary, Darius, was born (Seder Olam ch. 28). From the exile of Jeconiah until now were sixty-two years, and the master said: They were exiled in the days of Jehoiachin in the seventh year counting from the conquest of Jehoiakim, which is eight years after the beginning of Nebuchadnezzar’s reign. There remained to Nebuchadnezzar’s reign thirty-seven years, for he reigned forty-five years, and twenty-three of Evil-merodach’s, as our Sages stated in Tractate Megillah (11b), and the two years of Belshazzar that passed, totaling sixty-two.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:3:1)


**וְעֵלָּא מִנְּהוֹן.** וּלְמַעְלָה מֵהֶן:


**And over them** [as translated,] and over them.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:3:2)


**דִּי לֶהֱוֺן אֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנַיָּא.** שֶׁיִּתְּנוּ **הָאֲחַשְׁדַּרְפְּנַיָּא** הַלָּלוּ עֵצָה לִשְׁלֹשָׁה הָאֲפַרְכִּים:


**that these satraps should give them counsel** that these satraps should give counsel to the three viziers.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:3:3)


**וּמַלְכָּא לָא לֶהֱוֵא נָזִיק.** וְהַמֶּלֶךְ לֹא יְהֵא נִפְסָד בְּשׁוּם דָּבָר מַלְכוּת הַמּוּטָּל עֲלֵיהֶם לַעֲשׂוֹת:


**and the king should not suffer any injury** And the king should not suffer any loss in any royal matter that is incumbent upon them to perform.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:4:1)


**דָּנִיֵּאל דְּנָה.** דָּנִיֵּאל זֶה:


**this Daniel** [as translated,] this Daniel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:4:2)


**מִתְנַצֵּחַ.** מִתְגַּבֵּר:


**surpassed** [as translated,] surpassed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:4:3)


**וּמַלְכָּא עֲשִׁית.** הַמֶּלֶךְ הָיָה חוֹשֵׁב בְּלִבּוֹ לַהֲקִימוֹ עַל כֻּלָּם:


**and the king contemplated** The king was thinking in his heart to set him up over all of them.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:5:1)


**הֲווֹ בָעַיִן עִלָּה.** הָיוּ מְבַקְּשִׁים עֲלִילָה לִמְצוֹא לְדָנִיֵּאל בִּדְבַר הַמַּלְכוּת, שֶׁיּוּכְלוּ לְהַלְשִׁין עָלָיו:


**sought to find a pretext** They were seeking a pretext to find about Daniel in matters of the kingdom so that they could inform on him.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:6:1)


**דִּי לָא נְהַשְׁכַּח.** אָמְרוּ בֵּינֵיהֶם: לֹא נִמְצָא לְדָנִיֵּאל זֶה שׁוּם עֲלִילָה:


**No pretext can be found** They said among themselves, “No pretext can be found about this Daniel.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:6:2)


**לָהֵן הַשְׁכַּחְנָא עֲלוֹהִי.** אֲבָל נִמְצָא עָלָיו עֲלִילָה עַל יְדֵי תּוֹרַת אֱלֹהָיו, אִם נוּכַל לְבַקֵּשׁ מֵאֵת פְּנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ שׁוּם גְּזֵרָה שֶׁיִּגְזֹר לְהַעֲבִירוֹ עַל תּוֹרָתוֹ, וְהוּא לֹא יְקַיֵּם אֶת מִצְוַת הַמֶּלֶךְ, וַהֲרֵי הָעֲלִילָה:


**but we shall find [a pretext] against him** but we shall find a pretext against him through the law of his God if we ask the king to issue some decree to cause him to transgress His law, and he will not fulfill the king’s command; and this is the pretext.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:7:1)


**הִרְגִּישׁוּ.** מְשַׁמְּשׁוּ עַל הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**assembled** Aram. הַרְגִּישׁוּ, they approached the king.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:1)


**אִתְיָעַטוּ.** נוֹעֲצוּ:


**have taken counsel** [as translated,] have taken counsel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:2)


**לְקַיָּמָה קְיָם מַלְכָּא.** לְהַחֲזִיק בְּדַת הַמֶּלֶךְ בְּכֹחַ, לְפִי שֶׁמֵחָדָשׁ בָּאתָ לִמְלוֹךְ וּמִמְּדִינָה אַחֶרֶת אַתָּה, וְצָרִיךְ חִזּוּק:


**to preserve the king’s law** to preserve the king’s law with power because you have recently ascended the throne. [Moreover], you are from another country and are in need of support.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:3)


**דִּי יִבְעֶה בָעוּ.** אֲשֶׁר יְבַקֵּשׁ שׁוּם בַּקָּשָׁה:


**make any request** [as translated,] make any request.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:4)


**לָהֵן מִנָּךְ מַלְכָּא.** זוּלָתִי מִמְּךָ הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**except from you, O King** [as translated,] except from you, O King.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:8:5)


**לְגוֹב אַרְיָוָתָא.** לְבוֹר שֶׁאֲרָיוֹת הַמֶּלֶךְ שָׁם:


**into a pit of lions** into the pit where the king’s lions were [kept].



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:9:1)


**תָּקִים אֱסָרָא.** תַּעֲמִיד חֹק זֶה:


**you shall issue a decree** lit. you shall put up this decree.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:9:2)


**דִּי לָא לְהַשְׁנָיָה.** שֶׁלֹּא לְשַׁנּוֹת כְּדָת מָדַי וּפָרָס:


**that will not be amended** that will not be amended, like the law of Media and Persia.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:9:3)


**דִּי לָא תֶעְדֵּא.** שֶׁלֹּא תַעֲבוֹר:


**which will not be repealed** which will not pass.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:1)


**כְּדִי יְדַע.** וְכַאֲשֶׁר יָדַע:


**when he knew** and when he found out.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:2)


**וְכַוִּין פְּתִיחָן לֵהּ בְּעִלִּיתֵהּ.** בַּעֲלִיָּתוֹ:


**where there were open windows in his upper chamber** Aram. בְּעִלִּיתֵהּ, in his upper story.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:3)


**נֶגֶד יְרוּשָׁלַיִם.** לְצַד בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחָרַב, עָשָׂה כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר שְׁלֹמֹה ״וְהִתְפַּלְלוּ אֵלֶיךָ דֶּרֶךְ אַרְצָם״ (מלכים א ח:מח):


**opposite Jerusalem** toward the side of the Temple, and although it was in ruins, he did as Solomon said (I Kings 8:48): “and pray to You toward their land.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:4)


**וְזִמְנִין תְּלָתָה.** עַרְבִית וְשַׁחֲרִית וּמִנְחָה:


**and three times** the evening prayer, the morning prayer and the afternoon prayer.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:5)


**בָּרֵךְ עַל בִּרְכוֹהִי.** כּוֹרֵעַ עַל בִּרְכָּיו:


**kneeled on his knees** [as translated,] kneeled on his knees.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:11:6)


**כָּל קֳבֵל דִּי הוּא עָבֵד וְגוֹ׳.** כְּכָל אֲשֶׁר הָיָה עוֹשֶׂה לִפְנֵי זֹאת:


**as he had done prior to this** just as all that he had done before this.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:12:1)


**הִרְגִּישׁוּ.** מִשְׁמְשׁוּ וְתָפְשׁוּ:


**searched** Aram. הַרְגִישּׁוּ, tapped and searched.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:13:1)


**עַל אֱסָר מַלְכָּא.** עַל בִּטּוּל אִסּוּר הַמֶּלֶךְ אֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ לְהַלְשִׁין:


**concerning the king’s decree** concerning the infraction of the king’s decree, about which they had come to inform.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:13:2)


**יַצִּיבָא מִלְּתָא.** אֱמֶת הַדָּבָר:


**the thing is true** [as translated,] the thing is true.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:14:1)


**לֹא שָׂם עֲלָךְ מַלְכָּא טְעֵם.** לֹא נָתַן לֵב וְעֵצָה לָחוּשׁ עַל גְּזֵרוֹתֶיךָ:


**did not pay heed to you, O king** He did not pay heed or counsel to care about your decrees.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:14:2)


**בָּעֵא בָּעוּתֵהּ.** מִתְפַּלֵּל תְּפִלָּתוֹ:


**he offers up his prayer** Aram. בָּעֵא בָּעוּתֵה, he prays his prayer.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:1)


**כְּדִי מִלְּתָא שְׁמַע.** כַּאֲשֶׁר שָׁמַע הַדָּבָר:


**when...heard the thing** [as translated,] when he heard the thing.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:2)


**שַׂגִּיא בְאֵשׁ עֲלוֹהִי.** הַרְבֵּה הֵרַע בְּעֵינָיו, שֶׁהָיָה אוֹהֵב אֶת דָּנִיֵּאל וְקָשֶׁה עָלָיו מִיתָתוֹ:


**he became very displeased** It was very bad in his eyes because he loved Daniel, and his death would be painful to him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:3)


**וְעַל דָּנִיֵּאל שָׂם בָּל לְשֵׁיזָבוּתֵהּ.** עַל דָּנִיֵּאל שָׂם דְּחוּי לִדְחוֹת הַדָּבָר לְהַצִּילוֹ וְאָמַר לָהֶם: אֵינִי מַאֲמִין לָכֶם:


**and he gave Daniel a reprieve to save him** He gave Daniel a reprieve to push off the matter to save him, and he said to them, “I don’t believe you.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:4)


**בָּל.** אֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ דֻּגְמָתוֹ, אַךְ עִנְיָנוֹ יוֹרֶה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ:


**a reprieve** Aram. בָּל. I do not know of any word like it, but its context shows its meaning.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:15:5)


**וְעַד מְעָלֵי שִׁמְשָׁא.** וְעַד בֹּא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ הָיָה מִשְׁתַּדֵּל לְהַצִּילוֹ, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהִגִּיעַ זְמַן תְּפִלָּתוֹ כָּרַע לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עַל בִּרְכָּיו וְהִתְפַּלֵּל תְּפִלָּתוֹ, וְשׁוּב לֹא הָיָה לוֹ פִּתְחוֹן פֶּה לְהַצִּילוֹ:


**and until sunset** and until sunset he tried to save him, but when the time of his prayer arrived, he prayed his prayer, and the king had no longer had a pretext to save him.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:16:1)


**דִּי מַלְכָּא יְהָקֵם.** אֲשֶׁר הַמֶּלֶךְ יִגְזֹר וְיַעֲמֹד, לֹא לְאָדָם לְשַׁנּוֹתוֹ:


**that the king establishes** What the king decrees and ratifies, no man can alter.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:18:1)


**וְהֵיתָיִת אֶבֶן חֲדָה.** וְהוּבְאַת אֶבֶן אַחַת. בְּכָל אֶרֶץ בָּבֶל אֵין אֶבֶן כִּי אִם לְבֵנִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב ״הָבָה נִלְבְּנָה לְבֵנִים״ (בראשית יא:ג). לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁאֵין אֶבֶן בְּבָבֶל, וּלְפִי שָׁעָה הֱבִיאוּהָ מַלְאָכִים מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. אִם הִתְנָה בְּמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית עַל הַיָּם שֶׁיִּקָּרַע לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְעַל הָאֵשׁ שֶׁיִּצְטַנֵּן לַחֲנַנְיָה וַחֲבֵרָיו, וְעַל הָאֲרָיוֹת מֵהַזִּיק לְדָנִיֵּאל, לֹא הִתְנָה עַל זְרִיקַת אֶבֶן וְזַיִן שֶׁלֹּא יַזִּיק בָּאָדָם. וְהַמֶּלֶךְ אָמַר: מֵעִם הָאֲרָיוֹת אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְסַלְּקוֹ, שֶׁכְּבָר נִגְזְרָה גְּזֵרָה, אֶזְדַּהֵר בּוֹ מֵהֶזֵּקַת אָדָם, וְהַנֵּס אִם יָבֹא יָבֹא (ספרים אחרים אומרים):


**And a stone was brought** lit. and one stone was brought. Throughout the entire land of Babylon there are no stones but bricks, as it is written: (Gen 11:3): “Let us make bricks.” We learn that there are no stones in Babylon, but for the occasion, angels brought it [a stone] from the land of Israel. If God stipulated with the Creation on the sea that it should split for the Children of Israel, and on the fire that it cool off for Hananiah and his colleagues, and on the lions that they should not hurt Daniel, He did not stipulate about casting stones or weapons that they should not hurt any man. So the king said, “From the lions I cannot take him away. I shall be careful with him that no man hurt him, and if the miracle comes, let it come.” This does not appear in some editions.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:18:2)


**וְשָׂמַת עַל פֻּם גּוּבָּא.** הוּשְׂמָה עַל פִּי הַגּוּמָּא׃


**and placed on the mouth of the pit** [as translated,] and placed on the mouth of the pit.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:18:3)


**בְּעִזְקְתֵיהּ.** בְּטַבַּעְתּוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא יְזִיזוּהָ מִמְּקוֹמוֹ וְיַשְׁלִיכוּ עָלָיו אֲבָנִים לְהָרְגוֹ:


**with his signet ring** with his signet ring so that no one could move it from its place and cast stones upon him to kill him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:18:4)


**דִּי לָא תִשְׁנֵא צְבוּ בְדָנִיֵּאל.** שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁתַּנֶּה רְצוֹנוֹ, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשׂוּ לוֹ הֶיזֵּק שֶׁלֹּא כִּרְצוֹנוֹ (ספרים אחרים כִּרְצוֹנָם):


**that his will about Daniel not be altered** so that his will should not be changed; i.e., so that they should not harm him against his [the king’s] will.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:19:1)


**וּבַת טְוַת.** לָן בְּתַעֲנִית:


**and retired while fasting** [as translated,] and retired while fasting.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:19:2)


**וְדַחֲוָן.** וְשֻׁלְחָן בַּמִּשְׁנָה (כלים בבא מציעא ה:א); כָּךְ פֵּתַר דּוֹנַשׁ; לֹא הִכְנִיסוּ לְפָנָיו שֻׁלְחָן לֶאֱכֹל:


**table** Aram. וְּדַּחֲוָן, and a table, in the Mishnah (Tosefta Kelim, B.M. 5:1). So did Dunash explain (p. 58). They did not bring before him a table upon which to eat.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:19:3)


**וְשִׁנְתֵּהּ נַדַּת עֲלוֹהִי.** נָדְדָה שְׁנָתוֹ:


**and his sleep wandered from him** [as translated,] his sleep wandered.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:20:1)


**בְּשַׁפְרְפָּרָא.** בַּעֲלוֹת שַׁחַר, וְהַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ בְּתַרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן:


**at dawn** Aram. בִּשְׁפַרְפָרָה, at the rise of dawn, and there are many [examples of this] in Targum Jonathan.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:20:2)


**יָקוּם בְּנָגְהָא.** עָמַד לְאוֹר הַבֹּקֶר, וְלֹא בְּשָׁלֹשׁ שָׁעוֹת כִּשְׁאָר מְלָכִים:


**arose to the light of morning** He arose at the light of morning, not after three hours, like other kings.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:20:3)


**וּבְהִתְבְּהָלָה לְגֻבָּא דִי אַרְיָוָתָא אֲזַל.** וּבִבְהָלָה לַחֲפִירַת הָאֲרָיוֹת אֲזַל:


**and in haste went to the pit of the lions** [as translated,] and in haste went to the pit of the lions.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:23:1)


**חֲבוּלָה לָא עַבְדֵת.** שׁוּם דְּבַר כִּעוּר וְשֶׁקֶר לֹא עָשִׂיתִי:


**I have done no harm** No unseemly or false act have I committed.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:24:1)


**שַׂגִּיא טָאב עֲלוֹהִי.** הַרְבֵּה הוּטַב בְּעֵינָיו:


**it pleased...exceedingly** It was very good in his eyes.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:24:2)


**וּלְדָנִיֵּאל.** וְאֶת דָּנִיֵּאל צִוָּה לְהַעֲלוֹת מִן הַבּוֹר:


**and...Daniel** and he ordered that Daniel be lifted up out of the pit.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:24:3)


**דִּי הֵימַן בֵּאלָהֵהּ.** אֲשֶׁר הֶאֱמִין בֵּאלֹהָיו:


**because he believed in his God** [as translated,] because he believed in his God.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:25:1)


**דִּי אֲכַלוּ קַרְצוֹהִי.** אֲשֶׁר הִלְשִׁינוּ עָלָיו:


**who had informed on Daniel** [as translated,] who had informed on Daniel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 6:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:25:2)


**וְלָא מְטוֹ לְאַרְעִית גּוּבָּא.** לֹא הִגִּיעוּ לְתַחְתִּיּוֹת הַבּוֹר עַד שֶׁמָּשְׁלוּ בָּהֶם הָאֲרָיוֹת וְכָל עַצְמוֹתֵיהֶם הַדִּיקוּ:


**and they did not reach the bottom of the pit** They did not reach the bottom of the pit before the lions overwhelmed them and crushed their bones to powder.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:26:1)


**בְּאֵדַיִן דָּרְיָוֶשׁ מַלְכָּא וְכוּ׳ דִּי דָאֲרִין בְּכָל אַרְעָא** שַׁדְּרִין בְּכָל הָאָרֶץ, **שְׁלָמְכוֹן יִסְגֵּא**:


**Then King Darius...that lived in all the land, May your peace increase!** [as translated,] that lived in all the land, May your peace increase!



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Daniel 6:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/6:29:1)


**וּבְמַלְכוּת כּוֹרֶשׁ.** שֶׁמָּלַךְ אַחַר דָּרְיָוֶשׁ, שֶׁלֹּא מָלַךְ דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי אֶלָּא שָׁנָה אַחַת וְנֶהֱרַג בַּמִּלְחָמָה, כַּאֲשֶׁר כָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסֵף בֶּן גּוּרְיוֹן, וְהִמְלִיכוּ אֶת כּוֹרֶשׁ חֲתָנוֹ עֲלֵיהֶם בְּתוֹךְ הַחַיִל:


**and in the kingdom of Cyrus** who reigned after Darius, for Darius reigned only one year, and he was slain in battle, as it is written in the book of Joseph ben Gurion, and they crowned Cyrus his son- in-law in the midst of the battle.



## Chapter 7


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:1:1)


**בְּאֵדַיִן חֶלְמָא כְּתַב.** אָז (הַחֲלוֹם כָּתַב) וְרָאשֵׁי הַדְּבָרִים אָמַר:


**then he wrote the dream** Then [he wrote the dream] and told the beginnings of the matters.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:2:1)


**וַאֲרוּ.** וְהִנֵּה:


**and behold** [as translated,] and behold.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:2:2)


**אַרְבַּע רוּחֵי שְׁמַיָּא מְגִיחִין וְגוֹ׳.** אַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת נוֹשְׁבוֹת בָּאוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת הָעוֹלָם וְנִלְחָמוֹת עִם הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל וְסוֹעֲרוֹת אוֹתוֹ:


**the four winds of the heavens were stirring up, etc.** four blowing winds coming from the four directions of the world and waging war with the Great Sea and stirring it up.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:3:1)


**וְאַרְבַּע חֵיוָן.** וְאַרְבַּע חַיּוֹת:


**And four beasts** [as translated,] and four beasts.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:3:2)


**שָׁנְיָן דָּא מִן דָּא.** מְשֻׁנּוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ:


**each one different from the other** [as translated,] each one different from the other.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:4:1)


**קַדְמָיְתָא כְאַרְיֵה וְגַפִּין דִּי נְשַׁר לַהּ.** הִיא כְּאַרְיֵה וְלָהּ כַּנְפֵי נֶשֶׁר; זוֹ הִיא מַלְכוּת בָּבֶל שֶׁהָיְתָה מוֹשֶׁלֶת בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה, וְכָךְ רָאָה אוֹתָהּ יִרְמְיָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״עָלָה אַרְיֵה מִסֻּבְּכוֹ״ (ירמיהו ד:ז), וְאוֹמֵר ״הִנֵּה כַנֶּשֶּׁר יִדְאֶה וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיהו מח:מ):


**The first one was like a lion, and it had the wings of an eagle** It was like a lion, and it had the wings of an eagle; that is the kingdom of Babylon, which was ruling at that time, and so did Jeremiah see it (4:7): “A lion has come up from its thicket,” and he says also (48:40): “like an eagle he shall soar.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:4:2)


**עַד דִּי מְרִיטוּ גַפַּהּ.** נִמְרְטוּ כְּנָפֶיהָ, זוֹ הִיא רֶמֶז לְמַפַּלְתָּהּ:


**until its wings were plucked** Its wings were plucked, which is an allusion to its downfall


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:4:3)


**וּנְטִילַת מִן אַרְעָא.** לְשׁוֹן נִסְתַּלֵּק מִן הָאָרֶץ, רֶמֶז שֶׁתִּבָּטֵל הַמַּלְכוּת מִן הָאָרֶץ:


**and it was taken from the earth** an expression of being removed from the earth, an allusion to the curtailment of the kingdom from the world.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:4:4)


**וּלְבַב אֱנָשׁ.** לְשׁוֹן חֲלָשׁוּת, כְּמוֹ ״יֵדְעוּ גוֹיִם אֱנוֹשׁ הֵמָּה סֶּלָה״ (תהלים ט:כא):


**and the heart of a mortal** Aram. וּלְבַב אֶנָשׁ, an expression of weakness, like (Psalms 9:21): “Let the nations know that they are forever mortal men (אֶנוֹשׁ)”.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:1)


**אַחֲרֵי.** אַחֶרֶת:


**another** [as translated,] another.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:2)


**תִּנְיָנָה.** שֶׁעָלְתָה שְׁנִיָּה מִן הַיָּם:


**second** that emerged second from the sea.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:3)


**דָּמְיָה לְדֹב.** זֶה רֶמֶז לְמַלְכוּת פָּרַס שֶׁתִּמְלֹךְ אַחַר בָּבֶל, שֶׁאוֹכְלִים וְשׁוֹתִים כַּדֹּב וּמְסֻרְבָּלִים בְּבָשָׂר כַּדֹּב:


**resembling a bear** This represents the kingdom of Persia, which will reign after Babylon, who eats and drinks like a bear and is enwrapped in flesh like a bear.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:4)


**דָּמְיָה לְדֹב.** לְדֹב כְּתִיב, כְּמוֹ ״דִּיבָא״ תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל זְאֵב, שֶׁאַף זְאֵב נִקְרֵאת מַלְכוּת פָּרַס, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״עַל כֵּן הִכָּם אַרְיֵה מִיַּעַר זְאֵב עֲרָבוֹת יְשָׁדְדֵם״ (ירמיהו ה:ו):


**resembling a bear** It is spelled לְדֹב [without a “vav,”] like דִיבָא, the Aramaic for זְאֵב, a wolf, for the kingdom of Persia was also called a wolf, as it is said: (Jer. 5: 6): “Therefore a lion smote them, a wolf of the deserts spoils them.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:5)


**וּלְשְׂטַר חַד הֳקִימַת.** וּלְצַד אֶחָד עָמְדָה, רֶמֶז לְשֶׁיִּפְסֹק מַלְכוּת בָּבֶל, תַּמְתִּין פָּרַס שָׁנָה אַחַת שֶׁתִּמְלֹךְ מָדַי:


**and it stood to one side** and it stood to one side, indicating that when the kingdom of Babylon terminates, Persia will wait one year, when Media will reign.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:6)


**וּתְלָת עִלְעִין בְּפֻמַּהּ.** שָׁלֹשׁ צְלָעוֹת. רַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ: שָׁלֹשׁ מְדִינוֹת הָיוּ תָּמִיד מוֹרְדוֹת בָּהּ וּמַשְׁלִימוֹת עִמָּהּ, פְּעָמִים בּוֹלַעְתָּן וּפְעָמִים פּוֹלַטְתָּן (קידושין עב.); זֶהוּ **בְּפֻמַּהּ** בֵּין שִׁנֶּיהָ פְּעָמִים חוּץ לְשִׁנֶּיהָ פְּעָמִים לְתוֹכָן. וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר: **תְּלָת עִלְעִין** שְׁלֹשָׁה מְלָכִים שֶׁיַּעַמְדוּ לְפָרַס: כּוֹרֶשׁ וַאֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ וְדָרְיָוֶשׁ שֶׁבָּנָה הַבַּיִת:


**and there were three ribs in its mouth** Aram. וּתְלָת עִלָעִין בְּפֻמַּהּ, three ribs. Our Sages explained that three provinces were constantly rebelling against it [i.e., Persia] and making peace with it; sometimes it would swallow them and sometimes spit them out. That is the meaning of “in its mouth between its teeth,” sometimes outside its teeth, sometimes inside (Kid. 72a), but I say that the three עִלָעִין are three kings who will rise from Persia: Cyrus, Ahasuerus, and Darius who built the Temple.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:5:7)


**בָּשָׂר שַׂגִּיא.** בָּשָׂר הַרְבֵּה:


**much meat** [as translated,] much meat.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:6:1)


**גַּפִּין אַרְבַּע וְגוֹ׳ וְאַרְבָּעָה רֵאשִׁין.** הֵם אַרְבָּעָה מוֹשְׁלִים שֶׁחִלֵּק לָהֶם אֲלֶכְּסַנְדְּרוֹס מוּקְדּוֹן בְּמוֹתוֹ אֶת מַלְכוּתוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסֵף בֶּן גּוּרְיוֹן; שֶׁהַחַיָּה הַזֹּאת הַשְּׁלִישִׁית הִיא מַלְכוּת אַנְטִיוֹכוֹס, וְנִקְרָא **נְמַר** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהָיְתָה גּוֹזֶרֶת גְּזֵרוֹת עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל מְנֻמָּרוֹת וּמְשֻׁנּוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ:


**four wings...four heads** They are the four rulers to whom Alexander of Macedon allotted his kingdom at his death, as is written in the book of Joseph ben Gurion (Book 3, ch. 14), for this third beast is the kingdom of Antiochus, and it is called נָמֵר because it issued decrees upon Israel [which were] spotted (מְנֻמָּרוֹת) and varied one from the other.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:6:2)


**וְשָׁלְטָן יְהִב לַהּ.** וּמֶמְשָׁלָה נִתְּנָה לָהּ:


**and dominion was given it** [as translated,] and dominion was given it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:1)


**בְּחֶזְוֵי לֵילְיָא.** בְּלַיְלָה אַחֶרֶת. הַשָּׁלֹשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת רָאָה בְּלַיְלָה אַחַת, וְזוֹ בְּלַיְלָה אַחֶרֶת, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהִיא שְׁקוּלָה כְּנֶגֶד שְׁלָשְׁתָּן בְּוַיִּקְרָא רַבָּה:


**in the visions of the night** on another night. The first three he saw on one night, and this one on another night, because it is equal to them all. In Leviticus Rabbah (13:5).


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:2)


**וְשִׁנַּיִן דִּי פַרְזֶל לַהּ.** שִׁנֵּי בַרְזֶל לָהּ:


**and it had...iron teeth** [as translated,] iron teeth.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:3)


**וּמַדֱּקָה.** מְדִיקָה וְשׁוֹחֶקֶת הַדֵּק:


**and crushed** It crushed and ground finely.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:4)


**וּשְׁאָרָא.** מוֹתַר אֲכִילָתָהּ:


**and...the rest** what it left over from its eating.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:7:5)


**וְקַרְנַיִן עֲשַׂר**. עֶשֶׂר קַרְנוֹת. זוֹ פֵּרֵשׁ לוֹ הַמַּלְאָךְ (דניאל ז:כד), שֶׁהֵם עֲשָׂרָה מְלָכִים שֶׁיַּעַמְדוּ לְרוֹמִי קֹדֶם לְאַסְפַּסְיָנוּס שֶׁחָרַב אֶת הַבַּיִת:


**and...ten horns** Aram. וְקַרְנַיִן עֲשַׂר. The angel explained to him that these are the ten kings who would ascend [the throne] of Rome before Vespasian, who would destroy the Temple.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:8:1)


**וְאֵלּוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״וַאֲרוּ״ (דניאל ב:לא), לְשׁוֹן הִנֵּה:


**and behold** Aram. וַאֲלוּ, like וַאֲרוּ, an expression of “behold.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:8:2)


**מְמַלֵּל רַבְרְבָן.** דִּבְרֵי גַּאֲוָה, הוּא טִיטוּס שֶׁאָמְרוּ חֲכָמֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (גיטין נו:) שֶׁחֵרֵף וְגִדֵּף וְנִכְנַס לַהֵיכָל בְּעַזּוּת פָּנִים:


**speaking arrogantly** words of arrogance. That is Titus, about whom the Rabbis, of blessed memory, said (Gittin 56b) that he blasphemed and berated and entered the Heichal with brazenness.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:9:1)


**דִּי כָרְסָוָן רְמִיו.** כִּסְאוֹת הוּטְלוּ וְנִתְקְנוּ לֵישֵׁב בְּמִשְׁפָּט, אֶחָד לְדִין וְאֶחָד לִצְדָקָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


**until thrones were set up** Thrones were set up and established to sit in judgment, one for judgment for the nations of the world and one for charity for Israel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:9:2)


**וְעַתִּיק יוֹמִין יְתִב.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא יוֹשֵׁב לְמִשְׁפָּט עַל הַמַּלְכוּת הַזּוֹ וְעַל שֶׁלְּפָנֶיהָ, שֶׁהִכְעִיסוּ לְפָנָיו וְהֵצִיקוּ לְבָנָיו:


**and the Ancient of Days sat** The Holy One, blessed be He, was sitting in judgment on this kingdom and on those before it, who provoked Him and oppressed His children.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:9:3)


**כְּתַלַג חִוָּר.** לְלַבֵּן עֲוֹנוֹת עַמּוֹ:


**as white as snow** to whiten the iniquities of His people.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:9:4)


**וּשְׂעַר רֵאשֵׁהּ כַּעֲמַר נְקֵא.** מְנַקֶּה עַצְמוֹ מִזְּכֻיּוֹת שֶׁיֵּשׁ לְעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת לְפָנָיו, וּמְשַׁלֵּם לָהֶם כָּל שְׂכָרָם בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה (ויקרא רבה):


**and the hair of His head was like clean wool** He cleanses Himself of the merits that the nations have before Him, and He pays them all their reward in this world. In Lev. Rabbah.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:1)


**דִּי נוּר.** שֶׁל אֵשׁ:


**of fire** [as translated,] of fire.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:2)


**נוּר דָּלֵק.** אֵשׁ בּוֹעֵרָה:


**a burning fire** [as translated,] a burning fire.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:3)


**נָגֵד וְנָפֵק.** מוֹשֵׁךְ וְיָצָא:


**was flowing and emerging** [as translated,] was flowing and emerging.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:4)


**יְשַׁמְּשׁוּנֵּהּ.** לְעַתִּיק יוֹמִין:


**serve Him** i.e., the Ancient of Days.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:5)


**דִּינָא יְתִב.** הַמִּשְׁפָּט נִתְיַשֵּׁב לְפָנָיו:


**justice was established** The judgment is established before Him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:10:6)


**וְסִפְרִין פְּתִיחוּ.** הַסְּפָרִים נִפְתְּחוּ, סִפְרֵי הָעֲבֵירוֹת וְהָרָעוֹת שֶׁעָשׂוּ:


**and the books were opened** The books were opened; the book of the transgressions and the evils that they committed.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:11:1)


**מִן קָל מַלְיָא.** מִקּוֹל הַדְּבָרִים הַגְּבוֹהִים שֶׁהַקֶּרֶן מְדַבֶּרֶת עָלְתָה חֲמָתוֹ שֶׁל עַתִּיק יוֹמִין:


**from the sound of the arrogant words** From the sound of the haughty words that the horn was speaking, the wrath of the Ancient of Days was aroused.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:11:2)


**עַד דִּקְטִילַת חֵיוְתָא וְהוּבַד גִּשְׁמַהּ.** אֲבַד גּוּפָהּ:


**until the beast was slain and its body was destroyed and given** [as translated,] and its body was destroyed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:11:3)


**לִיקֵדַת אֶשָּׁא.** לְלַבַּת אֵשׁ, הוּא אִשּׁוֹ שֶׁל יַעֲקֹב: ״וְהָיָה בֵית יַעֲקֹב אֵשׁ וְגוֹ׳״ (עובדיה א:יח):


**to a flame of fire** to a flame of fire; this refers to Jacob’s fire etc.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:12:1)


**וּשְׁאָר חֵיוָתָא הֶעְדִּיו שָׁלְטָנְהוֹן.** וּלְשְׁאָר הַמַּלְכֻיּוֹת הֵסִירוּ מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם מֶמְשַׁלְתְּהוֹן׃


**But as for the other beasts, their dominion was removed** And from the other kingdoms, their dominion was removed by Heaven.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:12:2)


**וְאַרְכָה בְחַיִּין יְהִיבַת לְהוֹן.** וּזְמַן נִתַּן לָהֶם בַּחַיִּים עַד יוֹם מוֹעֵד לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא מִלְחֲמוֹת גּוֹג וּמָגוֹג (יחזקאל לח:א-ג):


**and they were given an extension of life** And He gave them time to live until a set day in the future, the wars of Gog and Magog.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:12:3)


**אַרְכָה.** הַמְתָּנָה:


**an extension** Aram. אַרְכָה, waiting.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:13:1)


**כְּבַר אֱנָשׁ אָתֵה.** הוּא מֶלֶךְ הַמָּשִׁיחַ:


**one like a man was coming** That is the King Messiah.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:13:2)


**וְעַד עַתִּיק יוֹמַיָּא.** שֶׁהָיָה יוֹשֵׁב בַּמִּשְׁפָּט וְדָן אֶת הָאוּמּוֹת:


**and...up to the Ancient of Days** Who was sitting in judgment and judging the nations.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:13:3)


**מַטָּה.** הִגִּיעַ׃


**came** arrived, reached.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:14:1)


**וְלֵהּ יְהִיב שָׁלְטָן.** וּלְאוֹתוֹ בֶּן אָדָם נָתַן מֶמְשֶׁלֶת הָאוּמּוֹת. דִּימָּה כַּחַיּוֹת, וְיִשְׂרָאֵל דִּימָּה כְּבֶן אָדָם עַל שֶׁהֵם עֲנְוְתָנִים וּתְמִימִים:


**And He gave him dominion** And to that man He gave dominion over the nations, for the heathens he likens to beasts, and Israel he likens to a man because they are humble and innocent.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:14:2)


**דִּי לָא יְעָדָהּ.** לָא יָסוּר:


**which will not be removed** [as translated,] will not be removed.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:15:1)


**אֶתְכְּרִיַּת רוּחִי.** לְשׁוֹן ״כִּי יִכְרֶה״ (שמות כב:לג), וְלָשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי וְעִבְרִי שָׁוִין בּוֹ. כְּלוֹמַר: נִסְחָה מִתּוֹךְ עֹמֶק כְּרִיָּתָהּ:


**My spirit... became troubled** Aram. אֶתְכְּרִיַת an expression of (Exod. 21:33): “or if...digs (יִכְרֶה),” and Hebrew and Aramaic are alike in this case; i.e., it was humbled because of the depth of its digging.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:15:2)


**בְּגוֹ נִדְנֶה.** בְּתוֹךְ תִּיקָה, נַרְתֵּק שֶׁלּוֹ, כְּלוֹמַר: נִבְהֲלָה רוּחִי בְּתוֹךְ הַגּוּף מִן הַחֲלוֹם הַזֶּה:


**within its sheath** Aram. בְּגוֹ נִדְנֶה ; i.e., my spirit became terrified in my body from this dream.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:16:1)


**קָרְבֵת עַל חַד מִן קָאֲמַיָּא.** קָרַבְתִּי אֵצֶל אֶחָד מִן הָעוֹמְדִים שָׁם לִפְנֵי עַתִּיק הַיָּמִים. הַמַּלְאָכִים נִקְרְאוּ עוֹמְדִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (זכריה ג:ז) ״בֵּין הָעֹמְדִים הָאֵלֶּה״, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם קְפָצִים:


**I drew near to one of those standing** I drew near to one of those standing there beside the Ancient of Days. The angels are called standing ones, as it is said: (Zech. 3:7): “among these who stand by (הָעמְדִים),” because they have no joints.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:16:2)


**וְיַצִּיבָא אֶבְעֵא מִנֵּהּ.** וְהָאֱמֶת שָׁאַלְתִּי מִמֶּנּוּ:


**and I asked him the truth** [as translated,] and I asked him the truth.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:17:1)


**אַרְבָּעָה מַלְכִין.** אַרְבַּע מַלְכִיּוֹת:


**four kingdoms** Aram. אַרְבְּעָא מַלְכִין, lit. four kings.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:18:1)


**וִיקַבְּלוּן מַלְכוּתָא קַדִּישֵׁי עֶלְיוֹנִין.** אֵלּוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁיִּטְּלוּ הַמַּלְכוּת מֵהָרְבִיעִית:


**And the high holy ones will receive the kingdom** These are Israel, who will take the kingdom from the fourth one.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:19:1)


**אֱדַיִן צְבִית לְיַצָּבָא.** אָז חָפַצְתִּי לַעֲמֹד עַל הָאֱמֶת מִן הַחַיָּה הָרְבִיעִית:


**Then I wished to determine the truth** Then I wished to determine the truth concerning the fourth beast.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:19:2)


**דִּי הֲוָת שָׁנְיָה מִן כָּלְּהֵין דְּחִילָה יַתִּירָה.** אֲשֶׁר הָיְתָה מְשֻׁנָּה מִכֻּלָּן בְּאֵימָה יְתֵרָה:


**which was different from all of them—excessively dreadful** which was different from all of them with excessive dreadfulness.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:20:1)


**וּנְפַלָה מִן קֳדָמַהּ תְּלָת.** וְנָפְלוּ מִפָּנֶיהָ שָׁלֹשׁ מִן הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת:


**and [the] three [that] fell before it** and three of the first ones fell before it.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:20:2)


**וְקַרְנָא דִכֵּן וְעַיְנִין לַהּ.** וְהַקֶּרֶן אֲשֶׁר כָּזֹאת וְעֵינַיִם לָהּ, כְּכָתוּב לְמַעְלָה (דניאל ז:ח):


**and the horn that was like this and that had eyes** and the horn that was like this, and had eyes, as is written above.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:21:1)


**חָזֵה הֲוֵית.** בַּחֲלוֹמִי הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁסִּפַּרְתִּי לְמַעְלָה (דניאל ז:א-ח):


**I looked** in my first dream, which I told above.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:21:2)


**עִם קַדִּישִׁין.** עַם יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**with the holy ones** with Israel.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:22:1)


**וְדִינָא יְהִב לְקַדִּישֵׁי עֶלְיוֹנִין.** וּנְקָמָה נָתַן לָהֶם מִמֶּנָּה׃


**and gave revenge to the high holy ones** and He gave them revenge from it.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:23:1)


**כֵּן אָמַר.** כֹּה עָנַנִי הַמַּלְאָךְ הַפּוֹתֵר לִי אֶת הַחֲלוֹם:


**So he said** So did the angel who interpreted the dream for me reply to me.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:24:1)


**וְקַרְנַיָּא עֲשַׂר.** וְקַרְנַיִם עֶשֶׂר אֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי לָהּ, זֶה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ:


**And the ten horns** And its ten horns that you saw—this is its interpretation.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:24:2)


**מְנָהּ מַלְכוּתָא.** מֵאוֹתָהּ הַמַּלְכוּת:


**from that kingdom** [as translated,] from that kingdom.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:24:3)


**וְאָחֳרָן יְקוּם אַחֲרֵיהוֹן.** וְהָאַחֲרוֹן יָקוּם אַחֲרֵיהֶן, הוּא טִיטוּס:


**and the last one will rise after them** and the last one will rise after them—that is Titus.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:25:1)


**לְצַד עִלָּאָה.** כְּלַפֵּי מַעְלָה:


**against the Most High** [as translated,] against the Most High.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:25:2)


**וּלְקַדִּישֵׁי עֶלְיוֹנִין יְבַלֵּא.** וּלְיִשְׂרָאֵל יַטְרִיחַ וְיָצִיק:


**and he will oppress the high holy ones** He will burden and oppress Israel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:25:3)


**וְיִסְבַּר לְהַשְׁנָיָא זִמְנִין וְדָת.** יִדְמֶה בְּלִבּוֹ לְהַעֲבִירָם עַל כָּל מוֹעֲדֵיהֶם וְדָתֵיהֶם׃


**and he will think to change the times and the law** He will plan in his heart to cause them to transgress all their appointed times and their laws.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:25:4)


**עַד עִדָּן וְעִדָּנִין וּפְלַג עִדָּן.** קֵץ סָתוּם הוּא זֶה, כַּאֲשֶׁר נֶאֱמַר לְדָנִיֵּאל ״סְתֹם הַדְּבָרִים וַחֲתֹם״ (דניאל יב:ד), וּדְרָשׁוּהוּ הָרִאשׁוֹנִים אִישׁ לְפִי דַעְתּוֹ, וְכָלוּ הַקִּצִּים. וְיֵשׁ לָנוּ לְפוֹתְרוֹ עוֹד כַּאֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי כָתוּב בְּשֵׁם רַב סְעַדְיָה: הֵם אֶלֶף וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת וּשְׁלֹשִׁים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים הָאָמוּר בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר ״אַשְׁרֵי הַמְחַכֶּה וְגוֹ׳״ (דניאל יב:יב); וּפֵרֵשׁ הַמּוֹעֵד עַד עֵת שְׁנֵי עִתִּים וַחֲצִי עֵת, וְאָמַר שֶׁהָעִתִּים הֵם אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וּשְׁמוֹנִים שֶׁמִּיּוֹם צֵאתָם מִמִּצְרַיִם עַד שֶׁנִּבְנָה הַבַּיִת, וְאַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְעֶשֶׂר יְמֵי מִקְדָּשׁ רִאשׁוֹן הֲרֵי שְׁמוֹנֶה מֵאוֹת וְתִשְׁעִים, וְעוֹד חֲצִי הָעֵת הַזֶּה ארבַּע מֵאוֹת אַרְבָּעִים וְחָמֵשׁ הֲרֵי אֶלֶף שְׁלוֹשׁ מֵאוֹת שְׁלוֹשִׁים וְחָמֵשׁ. אֶת אֵלֶּה חֲשׁוֹב מֵעֵת הוּסַר הַתָּמִיד עַד שׁוּב זֶבַח הַתָּמִיד אֶל מְקוֹמוֹ, וְהוּא הוּסַר שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים לִפְנֵי הַחֻרְבָּן; וְיֵשׁ קְצָת רְאָיָה בְּסֵפֶר זֶה. וְעוֹד יֵשׁ מְבִיאִין רְאָיָה לְחֶשְׁבּוֹן זֶה ״הַסְתֵּר אַסְתִּיר פָּנַי״ (דברים לא:יח) ״הַסְתֵּר אַסְתִּיר״ בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא אֶלֶף וּשְׁלוֹשׁ מֵאוֹת שְׁלוֹשִׁים וְחָמֵשׁ:


**until a time, two times, and half a time** This is an obscure end, as was said to Daniel (12:4): “And you, Daniel, close up the words and seal,” and the early commentators expounded on it, each one according to his view, and the ends have passed. We can still interpret it as I saw written in the name of Rav Saadia Gaon, that they are the 1,335 years stated at the end of the Book (12:12): “Fortunate is he who waits [and reaches the days one thousand three hundred and thirty-five],” and he explains the appointed time as until the time of two times and a half time, and he [Rav Saadia Gaon] said that the times are 480 [years], which is the time from the Exodus from Egypt until the Temple was built, and 410 [years], [which are] the days of the First Temple, totaling 890, and another half of this time, 445, totaling 1,335. Figure these from the time the daily sacrifice was discontinued until the daily sacrifice will be restored to its place; it was discontinued six years prior to the destruction, and there is somewhat of a proof in this Book. [See Rashi to 8:14.] Others bring further proof to this computation, namely that (Deut. 31:18): “And I, will hide My face” [the words] הַסְתֵּר אַסְתִּיר add up in gematria to 1,335.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:26:1)


**וְדִינָא יְתִב.** וְהַדִּין נִתְיַשֵּׁב לִפְנֵי הַמָּקוֹם׃


**And the judgment was established** And the judgment was established before Him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:26:2)


**וְשָׁלְטָנֵהּ יְהַעְדּוֹן.** וְשׁוּלְטָנוּ וּמֶמְשֶׁלֶת שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ הַמַּלְכוּת יָסִירוּ מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**and they will remove his dominion** from heaven. They will remove the dominion and the rule of that kingdom.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:27:1)


**וְיִשְׁתַּמְּעוּן.** יִהְיוּ נִשְׁמָעִים לְמִצְוָתוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ:


**and obey** They will obey his command to execute it.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Daniel 7:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:28:1)


**עַד כֹּה.** עַד כָּאן:


**until here** [as translated,] until here.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:28:2)


**שַׂגִּיא רַעְיוֹנַי יְבַהֲלוּנַּנִי.** הַרְבֵּה הָיוּ מַחְשְׁבוֹתַי מְבַהֲלוֹת אוֹתִי:


**my thoughts terrified me greatly** My thoughts were terrifying me greatly.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:28:3)


**וְזִיוַי יִשְׁתַּנּוֹן.** מַרְאִית יוֹפִי וְתֹאֲרִי נִשְׁתַּנּוּ:


**and my colors changed** The appearance of my good looks and my features changed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 7:28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/7:28:4)


**בְּלִבִּי נְטָרֵת.** בְּלִבִּי צָפַנְתִּי:


**I kept...in my heart** I hid in my heart.



## Chapter 8


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:1:1)


**בִּשְׁנַת שָׁלוֹשׁ.** אַחֲרֵי הַמַּרְאֶה הָרִאשׁוֹן, אַחַר שֶׁנִּרְאָה אֵלַי בַּתְּחִלָּה הַחֲלוֹם הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה, שֶׁנִּרְאָה לוֹ בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת לְבֵלְשַׁצַּר (לעיל ז:א):


**In the third year** after the first vision that appeared to me in the beginning, i.e., the dream stated above (7:1), which appeared to him in the first year of Belshazzar.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:2:1)


**עַל אוּבַל אוּלָי.** עַל הַנָּהָר שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ אוּלָי:


**beside the river Ulai** beside the river named Ulai.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:3:1)


**וְלוֹ קַרְנָיִם.** רֶמֶז לְמַלְכוּת פָּרַס וּמָדַי:


**and it had horns** symbolizing the kingdom of Persia and Media.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:3:2)


**וְהָאַחַת גְּבוֹהָה מִן הַשֵּׁנִית.** רֶמֶז לְמַלְכוּת פָּרַס שֶׁגְּדוֹלָה מִמַּלְכוּת מָדַי, שֶׁמַּלְכוּת מָדַי לֹא הָיְתָה אֶלָּא שָׁנָה אַחַת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (לקמן ט:א) ״בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ בֶּן אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ מִזֶּרַע מָדַי״, וְשָׁנִינוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם: לֹא מָצִינוּ שָׁנָה לְמָדַי בַּכְּתוּבִים אֶלָּא זוֹ בִּלְבַד:


**and one was higher than the other** symbolizing the kingdom of Persia, which was greater than the kingdom of Media, for the kingdom of Media existed for only one year, as it says (9:1): “In the first year of Darius the son of Ahasuerus of the seed of Media,” and we learned in Seder Olam (ch. 28): “We do not find any year ascribed to Media in the Holy Writ but this one only.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:3:3)


**וְהַגְּבֹהָה עוֹלָה בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה.** רֶמֶז הוּא שֶׁתַּקְדִּים מַלְכוּת מָדַי לְמַלְכוּת פָּרַס:


**and the higher one sprouted last** This symbolizes that the kingdom of Media will precede the kingdom of Persia.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:5:1)


**הָיִיתִי מֵבִין.** הָיִיתִי מִסְתַּכֵּל בּוֹ:


**I was pondering** I was contemplating it.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:5:2)


**צְפִיר.** שָׂעִיר:


**a he goat** Heb. צְפִיר.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:5:3)


**וְאֵין נוֹגֵעַ בָּאָרֶץ.** דּוֹמֶה הָיָה כִּמְדַלֵּג בָּאֲוִיר:


**and it did not touch the ground** He resembled one skipping in the air.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:5:4)


**קֶרֶן חָזוּת.** קֶרֶן גָּדוֹל לְמַרְאֵה הָעָיִן:


**a conspicuous horn** Heb. קֶרֶן חָזוּת, a large horn visible to the eye.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:7:1)


**וַיִּתְמַרְמַר אֵלָיו.** וַיִּלָּחֵם אֵלָיו, הוּא לְשׁוֹן כַּעַס מַר:


**and it fought with it** Heb. וַיִתְמַרְמַר אֵלָיו, and it fought with it. That is an expression of bitter (מַר) anger.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:8:1)


**חֲזוּת אַרְבַּע.** מַרְאֵה אַרְבַּע קְרָנַיִם:


**an appearance of four** an appearance of four horns.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:1)


**וּמִן הָאַחַת מֵהֶם.** וּמֵאַחַת מֵאֵלּוּ הַמַּלְכֻיּוֹת:


**And from one of them** and from one of these kingdoms.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:2)


**יָצָא קֶרֶן אַחַת.** מַלְכוּת טִיטוּס יוֹצֵאת מִמֶּנּוּ:


**emerged a...horn** The kingdom of Titus emerged from it.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:3)


**מִצְּעִירָה.** מַלְכוּת מוּצְעֶרֶת וְנִבְזֵית, כְּמוֹ שֶׁקָּרְאוּ לְמַעְלָה **קֶרֶן אַחַת מִצְּעִירָה.** וְהוּא עַל עִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״בָּזוּי אַתָּה מְאֹד״ (עובדיה א:ב):


**small** A small and despised kingdom, as he calls it above one small horn, and that is in the manner of (Obad. 1:2): “...you are very despised.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:4)


**וַתִּגְדַּל יֶתֶר.** וַתִּגְדַּל מְאֹד:


**and it became very great** Heb. וַתִּגְדַּל מְאֹד.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:5)


**אֶל הַנֶּגֶב.** הוּא מִצְרַיִם שֶׁהִיא בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**to the south** That is Egypt, which is to the south of the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:9:6)


**וְאֶל הַצְּבִי.** אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנִּקְרֵאת אֶרֶץ צְבִי, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״נַחֲלַת צְבִי צִבְאוֹת גּוֹיִם״ (ירמיהו ג:יט):


**and to the coveted land** Heb. הַצְבִי The land of Israel, called אֶרֶץ הַצְבִי, the coveted land [or the land of beauty] after the manner of (Jer. 3:19): “an inheritance of the beauty of hosts of nations.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:10:1)


**עַד צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם.** הֵם יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנִּמְשְׁלוּ לַכּוֹכָבִים (בראשית כב:יז):


**until the host of heaven** They are Israel, who were compared to the stars.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:11:1)


**וְעַד שַׂר הַצָּבָא.** הוּא בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ שֶׁהוּא בֵּיתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא. **שַׂר הַצָּבָא** כְּלַפֵּי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא חֵרֵף.


**And until the Prince of the host** That is the Temple, which is the House of the Holy One, blessed be He, the Prince of the host. He blasphemed the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:11:2)


**וּמִמֶּנּוּ הוּרַם הַתָּמִיד.** וְעַל יְדֵי גְדוּדָיו וְחֵילוֹתָיו שֶׁשָּׁלַח לִירוּשָׁלַיִם בָּטֵל הַתָּמִיד שֶׁשָּׁלַח שָׁם נִירוֹן קֵיסָר שַׂר צְבָאוֹ; כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה בְּמַסֶּכֶת גִּטִּין וּבְגוֹרְיוֹן:


**and through him the daily sacrifice was removed** And through his troops and his armies, which he sent to Jerusalem, the daily sacrifice was abolished, for he sent there Nero Caesar, his general, as our Rabbis relate in Gittin (56a) and in Gorion.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:12:1)


**וְצָבָא תִּנָּתֵן עַל הַתָּמִיד בְּפָשַׁע.** וּזְמַן מוֹעֵד נִתַּן לִהְיוֹת הַתָּמִיד מְסֻלָּק בִּשְׁבִיל פֶּשַׁע שׁוֹמֵם הִיא עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה. כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁתְּהֵא עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה נִתֶּנֶת בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם תְּמוּרַת הַתָּמִיד. וְכֵן הִיא קְרוּיָה בַּסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה בְּכַמָּה מְקוֹמוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״עַד מָתַי הֶחָזוֹן הַתָּמִיד וְהַפֶּשַׁע שֹׁמֵם״ (דניאל ח:יג), וְאוֹמֵר ״מֵעֵת הוּסַר הַתָּמִיד וְלָתֵת שִׁקּוּץ שֹׁמֵם״ (דניאל יב:יא):


**And a time will be given for the daily sacrifice** A set time will be given for the daily sacrifice to be discontinued because of transgression.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:12:2)


**וְצָבָא.** זְמַן, כְּמוֹ ״הֲלֹא צָבָא לֶאֱנוֹשׁ עֲלֵי אָרֶץ״ (אִיּוֹב ז:א):


**And a time** Heb. וְצָבָא, time, like (Job 7:1): “Is not man on earth for a limited time (צָבָא)”?


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:12:3)


**וְתַשְׁלֵךְ אֱמֶת אַרְצָה.** תּוֹרַת אֱמֶת הִשְׁפִּילָה:


**and it will cast truth to the earth** It humbled the Torah of truth.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:1)


**אֶחָד קָדוֹשׁ.** אֶחָד מִן הַמַּלְאָכִים:


**one holy one** one of the angels.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:2)


**וַיֹּאמֶר.** אוֹתוֹ הַמַּלְאָךְ:


**and...said** that angel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:3)


**לַפַּלְמוֹנִי.** הֲרֵי תֵּיבָה זוֹ כִּשְׁתַּיִם: לִפְלוֹנִי אַלְמוֹנִי. וְתִרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן בֶּן עֻזִּיאֵל בְּסֵפֶר שְׁמוּאֵל (שמואל א כא:ג): ״כְּסִי וְטָמִיר״, כְּמוֹ ״כִּי יִפָּלֵא מִמְּךָ דָבָר״ (דברים יז:ח). אַלְמוֹנִי, אַלְמוֹן מִבְּלִי שֵׁם, שֶׁלֹּא פֵּירַשׁ לוֹ מַהוּ וּמַה שְּׁמוֹ:


**to the anonymous** Heb. לַפַּלְמוֹנִי. This word is like two: פְּלוֹנִי אַלְמוֹנִי, and Jonathan ben Uziel translated it in the Book of Samuel (I 21: 3): “covered and hidden,” like (Deut. 17:8): “If a matter be hidden (יִפָּלֵא) from you.” אַלְמוֹנִי means a widower (אַלְמָן) without a name, [i.e., bereft of a name] for he [the angel] did not explain to him what he was and what his name was.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:4)


**לַפַּלְמוֹנִי הַמְדַבֵּר.** לַמַּלְאָךְ שֶׁהָיָה מְדַבֵּר וְגוֹזֵר גְּזֵרוֹת, **וְצָבָא** הַמּוֹעֵד **תִּנָּתֵן עַל הַתָּמִיד בְּפֶשַׁע**:


**to the anonymous one who was speaking** to the angel who was speaking and issuing decrees, viz. “And a set time will be given for the daily sacrifice because of transgression.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:5)


**עַד מָתַי הֶחָזוֹן.** הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר עַל **הַתָּמִיד** לִהְיוֹת מְסֻלָּק **וְהַפֶּשַׁע שֹׁמֵם** יִהְיֶה נָתוּן תַּחְתָּיו. פֶּשַׁע שֹׁמֵם עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה מֵעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים שֶׁהִיא כְּאֶבֶן דּוֹמֵם, וְכֵן הִיא קְרוּיָה בַּסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה בְּכַמָּה מְקוֹמוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר **עַד מָתַי הֶחָזוֹן הַתָּמִיד וְהַפֶּשַׁע שֹׁמֵם**, וְאוֹמֵר (דניאל יב:יא) ״מֵעֵת הוּסַר הַתָּמִיד וְלָתֵת שִׁקּוּץ שֹׁמֵם״:


**How long will be the vision** this [vision] which concerns the daily sacrifice, that it should be discontinued, and the silent abomination will be placed in its stead. The silent abomination is an idol worshipped by the pagans, which was like a mute stone, and so it is called in this Book in many places, as it says (12:11): “And from the time the daily sacrifice was removed and the silent abomination placed.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:6)


**שׁוֹמֵם.** לְשׁוֹן אִלֵּם וּמְשֻׁתָּק, כְּמוֹ ״אֶשְׁתּוֹמַם כְּשָׁעָה חֲדָא״ (דניאל ד:טז), ״וָאֵשֵׁב שָׁם שִׁבְעַת יָמִים מַשְׁמִים בְּתוֹכָם״ (יחזקאל ג:טו) כְּמוֹ אִלֵּם:


**mute** Heb. שֹּׁמֵם, an expression of being mute and silenced, like (4:16): “was bewildered (אֶשְּׁתּוֹמַם) for awhile;” (Ezek. 3:15): “and I sat there seven days bewildered (מַשְּׁמִים) among them,” like one who is dumb.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:7)


**תֵּת וְקֹדֶשׁ וְצָבָא מִרְמָס.** לָתֵת אֶת מְכוֹן הַמִּקְדָּשׁ וְאֶת צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם אֲשֶׁר הִפִּיל לָאָרֶץ מִרְמָס לְרַגְלָיו, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה ״וַתַּפֵּל אַרְצָה מִן הַצָּבָא וּמִן הַכּוֹכָבִים״ (דניאל ח:י):


**permitting the Sanctuary and the host to be trampled** to make the base of the Sanctuary and the host of heaven, which he cast down to the earth, to be a trampling for his feet, as is stated above (verse 10): “and it cast down to the ground some of the host and some of the stars.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:13:8)


**וְקֹדֶשׁ וְצָבָא**. וָ״ו יְתֵרָה, וְהַרְבֵּה כָּאֵלֶּה בַּמִּקְרָא: ״נִרְדָּם וְרֶכֶב וָסוּס״ (תהילים עו:ז), וְאַף בִּלְשׁוֹן בְּנֵי אָדָם יֵשׁ מְדַבְּרִים כֵּן:


**the Sanctuary and the host** Heb. וְקֹדֶשּׁ וְצָבָא. The “vav” [of וְקֹדֶשּׁ] is superfluous, and there are many such instances in Scripture, e.g. (Ps. 76:7): “chariot (וָרֶכֶב) and horse were stunned,” and also, in the language of people, some speak that way.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:14:1)


**עַד עֶרֶב בֹּקֶר אַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת.** רָאִיתִי פָּתַר בְּשֵׁם רַבֵּינוּ סְעַדְיָה לְדָבָר, וּכְבָר עָבַר וּפָתַר עוֹד: **עַד עֶרֶב בֹּקֶר** עַד שֶׁיָּבוֹא אוֹתוֹ **הָעֶרֶב** שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ ״וְהָיָה לְעֵת עֶרֶב יִהְיֶה אוֹר״ (זכריה יד:ז); וּבְטוּחִים אָנוּ כִּי ״דְּבַר אֱלֹהֵינוּ יָקוּם לְעוֹלָם״ (ישעיהו מ:ח), לֹא יוּפַר. וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר, כִּי **הָעֶרֶב וְהַבֹּקֶר** הָאָמוּר כָּאן גִּימַטְרִיָּא הוּא, וְיֵשׁ סֶמֶךְ לַדָּבָר מִשְּׁנֵי טְעָמִים: הָאֶחָד, שֶׁיְּהֵא חֶשְׁבּוֹן זֶה מְכֻוָּן עִם חֶשְׁבּוֹן אַחֵר שֶׁבְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר; וְהַשֵּׁנִי, שֶׁאָמַר גַּבְרִיאֵל לְדָנִיֵּאל לְמַטָּה בָּעִנְיָן: ״וּמַרְאֵה הָעֶרֶב וְהַבֹּקֶר אֲשֶׁר נֶאֱמַר אֱמֶת הוּא״ (דניאל ח:כו), וְאִם לֹא רֶמֶז הַחֶשְׁבּוֹן הוּא תָּלוּי, לָמָּה חָזַר וְכָפַל אוֹתוֹ לוֹמַר ״אֱמֶת הוּא״. וְהַחוֹזֶה נִצְטַוָּה לִסְתֹּם וְלַחְתֹּם הַדָּבָר (דניאל ט:כד; יב:ד), וְאַף הוּא לֹא נִגְלָה בְּלָשׁוֹן סָתוּם וְחָתוּם. וְאָנוּ נוֹחִיל לְהַבְטָחַת מַלְכֵּנוּ קֵץ אַחַר קֵץ; וּבַעֲבוּר קֵץ הַדּוֹרֵשׁ, נוֹדַע כִּי טָעָה בְּמִדְרָשָׁיו, וְהַבָּא אַחֲרָיו יָתוּר וְיִדְרֹשׁ דֶּרֶךְ אַחֶרֶת. יֵשׁ לִפְתֹּר **עֶרֶב וָבֹקֶר** בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא: חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת וְשִׁבְעִים וְאַרְבַּע, וְעוֹד **אַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת** הֲרֵי אַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁמוֹנֶה מֵאוֹת וְשִׁבְעִים וְאַרְבַּע:


**Until evening and morning, one thousand and three hundred** I saw an interpretation in the name of Rav Saadia Gaon for this matter, but it has already passed, and he interpreted further “until evening and morning,” that evening about which it says (Zech. 14:7): “and it shall come to pass that at eventide it shall be light,” and we are confident that our God’s word will stand forever; it will not be nullified. I say, however, that the עֶרֶב and בֹּקֶר stated here are a gematria, and there is support for this matter from two reasons: 1) that this computation should coincide with the other computation at the end of the Book, and 2) that Gabriel said to Daniel later on in this chapter (verse 26): “And the vision of the evening and the morning is true.” Now, if he had not hinted that the computation was doubtful, why did he repeat it to say that it was true? And the seer was commanded to close up and to seal the matter, and to him, too, the matter was revealed in a closed and sealed expression, but we will hope for the promise of our king for end after end, and when the end passes, it will be known that the expounder has erred in his interpretation, and the one who comes after him will search and expound in another manner. This can be interpreted [as follows]: namely, that עֶרֶב בֹּקֶר has the numerical value of 574, ע = 70; ר = 200; ב = 2; ב = 2; ק = 100; ר = 200. Added together, this equals 574; plus 2,300, we have 2,874.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:14:2)
