**וְנִצְדַּק קֹדֶשׁ.** יְכֻפַּר עֲוֹן יִשְׂרָאֵל, לְבַטֵּל גְּזֵרוֹת מִדְרְסָן וּמְרַפְּסָן מֵאָז גָּלוּ גָּלוּת רִאשׁוֹנָה לְמִצְרַיִם עַד יִגָּאֲלוּ וְיִוָּשְׁעוּ תְּשׁוּעַת עוֹלָמִים בְּמֶלֶךְ מְשִׁיחֵנוּ. וְחֶשְׁבּוֹן זֶה כָּלֶה לְסוֹף אֶלֶף וּמָאתַיִם וְתִשְׁעִים מִיּוֹם הוּסַר הַתָּמִיד, וְהוּא שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר ״וּמֵעֵת הוּסַר הַתָּמִיד לָתֵת שִׁקּוּץ שֹׁמֵם יָמִים אֶלֶף מָאתַיִם וְתִשְׁעִים״ (דניאל יב:יא) יִהְיֶה וְלֹא יוֹתֵר, שֶׁיָּבֹא הַמֶּלֶךְ מְשִׁיחֵנוּ וְיָסִיר שִׁקּוּץ הַשּׁוֹמֵם. וְהַתָּמִיד הוּסַר שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים לִפְנֵי הַחֻרְבָּן בַּיִת שֵׁנִי וְהֶעֱמִיד צֶלֶם בַּהֵיכָל, וְיוֹם שִׁבְעָה עָשָׂר בְּתַמּוּז הָיָה שֶׁשָּׂרַף אַפּוֹסְטְמוֹס אֶת הַתּוֹרָה וּבִטֵּל אֶת הַתָּמִיד וְהֶעֱמִיד צֶלֶם בַּהֵיכָל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת תַּעֲנִית (תענית כו:). וְשֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים שֶׁכָּתַבְתִּי אֵין בְּיָדִי רְאָיָה מְפֹרֶשֶׁת, אַךְ יֵשׁ רְאָיָה שֶׁשָּׁבוּעַ שָׁלֵם לֹא בָּטַל הַתָּמִיד לִפְנֵי הַחֻרְבָּן, שֶׁכֵּן נִבָּא דָּנִיֵּאל עַל טִיטוּס ״וַחֲצִי הַשָּׁבוּעַ יַשְׁבִּית זֶבַח וּמִנְחָה״ (דניאל ט:כז), כְּלוֹמַר: מִקְצָת הַשָּׁבוּעַ שֶׁלִּפְנֵי הַחֻרְבָּן יְבַטְּלוּ הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת; כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ לְמַטָּה בָּעִנְיָן (שם). נַחֲזֹר עַל הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת, כֵּיצַד מְכֻוָּן חֶשְׁבּוֹן **עֶרֶב בֹּקֶר אַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת** מִיּוֹם רֶדֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִצְרַיִם לִהְיוֹת כָּלֶה לְסוֹף אֶלֶף וּמָאתַיִם וַחֲמִשִּׁים לְיוֹם שֶׁהוּסַר הַתָּמִיד: מָאתַיִם וְעֶשֶׂר הָיוּ בְמִצְרַיִם, אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וּשְׁמוֹנִים מִיּוֹם צֵאתָם עַד ״וַיִּבֶן הַבַּיִת״ (מלכים א ו:א); אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְעֶשֶׂר הָיָה הַבַּיִת, וְשִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה גָּלוּת בָּבֶל, וְאַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְעֶשְׂרִים בַּיִת שֵׁנִי, תֵּן עֲלֵיהֶם אֶלֶף וּמָאתַיִם וְתִשְׁעִים שֶׁל קֵץ הַיָּמִים, הֲרֵי אַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁמוֹנֶה מֵאוֹת. צֵא מֵהֶן שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים שֶׁהוּסַר הַתָּמִיד לִפְנֵי הַחֻרְבָּן, שֶׁלֹּא מָנָה הַכָּתוּב אֶלֶף וּמָאתַיִם וְתִשְׁעִים אֶלָּא לְמֵעֵת הוּסַר הַתָּמִיד, הֲרֵי לְךָ חֶשְׁבּוֹן עֶרֶב וָבֹקֶר וְאַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת. עוֹד נוֹסָף בַּחֶשְׁבּוֹן ״אַשְׁרֵי הַמְחַכֶּה וְיַגִּיעַ לְיָמִים״ (דניאל יב:יב) אַרְבָּעִים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנָה יְתֵרִים עַל אֶלֶף וּמָאתַיִם וְתִשְׁעִים יֵשׁ לוֹמַר עַל חֶשְׁבּוֹן הָרִאשׁוֹן, יֵצֵא הַמֶּלֶךְ הַמָּשִׁיחַ וְיַחֲזֹר וְיִתְכַּסֶּה מֵהֶם אוֹתָם אַרְבָּעִים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים. וְרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר הַקַּלִּיר יִסֵּד בִּיסוֹד שִׁירוֹ (בְּסִלּוּק יוֹצֵר שֶׁל פָּרָשַׁת הַחֹדֶשׁ): שְׁבוּעִים שִׁשָּׁה הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁתַּיִם; וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר: הַשָּׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים שֶׁלֹּא בָּאוּ לְחֶשְׁבּוֹן שָׁבוּעַ לֹא מָנָה. וַאֲנִי מָצָאתִי כָּךְ בְּמִדְרַשׁ רוּת, שֶׁעָתִיד מֶלֶךְ הַמָּשִׁיחַ לְהִתְכַּסּוֹת אַרְבָּעִים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים לְאַחַר שֶׁיִּגָּלֶה, וּמֵבִיא רְאָיָה מִן הַמִּקְרָאוֹת הַלָּלוּ:


**and the holy ones shall be exonerated** The iniquity of Israel shall be expiated to bring an end to the decrees of their being trodden upon and crumbled since they were exiled in their first exile to Egypt, until they will be redeemed and saved with a perpetual salvation by our king Messiah, and this computation terminates at the end of 1,290 years from the day the daily sacrifice was removed, and that is what is stated at the end of the Book (12:11): “And from the time the daily sacrifice is removed, and the silent abomination placed, will be 1,290 years,” and no more, for our king Messiah will come and remove the silent abomination. The daily sacrifice was removed six years before the destruction of the Second Temple, and an image was set up in the Heichal. Now that was the seventeenth day of Tammuz, when Apostomos burned the Torah, put an end to the daily sacrifice, and set up an image in the Heichal, as we learned in Tractate Ta’anith (26b), but for the six years that I mentioned, I have no explicit proof, but there is proof that the daily sacrifice was abolished less than a complete shemittah cycle before the destruction, for so did Daniel prophesy about Titus (9:27): “...and half the week of years [shemittah cycle] he will curtail sacrifice and meal-offering,” meaning that a part of the week of years before the destruction, sacrifices will be abolished. So it is explained below in this section. Let us return to the earlier matters, how the computation of “evening and morning, two thousand and three hundred,” fits exactly with the time commencing from the descent to Egypt to terminate at the end of 1,290 years until the day that the daily sacrifice was abolished: 210 years they were in Egypt. 480 years transpired from the Exodus until the building of the Temple. 410 years the Temple existed. 70 years was the Babylonian exile. 420 years the Second Temple stood. 1,290 should be added until the end of days, totaling: 2,880. Subtract six years that the daily sacrifice was removed before the destruction, for Scripture counted 1,290 years only from the time that the daily sacrifice was removed. Here you have the computation of “evening and morning, and 2,300” added to the computation. Fortunate is he who waits and reaches the end of days 45 years over 1,290 [years]. We may say that the king Messiah will come according to the first computation, and he will subsequently be concealed from them for forty-five years. Rabbi Elazar HaKalir established (in the concluding poem of the portion dealing with the month of Nissan): in the foundation of his song: six weeks of years, totaling 42. We may say that the three years that did not total a week of years he did not count. And I found it so in Midrash Ruth that the king Messiah is destined to be concealed for forty-five years after he reveals himself, and proof is brought from these verses.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:15:1)


**וָאֲבַקְשָׁה בִינָה.** הָיִיתִי מִתְאַוֶּה שֶׁיָּבִינוּ לִי מִן הַשָּׁמָיִם:


**that I sought understanding** I was longing that they should enable me to understand [the vision] from heaven.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:1)


**בֵּין אוּלָי.** בְּתוֹךְ הַנָּהָר:


**in the midst of the Ulai** in the midst of the river.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:2)


**וַיִּקְרָא.** הָאָדָם:


**and he called** i.e., the man.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:3)


**וַיֹּאמֶר.** לַמַּלְאָךְ אַתָּה גַבְרִיאֵל:


**and said** to the angel, “You, Gabriel.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:4)


**הָבֵן לְהַלָּז אֶת הַמַּרְאֶה.** פָּרֵשׁ לָזֶה אֶת הֶחָזוֹן׃


**enable this one to understand the vision** explain the vision to this one.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:16:5)


**לְהַלָּז.** לְשׁוֹן חֲשִׁיבוּת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁקְּרָאוֹ חֲמוּדוֹת (דניאל ט:כג). כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ׳הַלָּז׳ בַּעַל צוּרָה הוּא, וּמִכָּאן לָמְדוּ בָּאַגָּדָה: יִצְחָק נִדְמָה לְאָבִיו בְּצֶלֶם וּבִדְמוּת, שֶׁנִּקְרָא ״הָאִישׁ הַלָּז״ בְּפָרָשַׁת ׳וַיְהִי כִּי זָקֵן יִצְחָק׳ (בראשית כז:א). ״וַיְהִי יוֹסֵף יְפֵה תֹאַר״ (בראשית לט:ו) זֶהוּ הַנּוֹי, הַמָּנוּי בַּחֲמִשָּׁה דְבָרִים שֶׁהָאָב זוֹכֶה לִבְנוֹ (עדויות ב:ט):


**this one** Heb. לְהַלָּז, an expression of esteem, as he called him חֲמוּדוֹת, one of desirable qualities. Wherever it says הַלָז it refers to a person of form, and from here they learned in the Aggadah: Isaac resembled his father in form and in likeness, for he is called הָאִישּׁ הַלָז in the section commencing, “Now it came to pass when Isaac was old.” (Gen. 39:6): “...and Joseph was fair in form and fair in appearance.” This is the beauty enumerated among the five things that a father merits for his son.

**to the time of the end.** For many days this vision will come about.

**for it is the end of the time** for it is at the time of the end of many days.

**מַלְכֵי מָדַי וּפָרָס.** שְׁתֵּי קְרָנַיִם שְׁתֵּי אֻמּוֹת מוֹלְכוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי לְמַעְלָה (דניאל ח:ג):


**The kings of Media and Persia** The two horns represent two ruling nations, as I explained above.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:21:1)


**הַמֶּלֶךְ הָרִאשׁוֹן.** הוּא אֲלֶכְּסַנְדְּרוֹס מוּקְדוֹן שֶׁהָרַג אֶת דָּרְיָוֶשׁ בֶּן אֶסְתֵּר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמָּצִינוּ בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסִיפוֹן:


**the first king** He is Alexander of Macedon who slew Darius, the son of Esther, as we find in the book of Josiphon (ch. 9).



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:22:1)


**וְהַנִּשְׁבֶּרֶת.** שֶׁרָאִיתָ שֶׁנִּשְׁבְּרָה:


**And the broken one** that you saw being broken.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:22:2)


**וַתַּעֲמֹדְנָה אַרְבַּע תַּחְתֶּיהָ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּסִפּוּר הַחֲלוֹם לְמַעְלָה: אַרְבַּע מַלְכֻיּוֹת, אֲשֶׁר יַעֲמֹדְנָה מֵאוֹתוֹ גּוֹי שֶׁחָלַק אֲלֶכְּסַנְדְּרוֹס מוּקְדָּן מַלְכוּתוֹ בְּמוֹתוֹ לְאַרְבַּע יְלָדִים. וְיוֹסֵף קְרָאָם בְּסִפְרוֹ אַרְבַּע רָאשֵׁי נָמֵר, וְלֹא בְּנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ הָיוּ:


**in whose stead stood four** as is written in the narration of the dream above (verse 8), [there] are four kingdoms which will arise from that nation, for Alexander of Macedon divided his kingdom upon his death [into parts for] four youths, and Joseph called them four heads of a leopard in his book, and they were not the sons of the king.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:22:3)


**וְלֹא בְכֹחוֹ.** וְלֹא יִהְיוּ הָאַחֲרוֹנִים כְּכֹחַ הַמֶּלֶךְ הָרִאשׁוֹן, כִּי חֲלָשִׁים מִמֶּנּוּ יִהְיוּ:


**but not with its strength** The last ones will not be as strong as the first king, but they will be weaker than him.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:23:1)


**וּבְאַחֲרִית מַלְכוּתָם כְּהָתֵם הַפּוֹשְׁעִים.** כְּשֶׁיַּגִּיעַ זְמַן שֶׁיִּתַּמּוּ רִשְׁעֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁבְּבַיִת שֵׁנִי:


**And at the end of their kingdom, when the transgressors have been destroyed** When the end arrives, and the wicked of Israel in the Second Temple will be finished.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:23:2)


**יַעֲמֹד מֶלֶךְ עַז פָּנִים.** הוּא טִיטוּס:


**a brazen-faced person will rise** he is Titus.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:1)


**וְעָצַם כֹּחוֹ.** וְלֹא בִּגְבוּרָה כִּי אִם בַּחֲלַקְלַקּוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר (דניאל יא:כא):


**And his power will become strong** and not with might but with smooth talk, as is explained at the end of the Book (11:21).


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:2)


**וְנִפְלָאוֹת יַשְׁחִית.** וּבְהַפְלֵא וָפֶלֶא **יַשְׁחִית** עוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים:


**and he will destroy wondrously** and with wonder upon wonder, he will destroy.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:3)


**וְהִצְלִיחַ וְעָשָׂה.** חֶפְצוֹ:


**and he will prosper and accomplish** his desire.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:4)


**וְהִשְׁחִית עֲצוּמִים.** הִשְׁחִית אֻמּוֹת רַבּוֹת:


**and he will destroy the mighty** many nations.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:24:5)


**וְעִם קְדוֹשִׁים.** יִשְׂרָאֵל הַמַּאֲמִינִים בַּתּוֹרָה:


**and the people of the holy ones** Israel, who believe in the Torah.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:1)


**וְעַל שִׂכְלוֹ וְהִצְלִיחַ מִרְמָה בְּיָדוֹ.** וְעַל יְדֵי שֶׁהוּא מַשְׂכִּיל בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר יִפְנֶה וְיַצְלִיחַ, יַחֲזִיק בַּמִּרְמָה שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ:


**And through his intellect, he will cause the deceit in his hand to prosper** And because he is clever wherever he turns and [because] he will prosper, he will hold onto deceit with his hand.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:2)


**וּבִלְבָבוֹ יַגְדִּיל.** יִתְגָּאֶה:


**and in his heart he will become proud** Heb. יַגְדִּיל, lit, he will grow larger.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:3)


**וּבְשַׁלְוָה יַשְׁחִית רַבִּים.** בְּמִרְמָה וּבַחֲלַקְלַקּוֹת יַשְׁחִית רַבִּים הַיּוֹשְׁבִים אִתּוֹ בִּבְרִית וְשָׁלוֹם:


**and in tranquility he will destroy many** With guile and with smooth talk he will destroy many who dwell with him with a covenant and in peace.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:4)


**וְעַל שַׂר שָׂרִים יַעֲמֹד.** כְּלַפֵּי מַעְלָה יַטִּיחַ דְּבָרִים. זֶהוּ פֵּרוּשׁ הַחֲלוֹם הַכָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה (״וְעַד שַׂר הַצָּבָא הִגְדִּיל״):


**and over the Prince of princes he will stand** He will speak blasphemously about Heaven. This is the meaning of the dream written above (verse 11): [“And until the Prince of the host it grew.”]


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:25:5)


**וּבְאֶפֶס יָד יִשָּׁבֵר.** וּבְאֵין כֹּחַ יִשָּׁבֵר עַל יְדֵי יַתּוּשׁ חַלָּשׁ שֶׁבַּבְּרִיּוֹת שֶׁנִּכְנַס בְּחוֹטְמוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת גִּטִּין (גיטין נו:):


**and without strength he will be broken** And without strength he will be broken, through a mosquito, the weakest of creatures, which entered his nose, as we learned in Tractate Gittin (56b).



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:26:1)


**וּמַרְאֵה הָעֶרֶב וְהַבֹּקֶר.** אֲשֶׁר נֶאֱמַר לְךָ בַּחֲלוֹם הָעֶלְיוֹן אֱמֶת הוּא:


**And the vision of the evening and the morning** which was said to you in the preceding dream, is true.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:26:2)


**וְאַתָּה סְתֹם הֶחָזוֹן.** אַל תְּפָרְשֵׁהוּ וְסָתְמֵהוּ בְּלִבְּךָ, כִּי לְיָמִים רַבִּים יָבֹא:


**and you close up the vision** Do not explain it, but close it up in your heart, for it will come about in many days.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Daniel 8:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:27:1)


**נִהְיֵיתִי.** לְשׁוֹן הֹוֶה וְשֶׁבֶר, כְּלוֹמַר: נִצְטַעַרְתִּי עַל הַפּוּרְעָנוּת הַזֶּה וְנִדְכֵּיתִי:


**became broken** Heb. נִהְיֵיתִי, an expression of calamity (הוָה) and breach; i.e., I was pained because of this trouble and I was depressed.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:27:2)


**אֶת מְלֶאכֶת הַמֶּלֶךְ.** שֶׁהָיִיתִי מְמֻנֶּה עַל פְּקֻדַּת עֲבוֹדַת מֶמְשֶׁלֶת מַלְכוּת בֵּלְשַׁצַּר, שֶׁמִּנָּהוּ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר אָבִיו, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּתְחִלַּת הַסֵּפֶר ״וְהַשְׁלְטֵיהּ עַל כָּל מְדִינַת בָּבֶל״ (דניאל ב:מח):


**the king’s work** for I was appointed over the assignment of the work of the government of Belshazzar’s kingdom, for Nebuchadnezzar his father had appointed him, as is written in the beginning of the Book (2:48): “...and gave him dominion over all the capital cities of Babylon.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:27:3)


**וָאֶשְׁתּוֹמֵם עַל הַמַּרְאֶה.** וָאֶתְבַּהֵל עַל הֶחָזוֹן:


**and I was terrified about the vision** Heb. וָאֶשְׁתּוֹמֵם עַל הַמַרְאֶה.


###### Rashi on Daniel 8:27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/8:27:4)


**וְאֵין מֵבִין.** שֶׁאֲנִי מִשְׁתּוֹמֵם, כִּי הָיִיתִי לִפְנֵי הַשָּׂרִים מִתְחַזֵּק:


**but no one realized** that I was terrified because I restrained myself before the princes.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:1:1)


**בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ בֶּן אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ.** לֹא זֶהוּ אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ שֶׁבִּימֵי הָמָן (אסתר ג:א), כִּי הוּא מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס הָיָה, וְזֶה דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי אֲשֶׁר הוּמְלַךְ עַל מַלְכוּת כַּשְׂדִּים כְּשֶׁנֶּהֱרַג בֵּלְשַׁצַּר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה ״וְדָרְיָוֶשׁ מָדָאָה קַבֵּל מַלְכוּתָא״ (דניאל ו:א):


**In the first year of Darius, the son of Ahasuerus** This is not the Ahasuerus of the days of Haman, for he was the king of Persia, whereas this one was Darius the Mede who was crowned over the kingdom of the Chaldeans when Belshazzar was slain, as is written above (6:1): “And Darius the Mede took (sic) the kingdom.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:2:1)


**בִּינוֹתִי בַסְּפָרִים.** נִסְתַּכַּלְתִּי בַחֶשְׁבּוֹנוֹת:


**Contemplated the calculations** Heb. בִּינֹתִי בַּסְפָרִים.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:2:2)


**מִסְפַּר הַשָּׁנִים וְגוֹ׳.** הָיִיתִי מְחַשֵּׁב חֶשְׁבּוֹן **הַשָּׁנִים** שֶׁהָיִיתִי סָבוּר עַל מַה שֶׁנִּבָּא ״כִּי לְפִי מְלֹאת לְבָבֶל שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה אֶפְקֹד אֶתְכֶם״ (ירמיה כט:י), דִּמִּיתִי שֶׁהַפְּקִידָה הַזֹּאת הִיא בִּנְיַן הַבַּיִת, וְשִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה כָּלִים בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי מִשֶּׁפָּשְׁטָה מַלְכוּת בָּבֶל יָד עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁכָּבַשׁ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר יְהוֹיָקִים לִהְיוֹת לוֹ עֶבֶד, וְהִיא הָיְתָה שָׁנָה שְׁנִיָּה לְמַלְכוּת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר; דְּאָמַר מַר: שָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה כָּבַשׁ נִינְוֵה, שָׁנָה שְׁנִיָּה עָלָה וְכָבַשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים. צֵא וַחֲשׁוֹב מֵאוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה וְעַד כָּאן וְתִמְצָאֵם. וְאַף בְּמִשְׁנַת סֵדֶר עוֹלָם נִמְצָא הַחֶשְׁבּוֹן הַזֶּה, וְשָׁנִינוּ שָׁם: בִּשְׁנַת מוֹת בֵּלְשַׁאצַּר הֲרֵי שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה מִיּוֹם שֶׁמָּלַךְ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר: שִׁבְעִים חָסֵר אַחַת מִיּוֹם שֶׁכָּבַשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים וְעוֹד שָׁנָה אַחַת לְבָבֶל, עָמַד דָּרְיָוֶשׁ וְהִשְׁלִימָהּ. וּבִרְאוֹתִי כִּי עֲדַיִן אֵין הַגְּאֻלָּה מְמַהֶרֶת לָבֹא, הֲבִינוֹתִי וְנָתַתִּי לֵב לִסְפִירַת הַחֶשְׁבּוֹן, וְיָדַעְתִּי שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לִי לִמְנוֹת לְפִי כִּבּוּשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים, אֶלָּא **לְמַלֹּאות לְחָרְבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלִַם** כְּשֶׁיִּמְלְאוּ **שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה** לְגָלוּת צִדְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁבּוֹ חָרְבָה יְרוּשָׁלַיִם; וְעוֹד יֵשׁ שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לָבֹא, שֶׁהַגָּלוּת הַזֹּאת הָיְתָה בִּשְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה לְכִבּוּשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם: גָּלוּ בְּשֶׁבַע, גָּלוּ בִּשְׁמֹנֶה, גָּלוּ בִּשְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה גָּלוּ בְּתִשְׁעָה עֶשְׂרֵה; וּפֵרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה בְּמַסֶּכֶת מְגִלָּה (מגילה יא:): גָּלוּ גָּלוּת יְכָנְיָה בְּשֶׁבַע לְכִבּוּשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים, שֶׁהוּא שְׁמֹנֶה לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר; גָּלוּ בַּשְּׁנִיָּה בִּשְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לְכִבּוּשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים, שֶׁהִיא תְּשַׁע עֶשְׂרֵה לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**the number of the years, etc.** I contemplated the calculation of the years, for I thought about what Jeremiah (29:10) prophesied: “For at the completion of seventy years of Babylon I will remember you,” and I thought that this remembrance is the building of the Temple, and that the seventy years end in the first year of Darius the Mede, since the kingdom of Babylon stretched forth a hand upon Israel, when Nebuchadnezzar vanquished Jehoiakim to be his slave. Now that was in the second year of Nebuchadnezzar’s reign, as the master said (Meg. 11b): “In the first year he conquered Nineveh; in the second year he went up and vanquished Jehoiakim.” Figure from that year until now, and you will find them [the 70 years]. This calculation is found also in the Mishnah of Seder Olam (ch. 28), and we learned there that in the year of Belshazzar’s death were 70 years from the day that Nebuchadnezzar ascended the throne: seventy minus one since the day that he conquered Jehoiakim, and yet one more year for Babylon, which Darius completed. And when I [Daniel] saw that the redemption was not hastening to come, I contemplated and put my heart to the calculation, and I knew that I should not have counted according to the conquest of Jehoiakim but [according] to the destruction of Jerusalem, when 70 years will be complete from the exile of Zedekiah, when Jerusalem was destroyed. And there are yet 18 years to come, for this exile was in the eighteenth year counting from the conquest of Jehoiakim, as we learned in Seder Olam: “They were exiled in the seventh year; they were exiled in the eighth year; they were exiled in the eighteenth year; they were exiled in the nineteenth year.” Our Sages in Tractate Megillah (11b) explained that they were exiled in the exile of Jeconiah in the seventh year, counting from the conquest of Jehoiakim, which is the eighth year of Nebuchadnezzar’s reign. They were exiled a second time in the eighteenth year, counting from the conquest of Jehoiakim, which is the nineteenth year of Nebuchadnezzar’s reign.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:4:1)


**וָאֶתְוַדֶּה.** עַל חַטַּאת עַמִּי:


**and I confessed** the sins of my people.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:4:2)


**הָאֵל הַגָּדוֹל וְהַנּוֹרָא.** וְלֹא אָמַר גִּבּוֹר. אָמַר: עוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים מִשְׁתַּעְבְּדִים בְּבָנָיו וְאַיֵּה גְּבוּרוֹתָיו?


**O great and awesome God** but he did not say, “mighty.” He said [to himself], “Heathens are enslaving His children, so where are His mighty deeds?”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:5:1)


**וְסוּר.** וְהָלַכְנוּ הָלוֹךְ וָסוֹר מִמִּצְוֹתֶיךָ, טִילְנ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**turning away** We were constantly turning away from Your commandments, tolant in Old French, turning away.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:7:1)


**לְךָ אֲדֹנָי הַצְּדָקָה.** כְּלוֹמַר: מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ וּפוּרְעָנוּת שֶׁהֵבֵאתָ עָלֵינוּ, הַדִּין וְהַצֶּדֶק עִמְּךָ:


**To you, O Lord, is the righteousness** i.e., Your judgments and retributions that You have brought upon us—justice and righteousness are with You.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:7:2)


**וְלָנוּ בֹּשֶׁת הַפָּנִים.** כְּדַאי אָנוּ לְהִתְבַּיֵּשׁ עַל הַמְּאוֹרָע לָנוּ, כִּי אֲנַחְנוּ גָּרַמְנוּ לְעַצְמֵנוּ:


**and to us is the shamefacedness** We should be ashamed for what has happened to us, for we caused it for ourselves.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:7:3)


**כַּיּוֹם הַזֶּה.** כַּאֲשֶׁר אֲנַחְנוּ רוֹאִים הַיּוֹם׃


**as of this day** as we see today.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:9:1)


**לַאדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ הָרַחֲמִים.** שֶׁלֹּא כָלִינוּ בַּעֲוֹנוֹתֵינוּ כִּי מָרַדְנוּ בוֹ:


**To the Lord our God are the mercies** that we did not perish for our iniquities, for we rebelled against Him.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:11:1)


**וַתִּתַּךְ עָלֵינוּ.** וַתַּגִּיעַ עָלֵינוּ, כְּמוֹ ״לֹא נִתַּךְ אָרְצָה״ (שמות ט:לג). וְתִרְגֵּם אוּנְקְלוֹס: ״לָא מְטָאת לְאַרְעָא״:


**have befallen us** Heb. וַתִּתַּךְ, and You caused to reach us, like (Exod. 9:33): לֹא נִתַּךְ אָרְצָה, and Onkelos rendered: did not reach the earth.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:11:2)


**הָאָלָה וְהַשְּׁבוּעָה.** אָלוֹת הַבְּרִית אֲשֶׁר הִשְׁבַּעְתָּנוּ בְּחוֹרֵב ״וְהָיָה אִם לֹא תִשְׁמְעוּ וְגוֹמֵר״ (ויקרא כו:יד), כָּל הַקְּלָלוֹת הַכְּתוּבוֹת שָׁם:


**the curse and the oath** the curses of the Covenant with which You adjured us at Horeb (Lev 26:14): “And if you do not listen etc.”; all the curses written there.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:12:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר נֶעֶשְׂתָה בִּירוּשָׁלִָם.** חֶרֶב, הֶרֶג, וּשְׂרֵפַת הָעִיר, וְנָשִׁים עֻנּוּ, וְשָׁבוּ בְּקוֹלָרִין:


**as was done in Jerusalem** the sword, slaying, the burning of the city; and they humbled women and put captives into neck irons.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:13:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר כָּתוּב בְּתוֹרַת מֹשֶׁה.** רֶמֶז שֶׁהֵעִיד בָּנוּ שֶׁאִם נַעֲבֹר עַל הַשְּׁבוּעָה תָּבֹא עָלֵינוּ **כָּל הָרָעָה הַזֹּאת**:


**As is written in the Law of Moses** a hint that He warned us that if we transgress the oath, all this evil will befall us.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:14:1)


**וַיִּשְׁקֹד.** וַיְמַהֵר:


**hastened** Heb. וַיִשְׁקֹד, and hastened.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:14:2)


**כִּי צַדִּיק.** כִּי הַדִּין עִמּוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ: צַדִּיק הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וּצְדָקָה הִיא עָלֵינוּ, שֶׁמִּהֵר וְהִקְדִּים שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים לְמִנְיַן ״וְנוֹשַׁנְתֶּם״ (דברים ד:כה), כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִתְקַיֵּם בָּנוּ ״כִּי אָבֹד תֹּאבֵדוּן״ (דברים ד:כו). ״וְנוֹשַׁנְתֶּם״ בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא: שְׁמוֹנֶה מֵאוֹת וַחֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם, וְהֵם גָּלוּ לְסוֹף שְׁמוֹנֶה מֵאוֹת וַחֲמִשִּׁים לְבִיאָתָם לָאָרֶץ:


**for...is righteous** for justice is with Him. Our Rabbis, however, explained that the Holy One, blessed be He, is charitable, and it is charity for us that He hastened and brought [the evil] two years before the numerical value of וַנוֹשַּׁנְתֶּם (Deut. 4:25): “and you will have grown old,” in order that [the curse of] “that you shall perish” should not be fulfilled with you. The numerical value of וַנוֹשַּׁנְתֶּם is 852, but they were exiled at the end of 850 [years] after their entry into the land.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:15:1)


**וְעַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ.** מִתְוַדִּים אֲנַחְנוּ כִּי חָטָאנוּ וְרָשַׁעְנוּ:


**And now, O Lord, our God** We confess that we have sinned and have dealt wickedly.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:16:1)


**לְחֶרְפָּה.** לְגִדּוּף:


**a mockery** [as translated,] a mockery.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:16:2)


**לְכָל סְבִיבוֹתֵינוּ.** לְכָל שְׁכֵנֵינוּ, כֻּלָּם מְחָרְפִים אוֹתָנוּ:


**to all those surrounding us** to all our neighbors; they all mock us.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:17:1)


**לְמַעַן ה׳.** לְמַעַן שֵׁם הַקָּדוֹשׁ הַנִּקְרָא עַל בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״מִקְדָּשׁ ה׳ כּוֹנְנוּ יָדֶיךָ״ (שמות טו:יז):


**for the sake of the Lord** For the sake of the holy Name that is called upon the Temple, as it says (Exod. 15:17): “the Sanctuary, O Lord, (אֲדֹנָי) which Your hands have established.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:18:1)


**וּרְאֵה שֹׁמְמוֹתֵינוּ.** וְשִׁמְמוֹת הָעִיר אֲשֶׁר נִקְרָא שִׁמְךָ עָלֶיהָ, כִּי לֹא עַל מִשְׁעֶנֶת צִדְקוֹתֵינוּ אֲנַחְנוּ מַפִּילִים תַּחֲנוּנֵינוּ לְהֵיטִיב לָנוּ, כִּי עַל רַחֲמֶיךָ בָּאנוּ:


**and see our desolations** and the desolations of the city upon which Your name is called, because not upon the support of our righteousness do we cast our supplications to benefit us, but we have come upon Your mercies.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:19:1)


**אֲדֹנָי שְׁמָעָה.** אֶל תַּחֲנוּנֵינוּ:


**O Lord, hear** our supplications.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:19:2)


**אֲדֹנָי סְלָחָה.** לַחֲטָאתֵינוּ׃


**O Lord, forgive** our sins.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:19:3)


**אֲדֹנָי הַקְשִׁיבָה.** אָזְנְךָ לְצַעֲקָתֵינוּ׃


**O Lord, hearken** Your ear to our cry.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:19:4)


**וַעֲשֵׂה.** אֶת בַּקָּשָׁתֵינוּ וְ**אַל תְּאַחַר**׃


**and do** our request and do not delay.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:20:1)


**עַל הַר קֹדֶשׁ אֱלֹהַי.** בְּעַד הַר בֵּית אֱלֹהִים שֶׁיִּבָּנֶה:


**about the mount of the Sanctuary of my God** for the mount of the House of God, that it be built.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:21:1)


**בַּתְּחִלָּה.** בִּימֵי בֵּלְשַׁצַּר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה:


**at first** in the days of Belshazzar, as is written above (8:15 26).


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:21:2)


**מוּעָף בִּיעָף.** הָיָה מוּעָף אֵלַי בִּפְרִיחָה וּבְטִיסָה:


**in swift flight** [He was] caused to fly to me with flying and with gliding.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:22:1)


**וַיָּבֶן.** וַיְבִינֵנִי:


**And he enabled me to understand** Heb. וַיָבֶן.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:23:1)


**בִּתְחִלַּת תַּחֲנוּנֶיךָ יָצָא דָבָר.** אֱמֶת מֵאֵת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֵלַי לְהַגִּיד לְךָ:


**In the beginning of your supplications, a word came forth** a true [word came] from the Holy One, blessed be He, to me to tell you.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:23:2)


**כִּי חֲמוּדוֹת אָתָּה.** דִּבְרֵי חֶמֶד יֵשׁ לְךָ לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**for you have desirable qualities** You have qualities that are desirable to the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:23:3)


**וּבִין בַּדָּבָר.** וְתֵן לֵב לְהָבִין בַּדָּבָר:


**now contemplate the word** Put your heart to understand the word.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:24:1)


**שָׁבֻעִים שִׁבְעִים נֶחְתַּךְ.** עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם מִיּוֹם חֻרְבַּן רִאשׁוֹן בִּימֵי צִדְקִיָּהוּ עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בַּשְּׁנִיָּה:


**Seventy weeks [of years] have been decreed** on Jerusalem from the day of the first destruction in the days of Zedekiah until it will be [destroyed] the second time.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:24:2)


**לְכַלֵּא הַפֶּשַׁע וּלְהָתֵם חַטָּאת.** שֶׁיְּקַבְּלוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת גְּמַר פּוּרְעָנוּתָם בְּגָלוּת טִיטוּס וְשִׁעְבּוּד, וּכְדֵי שֶׁיִּכְלוּ פִּשְׁעֵיהֶם וְיִתַּמּוּ חַטָּאתָם וְיִתְכַּפֵּר עֲוֹנוֹתָם, כְּדֵי **לְהָבִיא** עֲלֵיהֶם צֶדֶק עוֹלָמִים וְלִמְשׁוֹחַ (עֲלֵיהֶם) **קֹדֶשׁ קָדָשִׁים.** הָאָרוֹן וְהַמִּזְבְּחוֹת וּכְלֵי הַקֹּדֶשׁ שֶׁיָּבוֹאוּ לָהֶם עַל יְדֵי מֶלֶךְ הַמָּשִׁיחַ. וּמִנְיַן שָׁבוּעִים אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְתִשְׁעִים שָׁנָה, גָּלוּת בָּבֶל שִׁבְעִים, וּבַיִת שֵׁנִי אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְעֶשְׂרִים:


**to terminate the transgression and to end sin** so that Israel should receive their complete retribution in the exile of Titus and his subjugation, in order that their transgressions should terminate, their sins should end, and their iniquities should be expiated, in order to bring upon them eternal righteousness and to anoint upon them (sic) the Holy of Holies: the Ark, the altars, and the holy vessels, which they will bring to them through the king Messiah. The number of seven weeks is four hundred and ninety years. The Babylonian exile was seventy [years] and the Second Temple stood four hundred and twenty [years].



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:1)


**וְתֵדַע וְתַשְׂכֵּל מִן מוֹצָא דָבָר.** מִתּוֹךְ מוֹצָא דָבָר זֶה שֶׁיָּצָא בִּתְחִלַּת תַּחֲנוּנֶיךָ לְהַגִּיד לְךָ תֵּדַע לְהַשְׂכִּיל לְהָשִׁיב וְלִבְנוֹת יְרוּשָׁלִַם:


**And you shall know and understand from the emergence of the word** From the emergence of this word, which emerged at the beginning of your supplications to tell you, you shall know to understand [how] to restore and build Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:2)


**עַד מָשִׁיחַ נָגִיד.** זְמַן תִּנָּתֵן מִיּוֹם הַחֻרְבָּן עַד בֹּא כֹּרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס, שֶׁאָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עָלָיו שֶׁהוּא יָשׁוּב וְיִבְנֶה עִירוֹ, וּקְרָאוֹ מְשִׁיחוֹ וּנְגִידוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ לִמְשִׁיחוֹ לְכוֹרֶשׁ וְגוֹ׳ הוּא יִבְנֶה עִירִי וְגָלוּתִי יְשַׁלֵּחַ וְגוֹ׳״ (ישעיה מה:א,יג):


**until the anointed king** Time will be given from the day of the destruction until the coming of Cyrus, king of Persia, about whom the Holy One, blessed be He, said that he would return and build His city, and He called him His anointed and His king, as it says (Isa. 45:1): “So said the Lord to His anointed one, to Cyrus etc.” (verse 13): “He shall build My city and free My exiles, etc.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:3)


**שָׁבֻעִים שִׁבְעָה.** שֶׁבַע שְׁמִטּוֹת שְׁלֵמוֹת יִהְיוּ בַגּוֹלָה קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּבֹא כּוֹרֶשׁ, וְעוֹד הָיוּ יוֹתֵר שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, אֶלָּא מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁלֹּא שָׁלְמָה הַשָּׁבוּעַ לֹא נִמְנָה. בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי שֶׁהָיָה דָנִיֵּאל עוֹמֵד בָּהּ כְּשֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לוֹ הֶחָזוֹן הַזֶּה, כָּלוּ שִׁבְעִים לִכְבּוֹשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים. צֵא מֵהֶן שְׁמוֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה שֶׁקָּדְמָה כְבוּשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים לְחֻרְבַּן יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, נִשְׁאֲרוּ חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנָה. הִיא שֶׁשָּׁנוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ: חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנָה לֹא עָבַר אִישׁ בִּיהוּדָה (יומא נד.), הֵם חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנָה אֲשֶׁר מִיּוֹם הַחֻרְבָּן עַד שֶׁחָזְרוּ בִּימֵי כּוֹרֶשׁ. הֲרֵי שֶׁבַע שְׁמִטּוֹת וְשָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים:


**seven weeks** Seven complete shemittah cycles they will be in exile before Cyrus comes, and there were yet three more years, but since they did not constitute a complete shemittah cycle, they were not counted. In the one year of Darius, in which Daniel was standing when this vision was said to him, seventy years from the conquest of Jehoiakim terminated. Deduct eighteen years from them, in which the conquest of Jehoiakim preceded the destruction of Jerusalem, leaving fifty-two years. This is what our Rabbis learned (Yoma 54a): “For fifty-two years no one passed through Judea.” They are the fifty-two years from the day of the destruction until they returned in the days of Cyrus. Hence, we have seven shemittah cycles and three years.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:4)


**וְשָׁבֻעִים שִׁשִּׁים וּשְׁנַיִם תָּשׁוּב וְנִבְנְתָה.** הָעִיר בִּרְחוֹבוֹתֶיהָ וּבַחֲרִיצוֹתֶיהָ:


**and in sixty-two weeks it will return and be built** i.e., the city with its streets.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:5)


**וְחָרוּץ**. הֵם חֲרִיצִים שֶׁעוֹשִׂין סְבִיב הַחוֹמָה לְחִזּוּק הָעִיר, שֶׁקּוֹרִין קאבה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and moat** Heb. וְחָרוּץ. They are the moats that they make around the wall to strengthen the city, which are called fosse in French, ditch or moat.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:6)


**וּבְצוֹק הָעִתִּים.** וְאוֹתָן **הָעִתִּים** יִהְיוּ **בְצוֹק** וּבְצָרָה, כִּי בְּשִׁעְבּוּד מַלְכֵי פָרַס וְעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים יִהְיוּ מַטְרִיחִים עֲלֵיהֶם בְּשִׁעְבּוּד קָשֶׁה, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵם שִׁשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁבֻעִים וְעוֹד אַרְבַּע שָׁנִים שֶׁנּוֹתְרוּ מִן הַשָּׁבוּעַ הַשְּׁמִינִי, שֶׁהָיְתָה תְּחִלָּתוֹ נִבְלַעַת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים בְּתוֹךְ חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שֶׁל הֶמְשֵׁךְ הַגּוֹלָה, לֹא נִמְנוּ כָאן אוֹתָן אַרְבַּע שָׁנִים, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא מָנָה כָאן אֶלָּא שָׁבֻעִים. וַהֲרֵי אַתָּה מוֹצֵא שֶׁמִּתְּחִלָּה הִתְחִיל לִמְנוֹת שָׁבֻעִים שִׁבְעִים, וּבַסּוֹף כְּשֶׁפֵּרַשׁ עִתֵּיהֶם וּמִשְׁפָּטֵיהֶם, לֹא מָנָה אֶלָּא שִׁשִּׁים וָתֵשַׁע, אֶלָּא שָׁבוּעַ אֶחָד נֶחְלַק מִקְצָתוֹ לְכָאן וּמִקְצָתוֹ לְכָאן, וְהוּא לֹא הִזְכִּיר אֶלָּא הַשָּׁבוּעִים הַשְּׁלֵמִים:


**but in troubled times** But in those times they will be troubled and distressed, for in the subjugation of the kings of Persia and the heathens, they will burden them with harsh bondage. Now although there are sixty-two weeks and four years more that remain from the eighth week, whose beginning, viz. the three years, was included in the fifty-two years of the duration of the exile, those four years were not counted here because here he counted only weeks, and you find that from the beginning he started to count seventy weeks, and at the end, when he delineated their times and their judgments, he counted only sixty-nine, proving that one week was divided, part of it here and part of it there; and he mentioned only whole weeks.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:25:7)


**וּבְצוֹק הָעִתִּים.** בְּצוּקָה יִהְיוּ אוֹתָן הָעִתִּים:


**but in troubled times** They will be troubled in those times.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:1)


**וְאַחֲרֵי.** אוֹתָן **שָׁבוּעִים**:


**And after** those weeks.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:2)


**יִכָּרֵת מָשִׁיחַ.** יֵהָרֵג אַגְרִיפַּס מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה שֶׁהָיָה מוֹשֵׁל בִּימֵי הַחֻרְבָּן:


**the anointed one will be cut off** Agrippa, the king of Judea, who was ruling at the time of the destruction, will be slain.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:3)


**וְאֵין לוֹ.** כְּמוֹ וְאֵינֶנּוּ:


**and he will be no more** Heb. וְאֵין לוֹ, and he will not have. The meaning is that he will not be.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:4)


**מָשִׁיחַ.** אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן שַׂר וְגָדוֹל:


**the anointed one** Heb. מָשִׁיחַ. This is purely an expression of a prince and a dignitary.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:5)


**וְהָעִיר וְהַקֹּדֶשׁ.** וְהָעִיר וְהַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**and the city and the Sanctuary** lit. and the city and the Holy.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:6)


**יַשְׁחִית עַם נָגִיד הַבָּא.** עֲלֵיהֶם הוּא טִיטוּס וְאוּכְלוּסָיו:


**and the people of the coming monarch will destroy** [The monarch who will come] upon them. That is Titus and his armies.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:7)


**וְקִצּוֹ בַשֶּׁטֶף.** וְסוֹפוֹ לִקְלָלָה וַאֲבַדּוֹן שֶׁיִּשְׁטֹף כֹּחַ מַלְכוּתוֹ עַל יְדֵי מָשִׁיחַ:


**and his end will come about by inundation** And his end will be damnation and destruction, for He will inundate the power of his kingdom through the Messiah.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:8)


**וְעַד קֵץ מִלְחָמָה.** גּוֹג תְּהֵא הָעִיר:


**and until the end of the wars** of Gog the city will exist.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:26:9)


**נֶחֱרֶצֶת שֹׁמֵמוֹת.** חָרַץ שֶׁל שׁוֹמֵמוֹת:


**cut off into desolation** a destruction of desolation.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Daniel 9:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:1)


**וְהִגְבִּיר בְּרִית לָרַבִּים שָׁבוּעַ אֶחָד.** לָרַבִּים לַשָּׂרִים, כְּמוֹ ״וְכָל רַבֵּי הַמֶּלֶךְ״ בְּסֵפֶר יִרְמְיָה (ירמיהו לט:יג):


**And he will strengthen a covenant for the princes for one week** לָרַבִּים, for the princes, like “and all the officers of (רַבֵּי) the king,” in the Book of Jeremiah (39:13).


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:2)


**וְהִגְבִּיר.** טִיטוּס בְּרִית לְשָׂרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**will strengthen** Titus [will strengthen] a covenant with the princes of Israel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:3)


**שָׁבוּעַ אֶחָד.** יַבְטִיחֵם בְּהַגְבָּרַת בְּרִית וְשַׁלְוָה עַד שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים, וּבְתוֹךְ הַשָּׁבוּעַ יָפֵר אֶת בְּרִיתוֹ:


**for one week** He will promise them the strengthening of a covenant and peace for seven years, but within the seven years, he will abrogate his covenant.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:4)


**יַשְׁבִּית זֶבַח וּמִנְחָה.** וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר בַּחֲזָיוֹן הָרִאשׁוֹן ״וּבְשַׁלְוָה יַשְׁחִית רַבִּים״ (דניאל ח:כה), מִתּוֹךְ בְּרִית שַׁלְוָה יַשְׁחִיתֵם:


**he will abolish sacrifice and meal-offering** This is what he says in the first vision (8: 26): “and in tranquility he will destroy many.” Through a covenant of tranquility, he will destroy them.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:5)


**וְעַל כְּנַף שִׁקּוּצִים מְשֹׁמֵם.** כִּנּוּי שֶׁל גְּנַאי עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת הוּא, כְּלוֹמַר: **וְעַל** מוֹשַׁב גֹּבַהּ בֵּין **שִׁקּוּצִים** וְתִעוּב יַנִּיחַ אֶת **הַשּׁוֹמֵם**, אֶת הָעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, שֶׁהִיא נֶאֱלֶמֶת כְּאֶבֶן דּוֹמֵם:


**and on high, among abominations will be the dumb one** This is a pejorative for pagan deities. i.e., on a high place, among abominations and disgusting things, he will place the dumb one, the pagan deity, which is dumb like a silent stone.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:6)


**כָּנָף.** לְשׁוֹן גּוֹבַהּ, כִּכְנַף עוֹף הַפּוֹרֵחַ:


**high** Heb. כְּנַף, lit. wing, an expression of height, like the wing of a flying bird.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:7)


**וְעַד כָּלָה וְנֶחֱרָצָה תִּתַּךְ עַל שׁוֹמֵם.** וְתִתְקַיֵּם מֶמְשֶׁלֶת הַשִּׁקּוּץ **עַד** יוֹם אֲשֶׁר **תִּתַּךְ** עָלֶיהָ **כָּלָה וְנֶחֱרָצָה** הַנִּגְזוֹר עָלֶיהָ בִּימֵי מֶלֶךְ הַמָּשִׁיחַ:


**and until destruction and extermination befall the dumb one** and the ruling of the abomination will endure until the day that the destruction and extermination decreed upon it [will] befall it, in the days of the king Messiah.


###### Rashi on Daniel 9:27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/9:27:8)


**תִּתַּךְ עַל שׁוֹמֵם.** תָּגִיחַ וְתֵרֵד כִּלָּיוֹן חָרוּץ (ישעיה י:כב) עַל הָעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת וְעַל עוֹבְדֶיהָ:


**befall the dumb one** Heb. תִּתַּךְ, reach; and total destruction will descend upon the image of the pagan deity and upon its worshippers.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:1:1)


**וְצָבָא גָדוֹל.** וּלִזְמַן אָרוֹךְ יָבֹא:


**and for a long time** and it will come in a long time.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:1:2)


**וּבִין אֶת הַדָּבָר.** וּלְהָבִין אֶת הַדָּבָר נִגְלָה לִי אֶת הֶחָזוֹן:


**and he understood the word** and to understand the word, the vision was revealed to me.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:1:3)


**וּבִינָה לוֹ בַּמַּרְאֶה.** וּלְהָבִינָהּ לוֹ בַּמַּרְאֶה. וְאֵין הַתֵּיבָה הַזֹּאת שֵׁם דָּבָר כְּמוֹ ״אִם יָדַעְתָּ בִינָה״ (אִיּוֹב לח:ד), אֶלָּא ה״א יְתֵרָה, וְהוּא כְּמוֹ ״בִּינָה הֲגִיגִי״ (תְּהִלִּים ה:ב), לְפִיכָךְ הַטַּעַם לְמַעְלָה בַּבֵּי״ת, וּפֵירוּשָׁהּ כְּמוֹ הָבֵן:


**and he understood it in the vision** Heb. וּבִינָה and to make him understand it in the vision. Now this word is not a noun, like (Job. 38:4): “if you know understanding (בִּינָה) Instead, the “hey” is superfluous, and it is like (Ps 5:2) “understand (בִּינָה) my thoughts” Therefore, the accent is above on the “beth,” and its meaning is like הָבֵן, understand.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:2:1)


**הָיִיתִי מִתְאַבֵּל.** כְּשֶׁרָאָה שֶׁבִּטֵּל כּוֹרֶשׁ מְלֶאכֶת הַבַּיִת שֶׁצִּוָּה לְהַתְחִיל וְחָזַר בּוֹ עַל פִּי כְּתַב צָרֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין רְחוּם בְּעֵל טְעֵם וְשִׁמְשַׁי סָפְרָא, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר עֶזְרָא (עזרא ד:ח):


**had been mourning** when he saw that Cyrus had curtailed the construction of the Temple, for he had ordered to commence it, and he reneged because of the missive of the adversaries of Judah and Benjamin: Rehum the adviser and Shimshai the scribe, as is written in the Book of Ezra (4:5).


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:2:2)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה שָׁבֻעִים יָמִים.** עֶשְׂרִים וְאַחַת שָׁנָה הֵם, שְׁמוֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה מִשְּׁנָה אַחַת לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי אֲשֶׁר שָׂם אֶל לִבּוֹ לַחְשֹׁב שִׁבְעִים שְׁנֵי הַגּוֹלָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב, עַד שְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הַפַּרְסִי (עזרא ד:כד) בֶּן אֶסְתֵּר שֶׁבָּנָה הַבַּיִת. וְשָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים הַיְתֵרִים לֹא יָדַעְתִּי אִם לִפְנֵיהֶם הִתְחִיל לְהִתְעַנּוֹת, אוֹ מֶשֶׁךְ קַבָּלַת נֶדֶר תַּעֲנִיּוֹתָיו שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים לְאַחַר הַבִּנְיָן:


**three weeks of days** twenty-one years. They are eighteen years from the one year of Darius the Mede, when he [Daniel] put to his heart to calculate the seventy years of the exile, as it is written: until the second year of Darius the Persian, the son of Esther, who built the Temple. As for the three extra years, I do not know whether he started to fast before them or whether the duration of the acceptance of the vow of his fasts terminated three years after the building.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:3:1)


**לֶחֶם חֲמֻדוֹת.** פַּת נְקִיָּה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁתִּרְגֵּם אוּנְקְלוֹס אֶת ״בִּגְדֵי עֵשָׂו הַחֲמֻדוֹת״ (בראשית כז:טו): ״דַּכְיָתָא״:


**white bread** Heb. לֶחֶם חֲמֻדֹת, white (lit. clean) bread, as Onkelos rendered (Gen. 27:15): “Esau’s clean clothes (הַחֲמֻדֹת),” דַּכְיָתָא, clean.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:5:1)


**לְבוּשׁ בַּדִּים.** בֶּגֶד בּוּץ:


**clad in linen** a linen garment.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:5:2)


**בְּכֶתֶם אוּפָז.** בַּחֲגוֹרָה שֶׁל קְבוּצַת מַרְגָּלִיּוֹת. כָּל כֶּתֶם לְשׁוֹן קְבוּצַת זָהָב וְאֶבֶן יְקָרָה:


**with a girdle of gold studded with jewels** Heb. בְּכֶתֶם אוּפָז, with a girdle of a cluster of pearls. Every [instance of] כֶּתֶם is an expression of a cluster of gold and precious stones.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:6:1)


**וּגְוִיָּתוֹ כְתַרְשִׁישׁ.** פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה בִּשְׁחִיטַת חֻלִּין (צא.): גּוּפוֹ גָּדוֹל שְׁנֵי אֲלָפִים פַּרְסָה כְּמִדַּת יָם שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ תַרְשִׁישׁ וְהוּא יָמָּהּ שֶׁל אַפְרִיקָא:


**And his body was like tarshish** Our Sages of blessed memory explained in Tractate Hullin (9lb): “His body was two thousand parasangs large, like the measurement of the sea named Tarshish, and that is the sea of Africa (the Mediterranean).”


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:6:2)


**קָלָל.** צָרוּף וּמְזֻקָּק וּמַצְהִיב:


**brandished** Heb. קָלָל, smelted, refined, and shiny.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:6:3)


**כְּקוֹל הָמוֹן.** כְּקוֹל קְבוּצַת הָמוֹן רַב, שֶׁקּוֹלָם נִשְׁמָע לְמֵרָחוֹק:


**like the voice of a multitude** like the voice of a gathering of a large multitude, whose voice is heard from afar.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:7:1)


**וְהָאֲנָשִׁים אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ עִמִּי.** אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה הֵם חַגַּי זְכַרְיָה וּמַלְאָכִי (מגילה ג.):


**but the men who were with me** Our Sages of blessed memory said that they were Haggai, Zechariah, and Malachi.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:7:2)


**אֲבָל חֲרָדָה גְדוֹלָה נָפְלָה עֲלֵיהֶם.** אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (ברכות ו.): אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאָדָם אֵינוֹ רוֹאֶה דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא נִבְעָת מִמֶּנּוּ, מַזָּלוֹ שֶׁבָּרָקִיעַ רוֹאֵהוּ, וּלְכָךְ נִבְעָת:


**but a great quaking fell upon them** Our Sages of blessed memory said that although a person does not see something of which he is terrified, his guardian angel, who is in heaven, does see it; therefore, he becomes terrified.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:8:1)


**נֶהְפַּךְ עָלַי לְמַשְׁחִית.** לְהַשְׁחָתָה, וְדֶרֶךְ הַמִּקְרָאוֹת לְדַבֵּר כֵּן, כְּמוֹ (ירמיה ה:ח) ״סוּסִים מְזֻיָּנִים מַשְׁכִּים הָיוּ״, (מַשְׁכִּים) בְּהַשְׁכָּמַת הַבֹּקֶר:


**was turned in me into corruption** Heb. לְמַשְׁחִית, lit. to a destroyer. To corruption, and it is customary for Biblical verses to speak in this manner, like (Jer. 5:8): “They were like armed stallions early in the morning.” מַשְׁכִּים is the equivalent of בְּהַשְׁכָּמַת הַבּקֶר, in the early morning.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:8:2)


**וְלֹא עָצַרְתִּי כֹּחַ.** כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא כָּנַסְתִּי כָּל כֹּחִי (כִּי כֹּחִי) הָלַךְ מִקִּרְבִּי:


**I could not summon up strength** i.e., I did not gather. All my strength left my innards.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:9:1)


**נִרְדָּם.** מֵרוֹב פַּחַד:


**I fell into a sound sleep** out of great fright.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:10:1)


**וַתְּנִיעֵנִי.** כְּאָדָם הַמֵּנִיעַ אֶת הַיָּשֵׁן לַהֲקִיצוֹ:


**and moved me** as one who moves a sleeping person to awaken him.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:11:1)


**אִישׁ חֲמֻדוֹת.** אִישׁ טָהוֹר:


**man of desirable qualities** אִישׁ חֲמֻדוֹת, pure man.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:11:2)


**עַל עָמְדֶךָ.** עַל מַעֲמַד רַגְלֶיךָ:


**upright** Heb. עַל עָמְדֶךָ, on the stand of your feet.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:11:3)


**שָׁלַחְתִּי אֵלֶיךָ.** נִשְׁתַּלַּחְתִּי אֵלֶיךָ מֵאֵת הַמָּקוֹם:


**I have been sent to you** I have been sent to you from the Omnipresent.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:11:4)


**מַרְעִיד.** מַרְתִּית׃


**quaking** [as translated,] quaking.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:12:1)


**וַאֲנִי בָאתִי בִּדְבָרֶיךָ.** בָּאתִי בִּשְׁלִיחוּת זוֹ בִּשְׁבִיל דְּבָרֶיךָ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת יוֹמָא: וַאֲנִי בָאתִי לְתוֹךְ הַפַּרְגּוֹד עַל יָדְךָ, שֶׁהָיִיתִי מְגֹרָשׁ מִתּוֹכוֹ (יומא עז.):


**and I have come because of your words** I have come on this mission because of your words. Our Rabbis, however, explained in Tractate Yoma (77a): “and I came within the curtain because of you, for I was banned from its midst.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:13:1)


**עוֹמֵד לְנֶגְדִּי.** נִלְחָם עִמָּדִי בָּרָקִיעַ, כְּשֶׁשּׁוֹאֵל אֹרֶךְ מְלוּכָה לְפָרַס לְשַׁעְבֵּד אֶתְכֶם. הֲרֵי עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁתַּיִם יוֹם שֶׁהוּא **עוֹמֵד לְנֶגְדִּי**:


**has been standing against me** to battle with me in heaven by requesting an extension for the kingdom for Persia to enable them to subjugate you (Israel). Behold twenty-one days that he has been standing against me.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:13:2)


**הַשָּׂרִים הָרִאשֹׁנִים.** כְּלוֹמַר: הַחֲשׁוּבִים, רִאשׁוֹנָה בַּנִּכְנָסִים:


**the first princes** those counted first among those who enter.


###### Rashi on Daniel 10:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:13:3)


**וַאֲנִי נוֹתַרְתִּי שָׁם.** לְשַׁתֵּק אֶת שַׂר פָּרַס שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַיִם:


**and I remained there** to silence the princes of Persia in heaven.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:14:1)


**כִּי עוֹד חָזוֹן.** לְהַגִּיד לְךָ מַה שֶּׁלֹּא הֻגַּד לְךָ, וְיָבֹא עוֹד **לַיָּמִים** הָרַבִּים הַנְּתוּנִים לַמּוֹעֵד:


**for there is yet a vision** to tell you what has not yet been told, and it will come in many days at an appointed time.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:16:1)


**נֶהֶפְכוּ צִירַי עָלַי.** אֵלּוּ רָאשֵׁי עֲצָמוֹת כָּל אֵבֶר וְאֵבֶר הַתְּקוּעִים בְּחוֹרֵי מוֹשָׁבָם וְסוֹבֵב בְּתוֹכָם כְּדֶלֶת עַל צִירָהּ בְּחוֹר הַמִּפְתָּן. **צִירַי נֶהֶפְכוּ** לְהִשָּׁמֵט מִמְּקוֹמָם מֵרוֹב רֶתֶת וַחֲרָדָה:


**my joints turned upon me** Heb. צִירַי. These are the ends of the bones, all the limbs that are thrust into their sockets and turn within them like a door on its hinge in the socket of the threshold. My joints turned to become dislocated from their place out of much trembling and quaking.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:17:1)


**עֶבֶד אֲדֹנִי זֶה.** עַל עַצְמוֹ הוּא אוֹמֵר עֶבֶד וְעַל הַמַּלְאָךְ הוּא אוֹמֵר אֲדֹנִי, כְּלוֹמַר וְאֵיךְ יוּכַל עַבְדְּךָ זֶה לְדַבֵּר עִמָּךְ:


**this servant of my lord** He refers to himself as a servant, and to the angel as “my lord,” i.e., to say, How will your servant be able to speak with you?



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:20:1)


**וְעַתָּה אָשׁוּב לְהִלָּחֵם עִם שַׂר פָּרָס.** וְיָדַעְתִּי כִּי אוּכַל, אַךְ כְּבֹא עֵת מַלְכוּת אַנְטִיוֹכוּס יָדַעְתִּי שֶׁאֲנִי אֵצֵא וְהוּא יִכָּנֵס, לְאַחַר שְׁלוֹשִׁים וְאַרְבַּע שָׁנָה לְבִנְיַן הַבַּיִת, וְשֶׁיַּעֲמֹד אֲלֶכְּסַנְדְּרוֹס מוּקְדָּן. כִּי אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים לְמַלְכֵי פָרַס בְּאוֹתָן הַיָּמִים, עֲבוֹדָה נוֹחָה עָבְדוּ בָהֶם וְלֹא הִכְבִּידוּ עֲלֵיהֶם, כִּי נְתָנָם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְרַחֲמִים לָהֶם, אֲבָל מַלְכוּת יָוָן הֵטִילוּ עֲלֵיהֶם עֹל קָשֶׁה:


**And now I shall return to battle with the prince of Persia** and I know that I will be victorious, but when the time of the kingdom of the heathens [Greece Malbim ed., Antiochus Vilna ed.] arrives, I know that I will leave, and he will enter after thirty-four years of the existence of the Temple. Alexander of Macedon will rise, for although Israel was subjugated by the kings of Persia in those days, they exacted a light tribute from them, and they did not burden them heavily because the Holy One, blessed be He, caused them to have mercy on them. But the kings of the heathens [Greece, Antiochus] laid a heavy yoke upon them.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Daniel 10:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/10:21:1)


**אֶת הָרָשׁוּם בִּכְתָב אֱמֶת.** שְׁטַר הַגְּזֵרָה וֶאֱמֶת הִיא. וְאֵין אֶחָד בְּכָל הַשָּׂרִים הָעֶלְיוֹנִים מִתְחַזֵּק לְעָזְרֵנִי עַל כָּל **אֵלֶּה כִּי אִם וְגוֹ׳**:


**what is inscribed in a true script** the document of the decree is true, but not one of the heavenly princes is exerting himself to assist me with all these, except etc.



## Chapter 11


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:1:1)


**וַאֲנִי בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי.** בִּנְפֹל מַלְכוּת בָּבֶל וְהִתְחִילָה מֶמְשֶׁלֶת מָדַי וּפָרַס, וְהָיוּ מְבַקְּשִׁים שָׂרֵי מָדַי וּפָרַס מֵאֵת הַמָּקוֹם לְהַכְבִּיד עֹל מֶמְשֶׁלֶת אֵימָתָם עֲלֵיכֶם, אֲנִי גַבְרִיאֵל עָמַדְתִּי לְמַחֲזִיק וּלְמָעוֹז לְמִיכָאֵל שַׂרְכֶם:


**As for me, in the first year of Darius the Mede** when the kingdom of Babylon fell, and the rule of Media and Persia commenced, and the princes of Media and Persia entreated the Omnipresent to make the yoke of their frightful rule heavy upon you, I, Gabriel, stood as a supporter and as a stronghold for Michael, your prince.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:2:1)


**הִנֵּה עוֹד שְׁלֹשָׁה מְלָכִים וְגוֹ׳**. רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה אָמְרוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (פרק כח): זֶה כּוֹרֶשׁ וַאֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ וְדָרְיָוֶשׁ שֶׁבָּנָה הַבַּיִת, וּמַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר **רְבִיעִי**? רְבִיעִי לְמָדַי. אֲבָל בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסֵף בֶּן גּוּרְיוֹן כָּתוּב שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ בֵּן לְכוֹרֶשׁ שֶׁמָּלַךְ תַּחְתָּיו לִפְנֵי מְלוֹךְ אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ וּשְׁמוֹ בַּמְבִּישָׁה:


**Behold three more kings** Our Sages of blessed memory in Seder Olam (ch. 28) said: “This refers to Cyrus, Ahasuerus, and Darius who rebuilt the Temple. Now what is the meaning of fourth’? The fourth, counting from Media.” In the book of Joseph Ben Gorion, however, it is written that Cyrus had a son who succeeded him before the reign of Ahasuerus, named Cambyses.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:2:2)


**וּכְחֶזְקָתוֹ.** שֶׁל דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**and when he becomes strong** i.e., Darius.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:2:3)


**בְּעָשְׁרוֹ יָעִיר.** אֶת כָּל מַלְכוּתוֹ לְהִלָּחֵם עִם מַלְכוּת יָוָן:


**with his wealth, he will arouse** his entire kingdom to wage war against the kingdom of the heathens.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:3:1)


**וְעָמַד מֶלֶךְ גִּבּוֹר.** בְּיָוָן הוּא אֲלֶכְּסַנְדְּרוֹס מוּקְדוֹן:


**And a mighty king will arise** in Greece, viz. Alexander of Macedon.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:3:2)


**וְעָשָׂה כִּרְצוֹנוֹ.** בְּדָרְיָוֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס וְיַהַרְגֵהוּ וִיקַבֵּל מַלְכוּתוֹ, וְיִשְׁתַּעְבְּדוּ פַרְסִיִּים לַיְּוָנִים׃


**and do according to his will** with Darius the king of Persia, and he will slay him and receive his kingdom, and the Persians will be enslaved by the heathens [the Greeks.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:1)


**וּכְעָמְדוֹ תִּשָּׁבֵר מַלְכוּתוֹ.** וּכְשֶׁיִּתְחַזֵּק הַרְבֵּה וְיַעֲמֹד עַל חֶזְקָתוֹ, תִּשָּׁבֵר מַלְכוּתוֹ, שֶׁיָּמוּת:


**And when he arises, his kingdom will be broken** When he becomes very strong and reaches the height of his strength, his kingdom will be broken, meaning that he will die.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:2)


**וְתֵחָץ לְאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת הַשָּׁמָיִם.** כָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר בֶּן גּוּרְיוֹן שֶׁחָלַק מַלְכוּתוֹ לְאַרְבָּעָה רָאשִׁים מִמִּשְׁפַּחְתּוֹ, הֵם אַרְבָּעָה רָאשֵׁי נָמֵר שֶׁרָאָה דָנִיֵּאל ״וַאֲרוּ חֵיוָא אָחֳרִי כִּנְמַר״ (דניאל ז:ו) ״וְלַהּ גַּפִּין אַרְבַּע דִּי עוֹף עַל גַּבַּהּ וְאַרְבְּעָה רֵאשִׁין לְחֵיוְתָא״, הִמְשִׁיל זֶה בַּמִּזְרָח וְזֶה בַּמַּעֲרָב זֶה בַּצָּפוֹן וְזֶה בַּדָּרוֹם. וְכֵן בַּחִזָּיוֹן הָרִאשׁוֹן ״וַתַּעֲלֶינָה חָזוּת אַרְבַּע תַּחְתֶּיהָ״ (דניאל ח:ח) בְּקַרְנוֹ שֶׁל צְפִיר הַשָּׂעִיר:


**and it will be divided to the four directions of the heavens** It is written in the book of Ben Gorion (ch. 14) that he divided his kingdom between the four heads of his family; they are the four heads of the leopard that Daniel saw (above 7:6): “And behold another one, like a leopard, and it had four wings of a bird on its back, and the beast had four heads.” He gave this one dominion in the east, this one in the west, this one in the north and this one in the south, and so in the first vision (8:8): “an appearance of four sprouted in its stead,” concerning the horns of the he-goat.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:3)


**וְלֹא לְאַחֲרִיתוֹ.** וְלֹא תָבֹא הַמֶּמְשָׁלָה לְבָנָיו, אֶלָּא לִבְנֵי מִשְׁפַּחְתּוֹ. אַחֲרִיתוֹ אֵינוֹ נוֹפֵל אֶלָּא עַל לְשׁוֹן בָּנִים, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (עמוס ד:ב) ״וְנִשָּׂא אֶתְכֶם בְּצִנּוֹת וְאַחֲרִיתְכֶם בְּסִירוֹת דּוּגָה״, וְתִרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּבְנֵיכוֹן וּבְנָתֵיכוֹן בְּדוּגִית צַיָּדִין״:


**but not to his posterity** The dominion will not come to his sons but to his family members. [The word] אַחֲרִיתוֹ coincides only with the expression of sons, and so Scripture states (Amos 4:2): “and you shall be borne on shields and your posterity (אַחֲרִיתְכֶן) in fishing boats,” which Jonathan renders: and your sons (sic) and daughters in fishermen’s boats.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:4)


**וְלֹא כְמָשְׁלוֹ אֲשֶׁר מָשָׁל.** וְלֹא תְהֵא מַלְכוּת שֶׁל אֵלּוּ חֲזָקָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁל אֲלֶכְסַנְדְּרוֹס:


**and not like the dominion that he ruled** but the kingdom of these will not be as strong as that of Alexander.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:4:5)


**כִּי תִנָּתֵשׁ מַלְכוּתוֹ.** לְחַלֵּק לְאַרְבַּע רָאשִׁים הַלָּלוּ וְלַאֲחֵרִים מִלְּבַד אֵלֶּה:


**for his kingdom will be uprooted** to divide to these four heads and to others besides these.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:5:1)


**וְיֶחֱזַק מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב.** הָרֹאשׁ אֲשֶׁר יַמְלִיךְ בַּנֶּגֶב יֶחֱזַק מִן הָרֹאשׁ שֶׁכְּנֶגְדּוֹ הַמּוֹלֵךְ בַּצָּפוֹן וּמִן שָׂרָיו:


**And the king of the south will overwhelm** The head who will reign in the south will be stronger than the head opposite him who reigns in the north, and [stronger] than his officers.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:6:1)


**לַעֲשׂוֹת מֵישָׁרִים.** פְּשָׁרַת שָׁלוֹם בֵּינוֹ וּבֵין אָבִיהָ:


**to make a compromise** a compromise of peace between him and her father.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:6:2)


**וְלֹא תַעְצֹר וְגוֹ׳.** וְיִלְכְּדֶנָּה מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן וְאֶת מְבִיאֶיהָ וְאֶת אָבִיהָ הַיּוֹלְדָהּ וּמַחֲזִיקָהּ בְּכָל עֵת צָרָתָהּ:


**will not retain, etc.** The king of the north will capture her, those who brought her, and her father who begot her and supported her in her time of trouble.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:6:3)


**וְלֹא תַעְצֹר.** זְרוֹעַ מְבִיאֶיהָ כֹּחַ לַעֲמֹד (וְלֹא יַעֲמֹד אָבִיהָ) לְפָנָיו, לֹא הוּא וְלֹא זְרוֹעוֹ הֵם גִּבּוֹרֵי כֹחוֹ:


**will not retain** The arm of those who brought her [will not retain] strength to stand, [and her father will not prevail] before him, neither he nor his arm, meaning his mighty men.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:6:4)


**וְתִנָּתֵן.** בְּיַד מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן: **הִיא וּמְבִיאֶיהָ וְהַיֹּלְדָהּ.** הוּא אָבִיהָ:


**will be surrendered** into the hands of the king of the north, **she and those who brought her, and he who begot her**, i.e., her father.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:1)


**וְעָמַד מִנֵּצֶר שָׁרָשֶׁיהָ.** בֵּן יוֹשֵׁב עַל כַּנּוֹ בְּכִסֵּא הַמְּלוּכָה:


**A scion of her roots will arise** a son sitting on his position, on the throne of the kingdom.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:2)


**וַיָּבֹא אֶל הַחַיִל.** אֶל מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן:


**and he will come to the army** to the king of the north.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:3)


**בִּמְעוֹז מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן.** כְּלוֹמַר: בְּעָרֵי מָעוּזּוֹ בְּמִבְצָרָיו:


**into the stronghold of the king of the north** i.e., in the cities of his strength, in his fortresses.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:4)


**וְעָשָׂה בָהֶם.** וְהִצְלִיחַ בָּהֶם:


**and he will succeed in them** Heb. וְעָשָׂה בָהֶם, lit. and he will succeed against them.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:7:5)


**וְהֶחֱזִיק.** וְיִכְבּוֹשׁ אוֹתָם:


**and take hold** and he will conquer them.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:8:1)


**נְסִיכֵיהֶם.** שָׂרֵיהֶם:


**their princes** [as translated,] their princes.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:9:1)


**וּבָא בְּמַלְכוּת מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב.** דֶּרֶךְ מַלְכוּת מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב שֶׁהִיא אֲבִי אָבִיהָ יָשׁוּב אֶל אַדְמָתוֹ וְהִיא מִצְרַיִם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְמַעְלָה ״בַּשְּׁבִי יָבִיא מִצְרָיִם״ (דניאל יא:ח):


**And he will come into the kingdom of the king of the south** Through the kingdom of the king of the south, who was her father’s father, he will return to his land, which is Egypt, as is stated above: “will bring in captivity to Egypt.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:10:1)


**וּבָנָיו.** שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן יִתְגָּרוּ:


**And his sons** [i.e., the sons] of the king of the north will agitate.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:10:2)


**וּבָא בוֹא.** כְּמוֹ וְהוֹלֵךְ הָלוֹךְ. בְּנוֹ שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן יָבוֹא בוֹ וְשָׁטַף וְעָבַר אֶל אֶרֶץ הַנֶּגֶב:


**and he will come** Heb. וּבָא בוֹא, like וְהוֹלֵךְ הָלוֹךְ. The son of the king of the north will come and pass to the land of the south.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:10:3)


**וְיָשֹׁב וְיִתְגָּרֶה.** בְּמֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב:


**and he will return and agitate** against the king of the south.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:10:4)


**עַד מְעֻזּוֹ.** עִיר מִבְצָרָיו:


**until his stronghold** his fortified city.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:11:1)


**וַיִּתְמַרְמָר.** וַיִּלָּחֵם בָּעָם:


**will wage war** will fight against the people.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:11:2)


**וְנִתַּן הֶהָמוֹן.** שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן בְּיָדוֹ:


**and the multitude will be given into his hand** [i.e., the multitude] of the king of the north [will be given] into his hand.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:12:1)


**וְנִשָּׂא הֶהָמוֹן.** שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ צָפוֹן לְהִתְגָּאוֹת מְאֹד, כִּי יָזְמוּ בְּלִבָּם לְהִתְגַּבֵּר בַּמִּלְחָמָה:


**And the multitude will be raised up** that of the king of the north, to become very haughty, for they planned in their hearts to be victorious in the war.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:12:2)


**וְהִפִּיל רִבּוֹאוֹת.** רַבִּים בְּחֵיל מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב:


**and he will fell myriads** many of the army of the king of the south.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:12:3)


**וְלֹא יָעוֹז.** וּבְכָל זֹאת לֹא יִהְיֶה נִצָּחוֹן הַמִּלְחָמָה שֶׁלּוֹ:


**but he will not prevail** Nevertheless, the victory of the war will not be his.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:14:1)


**וּבְנֵי פָרִיצֵי עַמְּךָ יִנָּשְׂאוּ לְהַעֲמִיד חָזוֹן.** רָאִיתִי בְּשֵׁם רַב סְעַדְיָה שֶׁזּוֹ פְּרִיצֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְסִיעָתָם:


**and the sons of the renegades of your people will exalt themselves to bring about the vision** I saw in the name of Rav Saadia Gaon that they were the renegades of Israel and their company.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:15:1)


**וְעִם מִבְחָרָיו.** יִהְיוּ בַּמִּלְחָמָה:


**and the people of his chosen ones** will be in the war.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:15:2)


**וְאֵין כֹּחַ לַעֲמֹד.** מִפְּנֵי מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ כָּאן וָי״ו יְתֵרָה, דֶּרֶךְ מִקְרָא לְדַבֵּר כֵּן בְּלָשׁוֹן זֶה, כְּמוֹ ״חוֹרֶיהָ וְאֵין שָׁם מְלוּכָה יִקְרָאוּ״ (ישעיהו לד:יב):


**will have no strength to stand** before the king of the north, and although there is a superfluous “vav” here, it is customary for Scripture to speak in this manner as in, for example: “As for its nobles, there are none (וְאֵין) who proclaim the kingdom,” Isaiah (34:12).



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:16:1)


**וְיַעַשׂ.** הַמֶּלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן הַבָּא אֶל מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב כִּרְצוֹנוֹ:


**will do** The king of the north, who comes to the king of the south, [will do] as he wishes.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:16:2)


**בְּאֶרֶץ הַצְּבִי.** בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**in the land of beauty** in the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:16:3)


**וְכָלָה בְיָדוֹ.** וְכִלָּה אֶת הָאָרֶץ בְּאוּכְלוּסָיו:


**and he will destroy it** He will destroy the land with his army.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:1)


**וְיָשֵׂם פָּנָיו.** מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן לָבוֹא בְּתֹקֶף כָּל מַלְכוּתוֹ שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב:


**And he will set his face** i.e., the king of the north, to come into the strength of the entire kingdom of the king of the south.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:2)


**וִישָׁרִים עִמּוֹ.** עִם מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב, גַּם עֲלֵיהֶם יִלָּחֵם מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן בְּאוֹתָן הַיָּמִים:


**and the upright will be with him** and Israel will be with the king of the south. Also with them will the king of the north battle in those days.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:3)


**וְעָשָׂה.** וְהִצְלִיחַ׃


**and he will succeed** Heb. וְעָשָׂה, lit. and he will do.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:4)
