**וּבַת הַנָּשִׁים יִתֵּן לוֹ לְהַשְׁחִיתָהּ.** זוֹ כְּנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל ״הַיָּפָה בַּנָּשִׁים״ (שיר השירים א:ח), יְצַוֶּה מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן לְשַׂר צְבָאוֹ לְהַשְׁחִיתָהּ. וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי, שֶׁהוּא אַנְטִיוֹכוּס הָרָשָׁע מֶלֶךְ יָוָן, שֶׁגָּזַר גְּזֵרוֹת עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְצִוָּה לְפוֹלִיפוֹס שַׂר צְבָאוֹ לַהֲרֹג אֶת כָּל הַקּוֹרֵא שְׁמוֹ יְהוּדִי, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסִיפוֹן:


**Now the daughter of the women he will give him to destroy her** This is the nation of Israel, [referred to in Song of Songs 1:8 as] “the fairest of women.” The king of the north will command the general of his army to destroy her. I say that he is Antiochus, the king of Greece, who issued decrees against Israel, and he commanded his general, Phillip, to kill whoever identified himself as a Jew, as is written in the book of Josiphon (ch. 18).


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:5)


**וְלֹא תַעֲמֹד.** עֲצָתוֹ זֹאת:


**but it will not stand** this counsel of his.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:17:6)


**וְלֹא לוֹ תִהְיֶה.** בַּת הַנָּשִׁים שֶׁעָמַד מַתִּתְיָהוּ בֶּן יוֹחָנָן וּפָרַק עֻלּוֹ מֵעַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and she will not be for him** the daughter of the women, for Mattathias the son of Johanan will rise and break off his [Antiochus’s] yoke from Israel.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:18:1)


**וְהִשְׁבִּית קָצִין חֶרְפָּתוֹ לוֹ.** וְהִשְׁבִּית הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֶת גִּדּוּפוֹ וְאֶת חֶרְפָּתוֹ שֶׁהָיָה מְחָרֵף אֶת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְאֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסִיפוֹן; וְהֵשִׁיבוֹ עָלָיו שֶׁהִכָּהוּ בִּשְׁחִין רַע בְּעוֹדוֹ בַדֶּרֶךְ שֶׁהָלַךְ לָצוּר עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם וְהִסְרִיחַ בְּשָׂרוֹ וְנָשְׁרוּ אֵבָרָיו, וְאָמַר לַעֲבָדָיו לַהֲשִׁיבוֹ לְאַנְטוֹכְיָא, וְלֹא הִסְפִּיק לָבוֹא שָׁם עַד שֶׁמֵּת בְּחוֹלָיִים רָעִים:


**and the Prince will terminate his blasphemy to Him** His reproach and his blasphemy, with which he blasphemed the Holy One, blessed be He, and Israel, as is written in the book of Josiphon (ch. 20), and He punished him, for He smote him with evil boils while he was in transit, for he went to besiege Jerusalem, and his flesh became putrid, his limbs fell off and he ordered his slaves to bring him back to Antioch, but he did not manage to get there before he died of evil illnesses.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:19:1)


**לְמָעוּזֵּי אַרְצוֹ.** לָשׁוּב אֶל עִיר מִבְצָרָיו:


**to the strongholds of his land** to return to his fortified city.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:20:1)


**וְעָמַד עַל כַּנּוֹ מַעֲבִיר נוֹגֵשׂ.** וְיִתְחַזֵּק עַל בְּסִיסוֹ בְּהַר הַמּוֹדְעִית מַתִּתְיָהוּ בֶּן יוֹחָנָן הַמַּעֲבִיר נוֹגֵשׂ יָוָן מֵעַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ׳, וְהוּא **הֲדַר מַלְכוּת** בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, כִּי שַׂר וְגִבּוֹר יִהְיֶה הוּא וְכָל זַרְעוֹ אַחֲרָיו, בְּנֵי הַחַשְׁמוֹנִים:


**And one who removes the oppressor will stand on his base** and Mattathias the son of Johanan, who removed the oppressor from Israel, and who is the glory of the kingdom of Israel, will strengthen himself on his base on Mt. Modin, for he will be a prince and a mighty man, he and all his descendants after him, viz. the Hasmoneans.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:20:2)


**וּבְיָמִים אֲחָדִים יִשָּׁבֵר.** בְּיָמִים מוּעָטִים תִּשָּׁבֵר מַלְכוּתָם:


**in a few days it will be broken** In a few days, their kingdom will be broken.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:20:3)


**וְלֹא בְאַפַּיִם.** שֶׁל אֻמָּה אַחֶרֶת:


**but not with anger** of another nation.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:20:4)


**וְלֹא בְמִלְחָמָה.** כִּי מֵהֶם וּבָהֶם אֲשֶׁר יְקַנְּאוּ אֲרִיסְתוֹבְלוֹס וְהוֹרְקְנוֹס עַל דְּבַר הַמְּלוּכָה:


**and not with war** but from themselves and with themselves, that Hyrcanus and Aristobulus will be jealous over the throne.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:21:1)


**וְעָמַד עַל כַּנּוֹ נִבְזֶה.** וְיִתְחַזֵּק אָז עַל מַעֲמָדָהּ מַלְכוּת עֵשָׂו, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״בָּזוּי אַתָּה מְאֹד״ (עובדיה א:ב), וְיַעַמְדוּ רוֹמִים וְיִטְּלוּ מַלְכוּת מִיְּוָנִים:


**And a contemptible person will stand on his base** Then the kingdom of Rome will strengthen itself on its stand, as it says (Obad. 1:2): “you are very despised,” and the Romans will rise and take the kingdom from the Greeks.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:22:1)


**וּזְרֹעוֹת הַשֶּׁטֶף.** וְגִבּוֹרֵי מַלְכוּת יָוָן שֶׁהָיוּ מִלְּפָנֵיהֶם שׁוֹטְפִים וַחֲזָקִים יִשָּׁטְפוּ מִלִּפְנֵי רוֹמִיִּים וְיִשָּׁבְרוּ:


**And the arms of inundation** And the mighty of the kingdom, who previously were inundating and powerful, will be inundated before the Romans and be broken.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:22:2)


**וְגַם נְגִיד בְּרִית.** גַּם מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁיִּכְרֹת עִמָּם **בְּרִית** גַּם הוּא סוֹפוֹ שֶׁיִּשָּׁטֵף מִלְּפָנָיו, כִּי יַעַבְרוּ (רוֹמִים) עַל הַבְּרִית וְיִפְשְׁטוּ עֲלֵיהֶם. כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (עבודה זרה ח:): עֶשְׂרִין וְשִׁית שְׁנִין קָמוּ בְּהֵמָנוּתַיְיהוּ בַּהֲדֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, וַהֲדַר אִשְׁתַּעְבְּדוּ בְּהוֹן:


**and also the king with whom they made a covenant** Also the king of Israel, who will form a treaty with them, will ultimately be inundated from before him, for [the Romans] will violate the treaty and betray them, as our Sages of blessed memory said: “Twenty-six years they kept their trust with Israel, but later they subjugated them.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:23:1)


**וּמִן הִתְחַבְּרוּת אֵלָיו יַעֲשֶׂה מִרְמָה.** וּמִן הַשּׁוּתָפוּת שֶׁיִּשְׁתַּתֵּף (רוֹמִי) עִם יִשְׂרָאֵל יַעֲשֶׂה מִרְמָה, שֶׁלֹּא יְגַלֶּה מַחֲשָׁבָה רָעָה:


**And from the alliance with him he will work deceitfully** And from the alliance that Rome will make with Israel, he will work deceitfully, for he will not reveal his evil plan.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:23:2)


**וְעָלָה.** מִמְּקוֹמוֹ:


**and he will go up** from his place.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:23:3)


**וְעָצַם.** בְּכָל מְקוֹמוֹת שֶׁסְּבִיבוֹת אֶרֶץ יְהוּדָה, בֶּאֱדוֹם, בְּעַמּוֹן וּמוֹאָב:


**and overpower** in all places around the land of Judea: in Edom, in Ammon, and in Moab.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:23:4)


**בִּמְעַט גּוֹי.** לֹא יִצְטָרֵךְ לְחַיִל כָּבֵד, כִּי יַעֲזֹר אוֹתוֹ מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה. וְכֵן כָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסִיפוֹן:


**with few people** He will not require a massive army, for the king of Judea will aid him, and so it is written in the book of Josiphon (ch. 23).



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:24:1)


**בִּזָּה וְשָׁלָל וּרְכוּשׁ לָהֶם יְבַזֵּר.** יְחַלֵּק לְכָל הַמְקַבְּלִים עֻלּוֹ עֲלֵיהֶם, עַד שֶׁיִּכְבְּשׁוּ אֶת הַכֹּל בַּחֲנֻפָּה וּבַחֲלַקְלַקּוֹת:


**plunder and spoils and belongings he will distribute to them** He will distribute to those who accept their yoke upon themselves until they conquer all with flattery and smooth speech.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:24:2)


**יִבְזוֹר.** כְּמוֹ יְפַזֵּר, וְכֵן ״בִּזַּר עַמִּים״ (תהלים סח:לא):


**will distribute** Heb. יִבְזוֹר, like יִפְזוֹר. and so (Ps. 68:31): “he scatters (בִּזַר) peoples.”


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:24:3)


**וְעַל מִבְצָרִים.** עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת **יְחַשֵּׁב מַחְשְׁבוֹתָיו** לְהוֹשִׁיב בָּהֶם רָאשֵׁי גְּיָסוֹתָיו, **עַד עֵת** שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַכֹּל כָּבוּשׁ תַּחְתֵּיהֶם:


**and about the fortresses** of the nations he will devise in his plans—to station [in them] the heads of his troops until the time that all will be conquered under them.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:25:1)


**וְלֹא יַעֲמֹד.** מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב:


**and he will not stand** the king of the south.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:25:2)


**כִּי יַחְשְׁבוּ עָלָיו מַחֲשָׁבוֹת.** וְיִתְנַכְּלוּ עָלָיו לְהַפִּילוֹ עַל יְדֵי שֹׁחַד, שֶׁיַּשְׁחִידוּ אֶת שָׂרָיו לִבְגֹּד בּוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא מְסַיֵּם וְאוֹמֵר **וְאֹכְלֵי פַת בָּגוֹ יִשְׁבְּרוּהוּ**:


**because they will devise plans against him** They will plot against him to fell him through the bribery that they will bribe his officers to betray him, as he concludes: “and those who eat his food will break him.”



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:26:1)


**פַּת בָּגוֹ.** מַאֲכָלוֹ נוֹטֵל פְּרָס מִשֻּׁלְחָנוֹ:


**his food** Heb. פַּת־בָּגוֹ, his food; one who takes a portion from his table.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:1)


**וּשְׁנֵיהֶם הַמְּלָכִים.** שַׂר רוֹמִים וְנָגִיד הַבְּרִית הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה (דניאל יא:כב), הוּא הוֹרְקְנוֹס שֶׁיִּתְחַבֵּר לְעֶזְרָתוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּעֲזֹר גַּם הוּא עַל אַרְסְתּוֹבְּלוֹס אָחִיו הַמּוֹלֵךְ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**As for the two kings** the officer of the Romans and the king with whom they made to covenant, which is mentioned above. That is Hyrcanus, who will form an alliance to aid him so that he, too, will aid him against Aristobulus his brother, ruling in Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:2)


**לְבָבָם לְמֵרָע.** עַל בְּנֵי עַמְּךָ לְהָרַע לִיהוּדָה:


**their hearts are to do evil** to the people of your nation, to harm Judea.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:3)


**וְעַל שֻׁלְחָן אֶחָד.** עַל מַלְכוּת אַרְסְתּוֹבְּלוֹס יִתְלַחֲשׁוּ כְּזָבִים:


**and on one table** They will murmur lies about the kingdom of Aristobulus.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:4)


**וְלֹא תִצְלָח.** שֶׁיִּתְמַסְּרוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְהַשְׁחָתָה בְּאוֹתָן הַיָּמִים:


**but it will not succeed** for Israel will not be surrendered to destruction in those days.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:27:5)


**כִּי עוֹד קֵץ לַמּוֹעֵד.** הַחוּרְבָּן לִמְלֹאות שָׁבוּעִים שִׁשִּׁים וּשְׁנַיִם הָאֲמוּרִים בְּסֵפֶר זֶה (דניאל ט:כה-כו), וְאוֹתוֹ הַמּוֹעֵד בִּימֵי אַגְרִיפַּס בֶּן אַגְרִיפַּס מִזֶּרַע הוֹרְדוֹס:


**for there is still an end to the appointed time** of the destruction, at the end of sixty two weeks [of years], stated in this Book (9:25), and that appointed time is in the days of Agrippa, the son of Agrippa of the seed of Herod.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:28:1)


**וְיָשֹׁב.** מֶלֶךְ הָרוֹמִי לְאַרְצוֹ מֵעַל מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב **בִּרְכוּשׁ גָּדוֹל**:


**And he will return** The Roman king [will return] to his land from [fighting] with the king of the south with many possessions.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:28:2)


**וּלְבָבוֹ עַל בְּרִית קֹדֶשׁ.** לְהָפֵר בְּרִיתוֹ מֵעַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and his heart is on the covenant with the holy ones** to abrogate his treaty with Israel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:28:3)


**וְעָשָׂה.** כְּלוֹמַר: וְהִצְלִיחַ, וְשָׁב לְאַרְצוֹ:


**and he will succeed** Heb. וְעָשָׂה, lit. and he will do, i.e., and he will succeed and return to his land.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:29:1)


**לַמּוֹעֵד.** לְאַחַר זְמַן:


**After a time** Heb. לַמּוֹעֵד, after a time.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:29:2)


**וְלֹא תִהְיֶה.** הַבִּיאָה הַזֹּאת מֻצְלַחַת **כָּרִאשׁוֹנָה** הָאֲמוּרָה לְמַעְלָה ״וְעָמַד מִנֵּצֶר שָׁרָשֶׁיהָ וְגוֹ׳״ (דניאל יא:ז), שֶׁהִצְלִיחַ מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן עַל מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב, **וְכָאַחֲרוֹנָה** פַּעַם שְׁנִיָּה זוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ **וְשָׁב לְאַרְצוֹ בִּרְכוּשׁ גָּדוֹל**:


but this coming **will not be** as successful as the first one mentioned above (verse 7): “A scion of her roots will arise etc.,” in which the king of the north succeeded over the king of the south, or like the last, meaning this second time, about which we said: “and he will return to his land” with many possessions.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:1)


**וּבָאוּ בוֹ.** לְהִלָּחֵם בּוֹ:


**will come upon him** to battle with him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:2)


**צִיִּים.** סִיעוֹת:


**companies** Heb. צִיִים.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:3)


**כִּתִּים.** רוֹמִים, גְּדוּדִים מִמַּלְכוּת רוֹמִי שֶׁיִּמְרְדוּ בּוֹ:


**Kittites** Romans, troops from the kingdom of Rome who will rebel against him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:4)


**וְנִכְאֶה.** וְנִשְׁבָּר קְצָת:


**and he will be crushed** He will be slightly broken.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:5)


**וְשָׁב וְזָעַם עַל בְּרִית קֹדֶשׁ.** וְשָׁב לְאַרְצוֹ לְהָפֵר בְּרִית שֶׁכָּרַת עִם יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and he will return and be wroth with the holy covenant** And he will return to his land and abrogate the treaty he had made with Israel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:30:6)


**וְשָׁב וְיָבֵן עַל עוֹזְבֵי בְּרִית קֹדֶשׁ.** וְיִתֵּן לֵב וְיִתְבּוֹנֵן שֶׁעָזְבוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּרִית קֹדֶשׁ, וְתִרְבֶּה שִׂנְאַת חִנָּם וּמַחֲלֹקֶת בְּבַיִת שֵׁנִי, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסִיפוֹן, וְיִשְׁפְּכוּ דָּם נָקִי, וְעַל זֹאת יִבְטַח וְיֵדַע שֶׁיַּצְלִיחַ, וְיָפֵר אֶת בְּרִיתוֹ מֵעִמָּם וְיִתְגָּרֶה בָּם:


**and he will return and contemplate those who abandoned the holy covenant** He will put his mind to it and contemplate that Israel has abandoned the holy covenant (and law), and unwarranted hatred and controversy will increase in the Second Temple [era], as is written in the book of Josiphon (ch. 45), and they will shed innocent blood, and he will rely on this and know that he will succeed, and he will abrogate his treaty with them and provoke them.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:31:1)


**וּזְרֹעִים מִמֶּנּוּ יַעֲמֹדוּ.** יִשְׁלַח שָׂרָיו וְגִבּוֹרָיו לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**And arms from him will stand** He will send his officers and his mighty men to Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:31:2)


**וְחִלְּלוּ הַמִּקְדָּשׁ הַמָּעוֹז.** מְעוֹן בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**and they will profane** the stronghold of the Temple.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:32:1)


**וּמַרְשִׁיעֵי בְרִית יַחֲנִיף.** פְּרִיצֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁיִּתְחַבְּרוּ עִמּוֹ וְיַרְשִׁיעוּ בְּרִיתָם עִם חַבְרֵיהֶם וְיִהְיוּ מַמְרִים, **יַחֲנִיף** בַּחֲלַקְלַקּוֹת:


**And those who deal wickedly against the covenant** The renegades of Israel, who will join him and deal wickedly against their covenant with their fellows, he will flatter with smooth speech.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:32:2)


**יוֹדְעֵי אֱלֹהָיו.** וּכְשֵׁרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁיַּחֲזִיקוּ בְּיִרְאַת אֱלֹהֵיהֶם, כְּלוֹמַר: יֹאחֲזוּ בָהּ וְלֹא יַעַזְבוּהָ:


**but the people that knows its God** but the devout of Israel, who will adhere to the fear of their God will grasp it and not abandon it.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:32:3)


**וְעָשׂוּ.** אֶת תּוֹרָתָם:


**and perform** their Torah.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:33:1)


**וּמַשְׂכִּילֵי עָם.** חֲכָמִים שֶׁבָּהֶם, כְּגוֹן שֶׁל בֵּית רַבִּי וְחַכְמֵי הַדּוֹרוֹת:


**And the wise of the people** the wise among them, such as the household of Rabbi [Judah Hanasi] and the sages of the generations.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:33:2)


**יָבִינוּ לָרַבִּים.** יִדְרְשׁוּ לְעַם הָאָרֶץ אֶת הַתּוֹרָה וִיאַמְּצוּ יְדֵיהֶם לְהַחֲזִיק בָּהּ:


**will allow the public to understand** They will preach the Torah to the common people and encourage them to adhere to it.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:33:3)


**וְנִכְשְׁלוּ.** בְּגָלוּתָם **בְּחֶרֶב וּבְלֶהָבָה וְגוֹ׳**:


**will stumble** in their exile by the sword and by flames, etc.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:34:1)


**יֵעָזְרוּ.** עֵזֶר מְעַט עַל יְדֵי שֹׁחַד וּמָמוֹן שֶׁיַּעַבְדוּ אֶת אוֹיְבֵיהֶם יְסַיֵּעַ אוֹתָם בָּהֶם:


**they will be helped with a little help** Through bribes and the money that they will pay tribute to their enemies, they will be helped.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:35:1)


**יִכָּשְׁלוּ לִצְרוֹף בָּהֶם.** יִכָּשְׁלוּ בְּחֶשְׁבּוֹן הַקִּצִּים, שֶׁיִּתְּנוּ לֵב לְצָרְפָן וּלְלַבְּנָם לָדַעַת אוֹתָם, וְיִטְעוּ בָּהֶם:


**will stumble to clarify some of them** They will stumble in the calculations of the end, for they will put their mind to refine them and to resolve them to know them, but they will err concerning them.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:36:1)


**וְעָשָׂה כִרְצוֹנוֹ הַמֶּלֶךְ.** מַלְכוּת רוֹמִי:


**And the king will do as he wishes** the kingdom of Rome.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:36:2)


**וְהִצְלִיחַ עַד כָּלָה זַעַם.** עַד שׁוּב אַף הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל:


**and he will succeed until the fury is spent** until the wrath of the Holy One, blessed be He, returns from Israel.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:36:3)


**כִּי נֶחֱרָצָה נֶעֱשָׂתָה.** כְּשֶׁתִּכְלֶה גְּזֵרָתוֹ. **כִּי** כְּמוֹ כַּאֲשֶׁר:


**when it will be finished and executed** when His decree will be finished. כִּי is like כַּאֲשֶׁר, when.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:37:1)


**וְעַל אֱלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתָיו לֹא יָבִין.** לֹא יִתֵּן לֵב לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהוּא אֱלֹהֵי אַבְרָהָם יִצְחָק וְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲבוֹתָיו:


**And he will not contemplate the God of his fathers** he will not put his mind to the Holy One, blessed be He, Who is the God of Abraham, Isaac, and Israel, his [i.e., Israel’s] forefathers.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:37:2)


**חֶמְדַּת נָשִׁים.** כְּנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל הַיָּפָה בַּנָּשִׁים:


**the most desirable of women** the nation of Israel, [called] fairest of women.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:38:1)


**וְלֶאֱלוֹהַּ מָעֻזִּים.** לֵאלֹהֵי הַכְּרוּבִים אֲשֶׁר יִכְרְתוּ עִמּוֹ בְּרִית:


**But the god of the strongholds** the god of the cherubim who will make a treaty with him.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:38:2)


**עַל כַּנּוֹ יְכַבֵּד.** כִּי יַחֲנִיף אֶת הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת לִהְיוֹתָם כְּבוּשִׁים לוֹ:


**on its base he will honor** for he will flatter the nations to be subservient to him.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:1)


**וְעָשָׂה.** בִּנְיָנִים:


**And he will construct** buildings.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:2)


**לְמִבְצְרֵי מָעֻזִּים עִם אֱלוֹהַּ נֵכָר.** כְּלוֹמַר: לִכְבוֹד אֱלֹהֵי נֵכָר. אֲשֶׁר הִכִּיר:


**for the fortresses of the strongholds with a foreign god** i.e., in honor of a foreign god.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:3)


**יַרְבֶּה כָבוֹד.** אֶת הַשָּׂרִים אֲשֶׁר יִרְאֶה בַּלֵּב לְהַכִּיר פְּנֵיהֶם וּלְהַחֲנִיף יַרְבֶּה לָהֶם כָבוֹד:


**whomever he will recognize, he will honor increasingly** He will increase the honor [and greatness] of those princes whom he will see fit to recognize their faces and to flatter.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:4)


**וַאֲדָמָה יְחַלֵּק.** לָהֶם׃


**and he will apportion** to them.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:39:5)


**בִּמְחִיר.** בְּדָמִים קַלִּים׃


**for a price** for little money.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Daniel 11:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:40:1)


**וּבְעֵת קֵץ.** כְּשֶׁתִּקְרַב גְּאֻלָּתֵינוּ:


**And at the time of the end** when our redemption draws near.


###### Rashi on Daniel 11:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/11:40:2)


**וְיִשְׂתָּעֵר עָלָיו מֶלֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן.** עַל מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב:


**and the king of the north will storm over him** over the king of the south.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:1:1)


**וּבָעֵת הַהִיא יַעֲמֹד מִיכָאֵל.** יִשְׁתַּתֵּק כְּאִלֵּם, שֶׁיִּרְאֶה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא דָּן בְּעַצְמוֹ וְאוֹמֵר: הֵיאַךְ אֲאַבֵּד אֻמָּה גְּדוֹלָה כָּזוֹ בִּשְׁבִיל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**Now at that time, Michael...will be silent** He will be silent like a mute person, for he will see the Holy One, blessed be He, judging by Himself and saying, “How will I destroy a great nation like this for the sake of Israel?”


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:1:2)


**וְהָיְתָה עֵת צָרָה.** בְּפַמַלְיָא שֶׁל מַעְלָה, וְקָטֵיגוֹרְיָא בְּתַלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים: בָּזוּזֵי וּבָזוּזֵי דְּבָזוּזֵי, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בַּהַגָּדָה בְּפֶרֶק אַחֲרוֹן שֶׁל כְּתֻבּוֹת (כתובות קיב:):


**and it will be a time of distress** in heaven there will be accusations against Torah scholars, [and there will be] plunderers and plunderers of plunderers, as our Rabbis said in the Aggadah in the last chapter of Kethuboth (112b).


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:1:3)


**יִמָּלֵט עַמְּךָ.** תֹּאבַד מַלְכוּת אֲרָם, וְיִמָּלְטוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**your people will escape** The kingdom of Gog will be destroyed and Israel will escape.


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:1:4)


**כָּל הַנִּמְצָא כָתוּב בַּסֵּפֶר.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא קָצָר: כָּל הַנִּמְצָא כָתוּב בַּסֵּפֶר זֶה, עַל יְדֵי הַחֲלוֹמוֹת הַכְּתוּבִים בּוֹ, ״עַד דִּקְטִילַת חֵיוְתָא וִיקַבְּלוּן מַלְכוּתָא קַדִּישֵׁי עֶלְיוֹנִין״ (דניאל ז:יא,יח), הַכֹּל יִתְקַיֵּם:


**everyone who is found inscribed in the book** This is a short verse, [meaning] whoever is found inscribed in this Book, through the dreams inscribed in it (7: 11): “until the beast is slain”; (verse 18): “and the high holy ones will receive the kingdom.” All will be fulfilled.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:2:1)


**וְרַבִּים מִיְּשֵׁנֵי אַדְמַת עָפָר יָקִיצוּ.** יִחְיוּ הַמֵּתִים:


**And many who sleep in the dust of the earth will awaken** The dead will come to life.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:3:1)


**וְהַמַּשְׂכִּילִים.** שֶׁעָסְקוּ בַּתּוֹרָה וּבַמִּצְווֹת **יַזְהִירוּ כְּזֹהַר הָרָקִיעַ**:


**And the wise** who engaged in the Torah and in the commandments, will shine like the brightness of the sky.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:7:1)


**וּכְכַלּוֹת נַפֵּץ יַד עַם קֹדֶשׁ.** כְּשֶׁתִּכְלֶה כֹּחַ יִשְׂרָאֵל, כִּי ״אָזְלַת יָד וְאֶפֶס עָצוּר וְעָזוּב״ (דברים לב:לו):


**and when they have ended shattering the strength of the holy people** Heb. נַפֵּץ יַד, lit. shattering the hand. When Israel’s strength terminates, [similar to] (Deut. 32:36): “that power has vanished (אָזְלַת יַד) and nothing left to keep or abandon.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:8:1)


**וְלֹא אָבִין.** לֹא יָדַעְתִּי מַהוּ **מוֹעֵד מוֹעֲדִים וָחֵצִי**:


**but I did not understand** I did not know what “the time of two times and a half” means.


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:8:2)


**מָה אַחֲרִית אֵלֶּה.** סוֹף הַחֶשְׁבּוֹנוֹת הַלָּלוּ:


**what is the end of these** the end of these calculations.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:9:1)


**עַד עֵת קֵץ.** עַד אֲשֶׁר תִּקְרַב הַגְּאֻלָּה:


**until the time of the end** until our redemption draws near.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:1)


**יִתְבָּרֲרוּ וְיִתְלַבְּנוּ.** הַחֶשְׁבּוֹנוֹת הָאֵלֶּה:


**They will be clarified and whitened** [i.e.,] these calculations.


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:2)


**וְיִצָּרְפוּ.** בָּהֶם רַבִּים לַהֲבִינָם:


**and...will be purified** [i.e.,] many [will be purified] by them to understand them.


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:3)


**וְהִרְשִׁיעוּ רְשָׁעִים.** אֶת הַחֶשְׁבּוֹנוֹת, לְחַשְּׁבָם שֶׁלֹּא כַּמִּשְׁפָּט, וּכְשֶׁיִּכְלוּ יֹאמְרוּ: אֵין עוֹד גְּאֻלָּה:


**and the wicked will pervert** the calculations by computing them incorrectly, and when they terminate, they will say that there is no more redemption.


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:4)


**וְלֹא יָבִינוּ.** אוֹתָם כָּל רְשָׁעִים:


**will not understand** All the wicked [will not understand] them.


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:10:5)


**וְהַמַּשְׂכִּילִים יָבִינוּ.** אוֹתָהּ בְּ**בֹא עֵת קֵץ**:


**but the wise will understand** them when the time of the end arrives.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:11:1)


**וּמֵעֵת הוּסַר הַתָּמִיד.** כְּדֵי לָתֵת שִׁקּוּץ מְשׁוֹמֵם תַּחְתָּיו. יָמִים אֶלֶף וּמָאתַיִם וְתִשְׁעִים שָׁנָה הֵן מִיּוֹם הוּסַר הַתָּמִיד עַד שֶׁיָּשׁוּב בִּימֵי מְשִׁיחֵנוּ. וְחֶשְׁבּוֹן זֶה מְכֻוָּן עִם חֶשְׁבּוֹן ״עֶרֶב בֹּקֶר אַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת״ (דניאל ח:יד) מִיּוֹם גָּלוּתָם לְמִצְרַיִם עַד גְּאֻלָּה אַחֲרוֹנָה: גָּלוּת מִצְרַיִם מָאתַיִם וְעֶשֶׂר, מִיּוֹם צֵאתָם עַד בִּנְיַן בַּיִת רִאשׁוֹן אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וּשְׁמוֹנִים, יְמֵי הַבִּנְיָן אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְעֶשֶׂר, גָּלוּת בָּבֶל שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה, יְמֵי בַיִת שֵׁנִי אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְעֶשְׂרִים, הֲרֵי אֶלֶף וַחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת וְתִשְׁעִים, וְהַתָּמִיד הוּסַר לִפְנֵי הַחֻרְבָּן שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים הֲרֵי מִיּוֹם גָּלוּתָם לְמִצְרַיִם עַד הוּסַר הַתָּמִיד אֶלֶף וַחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת וּשְׁמוֹנִים וְאַרְבַּע, הוֹסִיף עֲלֵיהֶם אֶלֶף מָאתַיִם וְתִשְׁעִים הֲרֵי אַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁמֹנֶה מֵאוֹת וְשִׁבְעִים וְאַרְבַּע כְּמִנְיַן עֶרֶב בֹּקֶר בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא, אַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת:


**And from the time the daily sacrifice was removed** in order to place a silent abomination in its stead, are days of one thousand two hundred and ninety years since the daily sacrifice was removed until it will be restored in the days of our King Messiah, and this calculation coincides with the calculation of (8:14): “evening and morning, two thousand and three hundred” from the day of their exile to Egypt until the final redemption: Egyptian exile 210; From their Exodus until the First Temple 480; First Temple 410; Babylonian exile 70; Second Temple 420; Totalling 1590. The daily sacrifice was removed six years before the destruction, which equals 1584. Add 1290, and the total sum is 2874; like the numerical value of בֹּקֶר עֶרֶב [574] plus 2300 [2874].



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:12:1)


**אַשְׁרֵי הַמְחַכֶּה וְגוֹ׳.** אַרְבָּעִים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים נוֹסָפִים עַל חֶשְׁבּוֹן הָעֶלְיוֹן, שֶׁעָתִיד מְשִׁיחֵנוּ לְהִתְכַּסּוֹת אַחַר שֶׁנִּגְלָה, וְיָשׁוּב וְיִתְגַּלֶּה. וְכֵן מָצִינוּ בְּמִדְרַשׁ רוּת, וְכֵן יִסַּד רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר הַקַּלִּיר (בְּסִלּוּק יוֹצֵר פָּרָשַׁת הַחֹדֶשׁ): וְיִתְכַּסֶּה מֵהֶם שְׁבוּעִים שִׁשָּׁה:


**Fortunate is he who waits etc.** Forty five years are added to the above number, for our King Messiah is destined to be hidden after he is revealed and to be revealed again. So we find in Midrash Ruth, and so did Rabbi Eleazar HaKalir establish (in the concluding poem of the morning service of the portion dealing with the month of Nissan): “and he will be concealed from them six weeks of years.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Daniel 12:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:13:1)


**לֵךְ לַקֵּץ.** תִּפָּטֵר לְבֵית עוֹלָמְךָ:


**go to the end** You will depart to your everlasting home.


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:13:2)


**לְגוֹרָלְךָ.** לְקַבֵּל חֶלְקְךָ עִם הַצַּדִּיקִים:


**to your lot** to receive your portion with the righteous.


###### Rashi on Daniel 12:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Daniel/r/12:13:3)


**לְקֵץ הַיָּמִין.** כְּמוֹ בְּאַחֲרִית הַיָּמִים. וְאֵין לְפָרֵשׁ הַיָּמִין לְשׁוֹן יָד הַיְמָנִית, שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא מָנוּי בַּמָּסֹרֶת הַגְּדוֹלָה בְּשִׁיטַת תֵּיבוֹת הַמְיֻחָדִין בְּנוּ״ן זְקוּפָה בְּסוֹף תֵּיבָה הַמְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת בִּמְקוֹם מֵ״ם וְלֵית דְּכָוָתֵיהוֹן, כְּגוֹן ״וְעָלֶיהָ יִכְרְעוּן אָחֳרִין״ (איוב לא:י), ״יָקוּם וְלֹא יַאֲמִין בְּחַיִּין״ (איוב כד:כב), ״קַח לְךָ חִטִּין״ (יחזקאל ד:ט), ״יֶחֱרָדוּן אִיִּין״ (יחזקאל כו:יח), ״לִמְחוֹת מְלָכִין״ (משלי לא:ג), **לְקֵץ הַיָּמִין** (כאן), (וְעַיֵּן רש״י פְּסָחִים נו. דִּבּוּר הַמַּתְחִיל לְקֵץ הַיָּמִין וְעַיֵּן רש״י סַנְהֶדְרִין צב. דִּבּוּר הַמַּתְחִיל לֵךְ לְקֵץ וְכוּ׳ וּקְצָת צָרִיךְ עִיּוּן):


**at the end of the days** Heb. לְקֵץ הַיָמִין, like בְּאַחֲרִית הַיָמִים. We cannot interpret הַיָמִין as an expression of the right hand, because it is mentioned in the Large Masorah among the six words that are unusual because they have a final “nun” at the end of the word, which serves instead of a “mem,” and there is none like them, e.g. (Job 31:10): “and may others (אֲחֵרִין) kneel upon her;” (ibid. 24:22): “and he is not sure of life (בַּחַיִין)” (Ezek. 4:9): “take yourself wheat (חִטִין)”; (ibid. 26: 18): “Now the isles (הָאִיִן) will tremble;” (Prov. 31:3): “to the pleasures of kings;” (here): “to the end of the days (הַיָמִין).”
